local VERSION = 36
local BUILD = "1e2f7685a77c"
local G = _G
local hub = rawget(G, "OldfartMapsHub")
if hub == nil then
    hub = {}
    G.OldfartMapsHub = hub
end
hub.maps = hub.maps or {}
hub.runtimes = hub.runtimes or {}
local me = { version = VERSION, build = BUILD, order = #hub.runtimes + 1 }
hub.runtimes[#hub.runtimes + 1] = me
local R = {}
me.api = R
R.VERSION = VERSION
local settledLeader = nil
function R.leader()
    if settledLeader ~= nil then return settledLeader end
    local best = nil
    for _, rt in ipairs(hub.runtimes) do
        local v, bv = tonumber(rt.version) or 0, best ~= nil and (tonumber(best.version) or 0) or -1
        if best == nil or v > bv or (v == bv and tostring(rt.build or "") > tostring(best.build or "")) then
            best = rt
        end
    end
    return best
end
function R.settle()
    settledLeader = nil
    settledLeader = R.leader()
    return settledLeader
end
function R.leading()
    return R.leader() == me
end
function R.stepping_aside()
    return rawget(G, "OldfartModMenu") ~= nil
end
local function fn(name)
    local f = G[name]
    if type(f) == "function" then return f end
    return nil
end
local function call(name, ...)
    local f = fn(name)
    if f == nil then return nil end
    return f(...)
end
local function callnum(name, ...)
    return tonumber((call(name, ...)))
end
local function const(name, fallback)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return fallback end
    local v = G[name]
    if v == nil then return fallback end
    return v
end
local function is_host()
    local f = fn("network_is_server")
    if f == nil then return true end
    return f() == true
end
local function area_now()
    return math.floor(callnum("get_network_area_timer") or 0)
end
local function frame_now()
    return math.floor(callnum("get_global_timer") or 0)
end
local function me_mario()
    local ms = G.gMarioStates
    return ms ~= nil and ms[0] or nil
end
local function me_player()
    local nps = G.gNetworkPlayers
    return nps ~= nil and nps[0] or nil
end
local function say(text)
    call("djui_chat_message_create", text)
end
local function send(data, unreliable)
    for k, v in pairs(data) do
        if v == "" then data[k] = nil end
    end
    data.ofmaps = data.ofmaps or "?"
    call("network_send", not unreliable, data)
end
local function bhv(name)
    local id = type(name) == "string" and G[name] or nil
    return type(id) == "number" and id or nil
end
local function each_of(id, visit)
    if id == nil or visit == nil then return 0 end
    local first, nextOf = G.obj_get_first_with_behavior_id, G.obj_get_next_with_same_behavior_id
    if type(first) ~= "function" or type(nextOf) ~= "function" then return 0 end
    local o, n = first(id), 0
    while o ~= nil and n < 2048 do
        n = n + 1
        if o.activeFlags ~= 0 and visit(o) == false then break end
        o = nextOf(o)
    end
    return n
end
local function placed(o)
    local flags = o ~= nil and math.floor(tonumber(o.coopFlags) or 0) or 0
    return (flags & ((G.COOP_OBJ_FLAG_LUA or 2) | (G.COOP_OBJ_FLAG_NETWORK or 1))) ~= 0
end
local function same_room(np, me)
    return np ~= nil and me ~= nil and np.connected and np.currLevelNum == me.currLevelNum
        and np.currAreaIndex == me.currAreaIndex and np.currActNum == me.currActNum
end
local function room_shared()
    local me, nps = me_player(), G.gNetworkPlayers
    if me == nil or nps == nil then return false end
    for i = 1, (G.MAX_PLAYERS or 16) - 1 do
        if same_room(nps[i], me) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function first_here()
    local me, nps = me_player(), G.gNetworkPlayers
    if me == nil or nps == nil then return true end
    local mine = tonumber(me.globalIndex) or 0
    for i = 1, (G.MAX_PLAYERS or 16) - 1 do
        local np = nps[i]
        if same_room(np, me) and (tonumber(np.globalIndex) or 99) < mine then return false end
    end
    return true
end
local function play(sound)
    if sound == nil then return end
    local m = me_mario()
    local pos = m ~= nil and m.marioObj ~= nil and m.marioObj.header ~= nil
        and m.marioObj.header.gfx ~= nil and m.marioObj.header.gfx.cameraToObject or nil
    call("play_sound", sound, pos or G.gGlobalSoundSource)
end
local function clamp(v, lo, hi)
    v = tonumber(v) or lo
    if v < lo then return lo end
    if v > hi then return hi end
    return v
end
local function squash(text)
    return (string.lower(tostring(text or "")):gsub("[^%w]", ""))
end
R.maps = {}
R.byKey = {}
R.byLevel = {}
R.clashes = {}
function R.collect()
    local list, byKey = {}, {}
    R.clashes = {}
    for _, map in ipairs(hub.maps) do
        if type(map) == "table" and type(map.key) == "string" and type(map.script) == "string" then
            local had = byKey[map.key]
            if had ~= nil and had ~= map and tonumber(had.made) ~= tonumber(map.made) then
                R.clashes[map.key] = true
            end
            if had == nil then
                byKey[map.key] = map
                list[#list + 1] = map
            elseif (tonumber(map.made) or 0) > (tonumber(had.made) or 0) then
                for i, m in ipairs(list) do if m == had then list[i] = map end end
                byKey[map.key] = map
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b)
        local na, nb = string.lower(a.name or a.key), string.lower(b.name or b.key)
        if na ~= nb then return na < nb end
        return a.key < b.key
    end)
    R.maps, R.byKey = list, byKey
    return list
end
function R.register_levels()
    R.collect()
    R.byLevel = {}
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        local n = callnum("level_register", map.script, 0, map.name or map.key,
            map.key, 20000, 0, 0, 0) or 0
        map.level = n ~= 0 and math.floor(n) or nil
        if map.level ~= nil then R.byLevel[map.level] = map end
    end
    return #R.maps
end
function R.find(word)
    word = tostring(word or "")
    local n = tonumber(word)
    if n ~= nil then return R.maps[math.floor(n)] end
    local want = squash(word)
    if want == "" then return nil end
    if R.byKey[word] ~= nil then return R.byKey[word] end
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        if squash(map.key) == want or squash(map.name) == want then return map end
    end
    local found = nil
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        if string.sub(squash(map.name), 1, #want) == want or string.sub(squash(map.key), 1, #want) == want then
            if found ~= nil then return nil, "more" end
            found = map
        end
    end
    return found
end
function R.here()
    local np = me_player()
    if np == nil then return nil end
    local map = R.byLevel[np.currLevelNum]
    if map == nil or (np.currAreaIndex or 1) ~= 1 then return nil end
    return map
end
function R.extras(map)
    return map ~= nil and not R.stepping_aside()
end
R.locked = false
local YELLOW, WHITE, GREY, GREEN, RED = "\\#ffe066\\", "\\#ffffff\\", "\\#a0a0a0\\",
    "\\#7fe07f\\", "\\#ff8080\\"
R.back = nil
R.backLanding = nil
function R.remember_back()
    local np, m = me_player(), me_mario()
    if np == nil or m == nil or m.pos == nil then return false end
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        if map.level == np.currLevelNum then return false end
    end
    R.back = { level = np.currLevelNum, area = np.currAreaIndex or 1, act = np.currActNum or 0,
        x = m.pos.x, y = m.pos.y, z = m.pos.z, yaw = m.faceAngle ~= nil and m.faceAngle.y or 0 }
    return true
end
function R.go_back()
    local b = R.back
    if b == nil or b.level == nil then
        say(RED .. "Nowhere to go back to yet. " .. WHITE .. "Type " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap home" ..
            WHITE .. " for the start.")
        return false
    end
    if call("warp_to_level", b.level, b.area, b.act) == false then
        say(RED .. "Could not go back right now - try again in a moment.")
        return false
    end
    b.untilFrame = frame_now() + 300
    R.backLanding = b
    return true
end
function R.back_land(level)
    local b = R.backLanding
    if b == nil then return false end
    local np = me_player()
    if level ~= b.level or (np ~= nil and (np.currAreaIndex or 1) ~= b.area) then return false end
    R.backLanding = nil
    local m = me_mario()
    if m == nil or m.pos == nil then return false end
    m.pos.x, m.pos.y, m.pos.z = b.x, b.y, b.z
    if m.vel ~= nil then m.vel.x, m.vel.y, m.vel.z = 0, 0, 0 end
    m.forwardVel = 0
    if m.faceAngle ~= nil then m.faceAngle.y = b.yaw or 0 end
    local o = m.marioObj
    if o ~= nil then o.oPosX, o.oPosY, o.oPosZ = b.x, b.y, b.z end
    return true
end
function R.go(map)
    if map == nil then return false end
    if map.level == nil then
        say(RED .. map.name .. " is not installed on this computer (its levels folder is missing).")
        return false
    end
    R.remember_back()
    local menu = rawget(G, "OldfartModMenu")
    if menu ~= nil and type(menu.warp) == "function" then
        return menu.warp(map.level, 1, 0) ~= false
    end
    local up = R.select
    if up ~= nil and up.away ~= nil then return up.away.level == map.level end
    local np = me_player()
    if up == nil and np ~= nil and np.currLevelNum ~= map.level and R.extras(map)
        and R.open_select(map, { level = map.level, area = 1, act = 0 }) then
        return true
    end
    return R.warp(map)
end
function R.warp(map)
    local ok = call("warp_to_level", map.level, 1, 0)
    if ok == false then
        say(RED .. "Could not travel to " .. map.name .. " right now - try again in a moment.")
        return false
    end
    return true
end
function R.list_lines()
    local lines = {}
    if #R.maps == 0 then
        lines[#lines + 1] = YELLOW .. "No maps here yet." .. WHITE ..
            " Put a map's files from Oldfart Studio into a mod folder."
        return lines
    end
    lines[#lines + 1] = YELLOW .. "Maps you can visit:"
    for i, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        local extra = ""
        local stars = #R.star_ids(map)
        if stars > 0 and not R.stepping_aside() then
            extra = GREY .. "  (" .. R.stars_got(map) .. " of " .. stars .. " stars)"
        end
        if map.level == nil then extra = extra .. RED .. "  (not installed)" end
        lines[#lines + 1] = WHITE .. "  " .. i .. "  " .. GREEN .. (map.name or map.key) .. extra
    end
    local first = R.maps[1]
    lines[#lines + 1] = WHITE .. "Type " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap 1" .. WHITE .. " or " .. YELLOW ..
        "/ofmap " .. string.lower(string.match(first.name or first.key, "%w+") or first.key) ..
        WHITE .. " to go there."
    if R.locked then
        lines[#lines + 1] = GREY .. "The host has locked travel: only the host can take you."
    end
    local clashed = {}
    for key in pairs(R.clashes or {}) do clashed[#clashed + 1] = key end
    table.sort(clashed)
    for _, key in ipairs(clashed) do
        local map = R.byKey[key]
        lines[#lines + 1] = GREY .. "Two versions of \"" .. tostring(map and map.name or key) ..
            "\" are installed - the newer one is used. Two different maps? Export one again " ..
            "under another name to keep both."
    end
    return lines
end
function R.command(text)
    text = tostring(text or "")
    local words = {}
    for w in string.gmatch(text, "%S+") do words[#words + 1] = w end
    local first = string.lower(words[1] or "")
    if first == "" or first == "list" then
        for _, line in ipairs(R.list_lines()) do say(line) end
        return true
    end
    if first == "help" then
        say(YELLOW .. "/ofmap" .. WHITE .. " shows the maps. " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap 1" .. WHITE ..
            " takes you to map 1. " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap back" .. WHITE ..
            " takes you back where you set out from, " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap home" .. WHITE .. " to the start.")
        say(WHITE .. "The host can type " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap 1 all" .. WHITE ..
            " to take everybody, and " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap lock" .. WHITE .. " so only the host can travel.")
        say(YELLOW .. "/ofmap music" .. WHITE .. " lists the songs; the host's " .. YELLOW ..
            "/ofmap music 1" .. WHITE .. " plays one for everybody, " .. YELLOW ..
            "/ofmap music off" .. WHITE .. " stops it; " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap music me 1" .. WHITE ..
            " plays one just for you.")
        return true
    end
    if first == "lock" or first == "unlock" then
        if not is_host() then
            say(RED .. "Only the host can lock or unlock travel.")
            return true
        end
        R.locked = first == "lock"
        send({ ofmaps = "lock", on = R.locked })
        say(YELLOW .. (R.locked and "Travel locked: only you (the host) can travel now."
            or "Travel unlocked: everybody can travel again."))
        return true
    end
    if first == "music" then
        return R.music_command(table.concat(words, " ", 2))
    end
    if R.locked and not is_host() then
        say(RED .. "The host has locked travel - ask the host to take you.")
        return true
    end
    if first == "home" then
        call("warp_to_start_level")
        return true
    end
    if first == "back" then
        R.go_back()
        return true
    end
    local all = string.lower(words[#words] or "") == "all" and #words > 1
    local name = table.concat(words, " ", 1, all and #words - 1 or #words)
    local map, why = R.find(name)
    if map == nil then
        if why == "more" then
            say(RED .. "More than one map starts with \"" .. name .. "\" - type more of the name, or its number.")
        else
            say(RED .. "No map called \"" .. name .. "\". " .. WHITE .. "Type " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap" ..
                WHITE .. " to see the list.")
        end
        return true
    end
    if all then
        if not is_host() then
            say(RED .. "Only the host can take everybody. Type " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap " .. name ..
                RED .. " to go yourself.")
            return true
        end
        send({ ofmaps = "all", key = map.key })
        say(YELLOW .. "Taking everybody to " .. map.name .. "!")
    end
    R.go(map)
    return true
end
local function optional(name, fallback, ...)
    local f = fn(name)
    if f == nil then return fallback end
    local ok, result = pcall(f, ...)
    if not ok then return fallback end
    return result
end
local SHARED_VENUES = (function()
    local venues = setmetatable({ optional = optional }, { __index = R })
function venues.menu_text(text, x, y)
    local glyph, advance = venues.MENU_GLYPH, venues.DIALOG_FONT
    local top = y - 3 * 2 * glyph
    local at = x
    for ch in string.gmatch(text, utf8 ~= nil and utf8.charpattern or ".") do
        if ch ~= " " then djui_hud_print_text(ch, at, top, glyph) end
        at = at + djui_hud_measure_text(ch) * advance
    end
    return at - x
end
function venues.menu_width(text)
    return djui_hud_measure_text(text) * venues.DIALOG_FONT
end
venues.EMBLEM_R = 32
function venues.emblem_rows()
    local rows = {}
    local r = venues.EMBLEM_R
    for row = 0, 2 * r - 1 do
        local dy = row + 0.5 - r
        local half = math.ceil(math.sqrt(math.max(0, r * r - dy * dy)))
        if half > 0 then rows[#rows + 1] = { row = row, half = half } end
    end
    return rows
end
function venues.draw_emblem(cx, cy)
    local upper = venues.optional("get_texture_info", nil, "texture_menu_course_upper")
    local lower = venues.optional("get_texture_info", nil, "texture_menu_course_lower")
    if upper == nil or lower == nil then return false end
    if type(djui_hud_render_texture_tile) ~= "function" then return false end
    venues.emblemRows = venues.emblemRows or venues.emblem_rows()
    local r = venues.EMBLEM_R
    for _, line in ipairs(venues.emblemRows) do
        local tex, ty = upper, line.row
        if line.row >= r then tex, ty = lower, line.row - r end
        local w = 2 * line.half
        djui_hud_render_texture_tile(tex, cx - line.half, cy - r + line.row, 1 / w, 1,
            r - line.half, ty, w, 1)
    end
    return true
end
function venues.select_paint(screen)
    djui_hud_set_resolution(RESOLUTION_N64)
    local ox = (djui_hud_get_screen_width() - 320) / 2
    local best = tonumber(screen.best) or 0
    if best > 0 then
        djui_hud_set_font(FONT_HUD)
        djui_hud_set_color(255, 255, 255, 255)
        djui_hud_print_text("$", ox + 155, 106, 1)
        djui_hud_print_text("@", ox + 171, 106, 1)
        djui_hud_print_text(tostring(best), ox + 187, 106, 1)
        djui_hud_set_font(FONT_NORMAL)
        djui_hud_set_color(0, 0, 0, 255)
        djui_hud_print_text("MY SCORE", ox + 102, 240 - 118 - 16, venues.DIALOG_FONT)
    end
    djui_hud_set_font(FONT_NORMAL)
    djui_hud_set_color(0, 0, 0, 255)
    local world = string.upper(tostring(screen.world or ""))
    local w = djui_hud_measure_text(world) * venues.DIALOG_FONT
    djui_hud_print_text(world, ox + 160 - w / 2, 240 - 33 - 16, venues.DIALOG_FONT)
    djui_hud_set_color(255, 255, 255, 255)
    venues.draw_emblem(ox + 158, 159)
    djui_hud_set_font(FONT_HUD)
    local number = tostring(screen.number or 0)
    djui_hud_print_text(number, ox + (#number < 2 and 152 or 143), 158, 1)
    if screen.phase ~= "in" then
        djui_hud_set_font(FONT_NORMAL)
        djui_hud_set_color(0, 0, 0, 255)
        for i = 1, screen.n do
            venues.menu_text(tostring(i), ox + 139 - screen.n * 17 + i * 34, 38)
        end
        local name = string.upper(tostring(screen.act or ""))
        venues.menu_text(name, ox + 163 - venues.menu_width(name) / 2, 81)
    end
    djui_hud_set_resolution(RESOLUTION_DJUI)
    return true
end
function venues.course_number(layouts, key)
    local layout = key ~= nil and layouts[key] or nil
    if layout == nil then return 0 end
    if type(layout.course) == "number" and layout.course >= venues.COURSE_FIRST_BONUS then
        return math.floor(layout.course)
    end
    local top = venues.COURSE_FIRST_BONUS - 1
    local loose = {}
    for k, other in pairs(layouts) do
        if type(other.course) == "number" then
            top = math.max(top, math.floor(other.course))
        elseif type(other.stars) == "table" and #other.stars > 0 then
            loose[#loose + 1] = k
        end
    end
    table.sort(loose)
    for i, k in ipairs(loose) do
        if k == key then return top + i end
    end
    return 0
end
function venues.water_surface(spans, y)
    if #spans == 0 then return nil end
    table.sort(spans, function(a, b) return a[1] < b[1] end)
    local bodies = { { spans[1][1], spans[1][2] } }
    for i = 2, #spans do
        local last = bodies[#bodies]
        if spans[i][1] <= last[2] + 1 then
            if spans[i][2] > last[2] then last[2] = spans[i][2] end
        else
            bodies[#bodies + 1] = { spans[i][1], spans[i][2] }
        end
    end
    local ref = y
    if type(ref) ~= "number" then return bodies[#bodies][2] end
    local best = nil
    for _, body in ipairs(bodies) do
        if body[1] <= ref + 50 then best = body end
    end
    return best ~= nil and best[2] or nil
end
function venues.has_bit(value, flag)
    if value == nil or flag == nil or flag == 0 then return false end
    return math.floor(value / flag) % 2 == 1
end
function venues.dance_start(m)
    local action = ACT_STAR_DANCE_NO_EXIT
    local now = tonumber(m.action) or 0
    local water = ACT_FLAG_SWIMMING
    local metal = ACT_FLAG_METAL_WATER
    local air = ACT_FLAG_AIR
    if (water ~= nil and venues.has_bit(now, water))
        or (metal ~= nil and venues.has_bit(now, metal)) then
        action = ACT_STAR_DANCE_WATER
    end
    if air ~= nil and venues.has_bit(now, air) then
        action = ACT_FALL_AFTER_STAR_GRAB
    end
    if action == nil then return false end
    local lv = gLevelValues
    local heal = lv ~= nil and lv.starHeal
    if heal ~= nil and heal ~= false and heal ~= 0 then
        m.healCounter = 31
        m.hurtCounter = 0
    end
    venues.dancing = true
    set_mario_action(m, action, 1)
    return true
end
function venues.dance_step(m)
    if not venues.dancing or m == nil then return false end
    local action = m.action
    local fall = ACT_FALL_AFTER_STAR_GRAB
    if action == fall then return false end
    if action ~= ACT_STAR_DANCE_NO_EXIT
        and action ~= ACT_STAR_DANCE_WATER then
        venues.dancing = false
        return false
    end
    if (tonumber(m.actionTimer) or 0) < venues.DANCE_CUT or (m.actionState or 0) ~= 0 then
        return false
    end
    venues.dancing = false
    venues.optional("enable_background_sound", nil)
    local back = ACT_FREEFALL
    if action == ACT_STAR_DANCE_WATER then
        back = ACT_WATER_IDLE or back
    elseif m.pos ~= nil and m.floorHeight ~= nil and m.pos.y <= m.floorHeight then
        back = ACT_IDLE
    end
    set_mario_action(m, back, 0)
    return true
end
function venues.arrival_of(layout, race, globalIndex)
    local w = layout.world
    local spawns = type(layout.spawns) == "table" and layout.spawns or {}
    if race and #spawns > 0 then w = nil end
    if type(w) == "table" and type(w[1]) == "number" and type(w[3]) == "number" then
        local x0, y0, z0, yaw = w[1], tonumber(w[2]) or 0, w[3], tonumber(w[4]) or 0
        local i = math.floor(tonumber(globalIndex) or 0)
        if i <= 0 then return { x = x0, y = y0, z = z0, yaw = yaw } end
        local a = (i - 1) * (2 * math.pi / 15)
        local x = x0 + math.sin(a) * venues.WORLD_RING
        local z = z0 + math.cos(a) * venues.WORLD_RING
        local floor = venues.optional("find_floor_height", nil, x, y0 + 100, z)
        if type(floor) == "number" and math.abs(floor - y0) <= 60 then
            return { x = x, y = floor, z = z, yaw = yaw }
        end
        return { x = x0, y = y0, z = z0, yaw = yaw }
    end
    if #spawns == 0 then return nil end
    local row = spawns[(math.floor(tonumber(globalIndex) or 0) % #spawns) + 1]
    return { x = row[1], y = row[2], z = row[3], yaw = row[4] }
end
venues.SKY_TINTS = {
    night = { 60, 74, 125 },
    dusk = { 235, 140, 125 },
    purple = { 150, 100, 175 },
}
function venues.sky_tint_of(look, hour)
    if type(look) ~= "table" then return nil end
    local t = look.skyTint
    if type(t) == "table" and type(t[1]) == "number" then
        return { t[1], t[2] or t[1], t[3] or t[1] }
    end
    local words = { tostring(hour or ""), tostring(look.darkness or "") }
    local mood = look.mood
    if type(mood) == "table" then
        for _, v in pairs(mood) do words[#words + 1] = tostring(v) end
    elseif mood ~= nil then
        words[#words + 1] = tostring(mood)
    end
    local text = string.lower(table.concat(words, " "))
    local pick = nil
    if string.find(text, "purple", 1, true) then
        pick = venues.SKY_TINTS.purple
    elseif string.find(text, "dusk", 1, true) or string.find(text, "sunset", 1, true)
        or string.find(text, "evening", 1, true) or string.find(text, "twilight", 1, true) then
        pick = venues.SKY_TINTS.dusk
    elseif string.find(text, "night", 1, true) or string.find(text, "pitch", 1, true)
        or string.find(text, "cold blue", 1, true) then
        pick = venues.SKY_TINTS.night
    end
    if pick == nil then return nil end
    return { pick[1], pick[2], pick[3] }
end
function venues.sky_mix(amount, r, g, b, dim, tint, bright)
    amount = math.max(0, math.min(1, tonumber(amount) or 0))
    dim = tonumber(dim) or 255
    bright = tonumber(bright) or 1
    local air = { r or 255, g or 255, b or 255 }
    local out = {}
    for c = 1, 3 do
        local v = (255 - (255 - air[c]) * amount) * dim / 255
        if tint ~= nil then v = v * (tint[c] or 255) / 255 end
        out[c] = math.floor(math.max(0, math.min(255, v * bright)))
    end
    return out[1], out[2], out[3]
end
function venues.counter_paint(count)
    djui_hud_set_resolution(RESOLUTION_N64)
    djui_hud_set_font(FONT_HUD)
    local text = tostring(count)
    local cross = count < 100 and 14 or 0
    local width = 16 + cross + 12 * #text
    local x = djui_hud_get_screen_width() - venues.HUD_STARS_X - venues.HUD_GAP - width
    local y = venues.HUD_ROW
    djui_hud_set_color(255, 255, 255, 255)
    djui_hud_print_text("*", x, y, 1)
    if cross > 0 then djui_hud_print_text("@", x + 16, y, 1) end
    djui_hud_print_text(text, x + 16 + cross, y, 1)
    djui_hud_set_resolution(RESOLUTION_DJUI)
    return true
end
function venues.fog_slot_on(cmd, op, w0, w1, mul, off)
    local set = gfx_set_command
    if set == nil then return end
    if op == 0xD9 then
        local clear = (~w0) & 0x00FFFFFF
        set(cmd, "gsSPGeometryMode(%i, %i)", clear & ~venues.G_FOG, (w1 | venues.G_FOG) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
    elseif op == 0xDB then
        set(cmd, "gsSPFogFactor(%i, %i)", mul, off)
    elseif op == 0xF8 then
        set(cmd, "gsDPSetFogColor(%i, %i, %i, %i)", 255, 255, 255, 255)
    elseif op == 0xE2 then
        local len = (w0 & 0xFF) + 1
        local sft = 32 - len - ((w0 >> 8) & 0xFF)
        local data = w1 & 0xFFFFFFFF
        set(cmd, "gsSPSetOtherMode(%i, %i, %i, %i)", 0xE2, sft, len,
            (data & ~0xCCC00000) | 0xC8000000)
    end
end
function venues.fog_slot_back(cmd, op, w0, w1)
    local set = gfx_set_command
    if set == nil then return end
    if op == 0xD9 then
        set(cmd, "gsSPGeometryMode(%i, %i)", (~w0) & 0x00FFFFFF, w1 & 0xFFFFFFFF)
    elseif op == 0xDB then
        local fm, fo = (w1 >> 16) & 0xFFFF, w1 & 0xFFFF
        if fm >= 0x8000 then fm = fm - 0x10000 end
        if fo >= 0x8000 then fo = fo - 0x10000 end
        set(cmd, "gsSPFogFactor(%i, %i)", fm, fo)
    elseif op == 0xF8 then
        set(cmd, "gsDPSetFogColor(%i, %i, %i, %i)", (w1 >> 24) & 0xFF, (w1 >> 16) & 0xFF,
            (w1 >> 8) & 0xFF, w1 & 0xFF)
    elseif op == 0xE2 then
        local len = (w0 & 0xFF) + 1
        local sft = 32 - len - ((w0 >> 8) & 0xFF)
        set(cmd, "gsSPSetOtherMode(%i, %i, %i, %i)", 0xE2, sft, len, w1 & 0xFFFFFFFF)
    end
end
venues.headsHeld = {}
function venues.fog_slots_write(entries, on, mul, off)
    local get, opOf = gfx_get_command, gfx_get_op
    if not on then
        local n = #venues.headsHeld
        for i = n, 1, -1 do
            local h = venues.headsHeld[i]
            pcall(venues.fog_slot_back, h[1], h[2], h[3], h[4])
        end
        venues.headsHeld = {}
        return n
    end
    if get == nil or opOf == nil then return 0 end
    local written = 0
    for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
        for k = 0, 3 do
            local cmd = get(entry[1], entry[2] + k)
            if cmd ~= nil then
                local held = nil
                for _, h in ipairs(venues.headsHeld) do
                    if h[1] == cmd then held = h end
                end
                if held == nil then
                    held = { cmd, opOf(cmd), (cmd.w0 or 0) & 0xFFFFFFFF, (cmd.w1 or 0) & 0xFFFFFFFF }
                    venues.headsHeld[#venues.headsHeld + 1] = held
                end
                pcall(venues.fog_slot_on, cmd, held[2], held[3], held[4], mul or 3200, off or -2944)
                written = written + 1
            end
        end
    end
    return written
end
    return venues
end)()
local SHARED_MUSIC = (function()
    local music = { optional = optional }
music.PAUSE_SCALE = 0.5
music.JINGLE_SCALE = 20 / 127
music.JINGLE_STILL = 3
music.STREAM_BOOST = 2
music.LOWERED_CAP = 40
music.NO_LOAD = 255
function music.engine_cap()
    local cap = 127
    if music.optional("is_current_background_music_volume_lowered", 0) == 1
        or music.optional("is_current_background_music_volume_lowered", 0) == true then
        cap = math.min(cap, music.LOWERED_CAP)
    end
    for _, getter in ipairs({ "get_current_background_music_max_target_volume",
            "get_current_background_music_target_volume" }) do
        local raw = tonumber(music.optional(getter, 0)) or 0
        if raw >= 0x80 then cap = math.min(cap, raw - 0x80) end
    end
    return cap
end
function music.stream_gain(rowVolume, dial, paused, jingle)
    local volume = math.max(0, math.min(127, tonumber(rowVolume) or 75))
    local gain = volume / 127 * (tonumber(dial) or 100) / 100 * music.STREAM_BOOST
    if paused then gain = gain * music.PAUSE_SCALE end
    local held = 1
    if volume > 0 then held = math.min(1, music.engine_cap() / volume) end
    if jingle then held = math.min(held, music.JINGLE_SCALE) end
    return gain * held
end
function music.env_step(st)
    local acc = music.optional("sequence_player_get_tempo_acc", nil, SEQ_PLAYER_ENV or 1)
    if acc == nil then st.on = false return false end
    if st.acc == nil then st.acc = acc return st.on end
    if acc ~= st.acc then
        st.acc, st.still, st.on = acc, 0, true
        return true
    end
    st.still = st.still + 1
    if st.still >= music.JINGLE_STILL then st.on = false end
    return st.on
end
function music.gain_toward(gain, target, snap, fade)
    if snap then return target end
    if gain < target then return math.min(target, gain + 1 / fade) end
    if gain > target then return math.max(target, gain - 1 / fade) end
    return gain
end
function music.refuses_load(seqPlayer, selectUp, streamInForce)
    if seqPlayer ~= (SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0) then return false end
    if selectUp then return false end
    return streamInForce == true
end
music.STILL_FRAMES = 3
function music.level_stalled(st)
    if type(sequence_player_get_tempo_acc) ~= "function" then return false end
    if music.optional("is_game_paused", false) then
        st.acc, st.still = nil, 0
        return false
    end
    if (tonumber((music.optional("sequence_player_get_fade_volume", 1.0,
        SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0))) or 1.0) <= 0 then
        st.acc, st.still = nil, 0
        return false
    end
    local acc = sequence_player_get_tempo_acc(SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0)
    if acc ~= st.acc then
        st.acc, st.still = acc, 0
        return false
    end
    st.still = st.still + 1
    return st.still >= music.STILL_FRAMES
end
    return music
end)()
local SHARED_WEATHER = (function()
    local celebrate = {}
celebrate.WEATHER = {
    rain  = { drops = 70,  fall = 15,  lean = -1.6, long = 11, storm = false },
    storm = { drops = 130, fall = 19,  lean = -2.6, long = 14, storm = true  },
}
celebrate.RAIN_MAX = 140
celebrate.RAIN_COLOUR = { r = 178, g = 202, b = 232 }
celebrate.RAIN_ALPHA = 120
function celebrate.drop_paint(kind)
    local c = celebrate.RAIN_COLOUR
    return kind.r or c.r, kind.g or c.g, kind.b or c.b, kind.a or celebrate.RAIN_ALPHA, kind.wide or 1
end
celebrate.STRIKES = {
    { wait = 210, delay = 62, peak = 150 },
    { wait = 130, delay = 38, peak = 190 },
    { wait = 260, delay = 88, peak = 110 },
    { wait = 95,  delay = 14, peak = 235 },
    { wait = 175, delay = 50, peak = 165 },
    { wait = 320, delay = 74, peak = 125 },
    { wait = 110, delay = 22, peak = 215 },
}
celebrate.FLASH = { 1.0, 0.45, 0.95, 0.60, 0.35, 0.18, 0.07 }
function celebrate.storm_new(kind)
    local st = { kind = kind, drops = {}, at = 0, wait = 0, strikes = 0, flash = 0, peak = 0,
                 bang = nil, far = false }
    if kind ~= nil and celebrate.WEATHER[kind].storm then
        st.wait = celebrate.STRIKES[1].wait
    end
    return st
end
function celebrate.storm_flash(st)
    local flash = celebrate.FLASH
    if st == nil or st.flash <= 0 or st.flash > #flash then return 0 end
    return math.floor(st.peak * flash[st.flash])
end
function celebrate.storm_tick(st, showing, spectacle)
    local landed = nil
    if st.bang ~= nil then
        st.bang = st.bang - 1
        if st.bang <= 0 then
            st.bang = nil
            landed = st.far and SOUND_GENERAL_VOLCANO_EXPLOSION or SOUND_GENERAL_BOWSER_BOMB_EXPLOSION
        end
    end
    if st.flash > 0 then st.flash = st.flash + 1 end
    if st.flash > #celebrate.FLASH then st.flash = 0 end
    if st.kind == nil or not celebrate.WEATHER[st.kind].storm then return landed end
    if not showing then return landed end
    st.wait = st.wait - 1
    if st.wait > 0 then return landed end
    local strikes = celebrate.STRIKES
    st.at = (st.at % #strikes) + 1
    local strike = strikes[st.at]
    st.wait = strikes[(st.at % #strikes) + 1].wait
    st.strikes = st.strikes + 1
    if spectacle then
        st.flash = 1
        st.peak = strike.peak
    end
    st.bang = strike.delay
    st.far = strike.delay >= 40
    return landed
end
function celebrate.rain_frame(st, width, height, across, down, rect, hidden)
    local kind = celebrate.WEATHER[st.kind]
    local dr, dg, db, da, dwide = celebrate.drop_paint(kind)
    local lean = kind.lean
    local drops = st.drops
    local wanted = math.min(kind.drops, celebrate.RAIN_MAX)
    while #drops < wanted do
        table.insert(drops, {
            x = width * 0.5 + across(math.floor(width * 0.62)),
            y = down(math.floor(height)),
        })
    end
    while #drops > wanted do table.remove(drops) end
    for index = 1, #drops do
        local one = drops[index]
        one.y = one.y + kind.fall
        one.x = one.x + lean
        if one.y > height or one.x < -kind.long or one.x > width + kind.long then
            one.y = -kind.long - math.abs(down(math.floor(height * 0.5)))
            one.x = width * 0.5 + across(math.floor(width * 0.62))
        elseif not hidden then
            rect(one.x, one.y, dwide, kind.long, dr, dg, db, da)
        end
    end
    local alpha = celebrate.storm_flash(st)
    if alpha > 0 and not hidden then
        rect(0, 0, width, height, 255, 255, 255, alpha)
    end
end
    return celebrate
end)()
local SHARED_FOG = (function()
    local modifiers = {}
function modifiers.fog_painter()
    local P = {}
    local G_MOVEWORD_OP, G_SETOTHERMODE_L_OP = 0xDB, 0xE2
    local G_GEOMETRYMODE_OP, G_SETFOGCOLOR_OP = 0xD9, 0xF8
    local G_MW_FOG_INDEX = 0x08
    local G_FOG_BIT = 0x00010000
    P.CYCLE1_MASK = 0xCCC00000
    P.FOG_SHADE_A = 0xC8000000
    P.mul, P.off = 3200, -2944
    P.positions = {}
    P.BUDGET = 4096
    P.EVERY = 15
    local DL_NODES = {
        [0x015] = true, [0x016] = true, [0x017] = true, [0x019] = true,
        [0x01A] = true, [0x01B] = true, [0x01C] = true, [0x01D] = true,
        [0x030] = true,
    }
    P.held = {}
    P.painted = {}
    P.ownOnly = false
    local SYNCS = { [0xE7] = "gsDPPipeSync()", [0xE6] = "gsDPLoadSync()", [0xE8] = "gsDPTileSync()" }
    local VTX_OPS = { [0x01] = true, [0x11] = true }
    local DRAW_OPS = { [0x05] = true, [0x06] = true, [0x07] = true, [0x12] = true }
    local STATE_WORDS = {
        "gsSPGeometryMode(%i, %i)", "gsSPFogFactor(%i, %i)", "gsDPSetFogColor(%i, %i, %i, %i)" }
    local function spend_sync(cmd, op, which)
        if which == 1 then
            gfx_set_command(cmd, STATE_WORDS[1], 0, G_FOG_BIT)
        elseif which == 2 then
            gfx_set_command(cmd, STATE_WORDS[2], P.mul, P.off)
            P.positions[#P.positions + 1] = cmd
        else
            gfx_set_command(cmd, STATE_WORDS[3], 255, 255, 255, 255)
        end
        P.held[#P.held + 1] = { cmd, "sync", SYNCS[op] }
    end
    local function fogable(cmd)
        local w0, w1 = cmd.w0 or 0, cmd.w1 or 0
        local len = (w0 & 0xFF) + 1
        local sft = 32 - len - ((w0 >> 8) & 0xFF)
        if sft > 18 or sft + len < 32 then return nil end
        local data = w1 & 0xFFFFFFFF
        if (data & P.CYCLE1_MASK) == P.FOG_SHADE_A then return nil end
        return sft, len, data
    end
    local function fog_render_mode(cmd)
        local sft, len, data = fogable(cmd)
        if sft == nil then return end
        local now = (data & ~P.CYCLE1_MASK) | P.FOG_SHADE_A
        gfx_set_command(cmd, "gsSPSetOtherMode(%i, %i, %i, %i)",
            G_SETOTHERMODE_L_OP, sft, len, now)
        P.held[#P.held + 1] = { cmd, "mode", data, sft, len }
    end
    function P.plan_list(cmds)
        local firstMode, firstVtx = nil, nil
        local modes = {}
        local have = {}
        for i, one in ipairs(cmds) do
            local op, cmd = one[2], one[1]
            if op == G_SETOTHERMODE_L_OP and fogable(cmd) ~= nil then
                modes[#modes + 1] = cmd
                firstMode = firstMode or i
            elseif VTX_OPS[op] then
                firstVtx = firstVtx or i
            elseif op == G_GEOMETRYMODE_OP and ((cmd.w1 or 0) & G_FOG_BIT) ~= 0 then
                have[1] = have[1] or i
            elseif op == G_MOVEWORD_OP and (((cmd.w0 or 0) >> 16) & 0xFF) == G_MW_FOG_INDEX then
                have[2] = have[2] or i
            elseif op == G_SETFOGCOLOR_OP then
                have[3] = have[3] or i
            end
        end
        if firstMode == nil then return nil, modes, false end
        local callable = firstVtx == nil or firstVtx > firstMode
        local firstDraw = #cmds + 1
        for i = firstMode + 1, #cmds do
            if DRAW_OPS[cmds[i][2]] then firstDraw = i break end
        end
        local limit = { firstVtx or firstDraw, firstVtx or firstDraw, firstDraw }
        if limit[1] > firstDraw then limit[1], limit[2] = firstDraw, firstDraw end
        local spend, at = {}, 1
        for which = 1, 3 do
            if have[which] == nil or have[which] >= limit[which] then
                while at < limit[which] and SYNCS[cmds[at][2]] == nil do at = at + 1 end
                if at >= limit[which] then return nil, modes, callable end
                spend[#spend + 1] = { at, which }
                at = at + 1
            end
        end
        return spend, modes, callable
    end
    P.NAME = "of_fogmode_"
    P.made, P.live = {}, {}
    local function mode_list(data)
        local dl = P.made[data]
        if dl ~= nil then
            if not P.live[data] then
                local factor = gfx_get_command(dl, 1)
                gfx_set_command(factor, STATE_WORDS[2], P.mul, P.off)
                P.positions[#P.positions + 1] = factor
                P.live[data] = true
            end
            return dl
        end
        if gfx_create == nil or gfx_get_command == nil then return nil end
        local name = P.NAME .. string.format("%08x", data)
        if gfx_get_from_name ~= nil then dl = gfx_get_from_name(name) end
        if dl == nil then dl = gfx_create(name, 5) end
        if dl == nil then return nil end
        gfx_set_command(gfx_get_command(dl, 0), STATE_WORDS[1], 0, G_FOG_BIT)
        local factor = gfx_get_command(dl, 1)
        gfx_set_command(factor, STATE_WORDS[2], P.mul, P.off)
        P.positions[#P.positions + 1] = factor
        P.live[data] = true
        gfx_set_command(gfx_get_command(dl, 2), STATE_WORDS[3], 255, 255, 255, 255)
        gfx_set_command(gfx_get_command(dl, 3), "gsSPSetOtherMode(%i, %i, %i, %i)",
            G_SETOTHERMODE_L_OP, 3, 29, (data & ~P.CYCLE1_MASK) | P.FOG_SHADE_A)
        gfx_set_command(gfx_get_command(dl, 4), "gsSPEndDisplayList()")
        P.made[data] = dl
        return dl
    end
    local function call_render_mode(cmd)
        local sft, len, data = fogable(cmd)
        if sft == nil or sft ~= 3 or len ~= 29 then return false end
        local dl = mode_list(data)
        if dl == nil then return false end
        gfx_set_command(cmd, "gsSPDisplayList(%g)", dl)
        P.held[#P.held + 1] = { cmd, "mode", data, sft, len }
        return true
    end
    local function own_word(cmd)
        local w0, w1 = cmd.w0 or 0, cmd.w1 or 0
        if ((w0 >> 16) & 0xFF) ~= G_MW_FOG_INDEX then return end
        local mul = (w1 >> 16) & 0xFFFF
        local off = w1 & 0xFFFF
        if mul >= 0x8000 then mul = mul - 0x10000 end
        if off >= 0x8000 then off = off - 0x10000 end
        if mul == P.mul and off == P.off then return end
        gfx_set_command(cmd, "gsSPFogFactor(%i, %i)", P.mul, P.off)
        P.positions[#P.positions + 1] = cmd
        P.held[#P.held + 1] = { cmd, "word", mul, off }
    end
    function P.paint_list(gfx)
        if gfx == nil then return end
        if P.ownOnly then
            gfx_parse(gfx, function(cmd, op)
                if op == G_MOVEWORD_OP then own_word(cmd) end
            end)
            return
        end
        local cmds = {}
        gfx_parse(gfx, function(cmd, op) cmds[#cmds + 1] = { cmd, op } end)
        local spend, modes, callable = P.plan_list(cmds)
        if spend ~= nil then
            for _, one in ipairs(spend) do
                local at = cmds[one[1]]
                spend_sync(at[1], at[2], one[2])
            end
            for _, cmd in ipairs(modes) do fog_render_mode(cmd) end
        elseif callable then
            for _, cmd in ipairs(modes) do
                if not call_render_mode(cmd) then break end
            end
        end
    end
    P.LISTS_PER_FRAME = 48
    P.queue, P.queued = {}, 0
    function P.drain(most)
        local done = 0
        while P.queued > 0 and done < most do
            local gfx = P.queue[P.queued]
            P.queue[P.queued] = nil
            P.queued = P.queued - 1
            P.paint_list(gfx)
            done = done + 1
        end
        return done
    end
    function P.walk(first, budget)
        if first == nil or budget <= 0 then return budget end
        local node = first
        repeat
            budget = budget - 1
            if budget <= 0 then return 0 end
            local kind = node.type
            if kind ~= nil and DL_NODES[kind] and cast_graph_node ~= nil then
                local shaped = cast_graph_node(node)
                if shaped ~= nil and shaped.displayList ~= nil then
                    P.queued = P.queued + 1
                    P.queue[P.queued] = shaped.displayList
                end
            end
            budget = P.walk(node.children, budget)
            node = node.next
        until node == nil or node == first or budget <= 0
        return budget
    end
    function P.paint_models(budget)
        if obj_get_first == nil or obj_get_next == nil then return budget end
        local seen, seenCount = {}, 0
        for list = 0, 12 do
            local object = obj_get_first(list)
            while object ~= nil and budget > 0 do
                local gfxNode = object.header ~= nil and object.header.gfx or nil
                local shared = gfxNode ~= nil and gfxNode.sharedChild or nil
                if shared ~= nil then
                    local already = false
                    for index = 1, seenCount do
                        if seen[index] == shared then already = true break end
                    end
                    if not already then
                        seenCount = seenCount + 1
                        seen[seenCount] = shared
                        budget = P.walk(shared, budget)
                    end
                end
                object = obj_get_next(object)
            end
        end
        return budget
    end
    function P.can()
        return gfx_parse ~= nil and gfx_set_command ~= nil and cast_graph_node ~= nil
    end
    function P.area(budget)
        local me = gMarioStates ~= nil and gMarioStates[0] or nil
        local area = me ~= nil and me.area or nil
        local root = area ~= nil and area.root or nil
        local top = root ~= nil and root.node or nil
        if top ~= nil and not P.painted[top] then
            P.painted[top] = true
            budget = P.walk(top.children, budget)
        end
        return budget
    end
    function P.restore()
        for index = #P.held, 1, -1 do
            local one = P.held[index]
            pcall(function()
                if one[2] == "sync" then
                    gfx_set_command(one[1], one[3] or "gsDPPipeSync()")
                elseif one[2] == "word" then
                    gfx_set_command(one[1], "gsSPFogFactor(%i, %i)", one[3], one[4])
                else
                    gfx_set_command(one[1], "gsSPSetOtherMode(%i, %i, %i, %i)",
                        G_SETOTHERMODE_L_OP, one[4], one[5], one[3])
                end
            end)
        end
        P.held, P.painted, P.positions, P.live = {}, {}, {}, {}
        P.queue, P.queued = {}, 0
    end
    function P.words(mul, off)
        mul, off = math.floor(mul), math.floor(off)
        if mul == P.mul and off == P.off then return false end
        P.mul, P.off = mul, off
        for _, cmd in ipairs(P.positions) do
            pcall(gfx_set_command, cmd, "gsSPFogFactor(%i, %i)", mul, off)
        end
        return true
    end
    return P
end
function modifiers.fog_in_the_dark(r, g, b, dark)
    r, g, b = r or 255, g or 255, b or 255
    if type(dark) ~= "table" then return r, g, b end
    local function one(c, d)
        d = math.max(0, math.min(255, tonumber(d) or 255))
        return math.floor(c * d / 255 + 0.5)
    end
    return one(r, dark[1]), one(g, dark[2] or dark[1]), one(b, dark[3] or dark[1])
end
    return modifiers
end)()
R.sharedVenues, R.sharedMusic, R.sharedWeather, R.sharedFog =
    SHARED_VENUES, SHARED_MUSIC, SHARED_WEATHER, SHARED_FOG
R.worn = nil
R.held = {}
R.fogWords = { 3200, -2944 }
local function wear_fog(fog, on, dark)
    if fog == nil then return end
    if on and fog[5] ~= nil and fog[6] ~= nil then R.fogWords = { fog[5], fog[6] } end
    local c = { SHARED_FOG.fog_in_the_dark(fog[2] or 255, fog[3] or 255, fog[4] or 255, dark) }
    for i = 0, 2 do call("set_fog_color", i, on and c[i + 1] or 255) end
    call("set_fog_intensity", on and (fog[1] or 1.0) or 1.0)
    local amount = on and clamp(((fog[1] or 1) - 1.0) / 0.4, 0, 1) or 0
    R.skyFog = on and { amount, fog[2] or 255, fog[3] or 255, fog[4] or 255 } or nil
    R.paint_sky()
end
R.skyFog = nil
function R.paint_sky()
    local map = R.moodOn and R.worn or nil
    local tint = nil
    if map ~= nil and not R.mood_held() then tint = SHARED_VENUES.sky_tint_of(map.look, map.hour) end
    local f = R.skyFog
    local o1, o2, o3 = SHARED_VENUES.sky_mix(f ~= nil and f[1] or 0, f ~= nil and f[2] or 255,
        f ~= nil and f[3] or 255, f ~= nil and f[4] or 255, 255, tint, 1)
    local out = { o1, o2, o3 }
    local was = R.skyPainted
    if was ~= nil and was[1] == o1 and was[2] == o2 and was[3] == o3 then return out end
    R.skyPainted = out
    for i = 0, 2 do call("set_skybox_color", i, out[i + 1]) end
    return out
end
local FOGP = SHARED_FOG.fog_painter()
R.fogPainter = FOGP
R.FOG_LISTS_PER_FRAME = FOGP.LISTS_PER_FRAME
R.FOG_PAINT_EVERY = FOGP.EVERY
R.FOG_BUDGET = FOGP.BUDGET
R.fogTick = 0
function R.fog_drain(most)
    return FOGP.drain(most)
end
function R.fog_queued()
    return FOGP.queued
end
function R.fog_models(budget)
    return FOGP.paint_models(budget)
end
R.G_FOG, R.G_LIGHTING, R.G_LE_EXT = 0x00010000, 0x00020000, 0x00004000
R.G_CULL_FRONT, R.G_CULL_BACK, R.G_SHADE, R.G_SMOOTH = 0x200, 0x400, 0x4, 0x00200000
R.RM_OPA, R.RM_XLU = 0x00552078, 0x005049D8
R.RM_OPA_FOG, R.RM_XLU_FOG = 0xC8152078, 0xC81049D8
function R.fog_lists(map)
    local out = {}
    if map == nil or tonumber(map.lightfog) == nil or tonumber(map.lightfog) < 2 then return out end
    for _, name in ipairs(type(map.fogHeads) == "table" and map.fogHeads or {}) do
        out[#out + 1] = { name, 3, not tostring(name):match("_water_dl$") }
    end
    for _, name in ipairs(type(map.fogBillboards) == "table" and map.fogBillboards or {}) do
        out[#out + 1] = { name, 1, false, true }
    end
    return out
end
function R.fog_slots(map, on)
    local entries = {}
    for _, one in ipairs(R.fog_lists(map)) do
        local gfx = call("gfx_get_from_name", one[1])
        if gfx ~= nil then entries[#entries + 1] = { gfx, one[2] } end
    end
    return SHARED_VENUES.fog_slots_write(entries, on, R.fogWords[1], R.fogWords[2])
end
function R.fog_restore()
    SHARED_VENUES.fog_slots_write({}, false)
    FOGP.restore()
    R.fogTick = 0
end
function R.fog_paint(on)
    if not on then
        R.fog_restore()
        return 0
    end
    if not FOGP.can() then return #FOGP.held end
    FOGP.words(R.fogWords[1], R.fogWords[2])
    local here = R.worn or R.here()
    local heads = here ~= nil and tonumber(here.lightfog) ~= nil and tonumber(here.lightfog) >= 2
    R.fogTick = R.fogTick + 1
    local modelsOnly = R.fogTick % FOGP.EVERY ~= 1
    local budget = FOGP.BUDGET
    if heads and not modelsOnly then R.fog_slots(here, true) end
    if not modelsOnly and not heads then budget = FOGP.area(budget) end
    FOGP.drain(FOGP.LISTS_PER_FRAME)
    FOGP.paint_models(budget)
    return #FOGP.held
end
local function wear_vision(list, on)
    for _, pair in ipairs(list or {}) do
        local flag = const(pair[1])
        if flag ~= nil then
            if on then
                call("set_shader_flag_value", flag, pair[2])
                call("set_shader_flag_enabled", flag, true)
            else
                call("set_shader_flag_value", flag, pair[3] or 0)
                call("set_shader_flag_enabled", flag, false)
            end
        end
    end
end
function R.track(map)
    local look = map ~= nil and map.look or nil
    if look == nil then return nil end
    if type(look.musicSeq) == "number" then return look.musicSeq end
    if type(look.music) == "string" then return const(look.music) end
    return nil
end
R.lightOwned = false
R.LE_ACTORS_MODE = 1
R.LE_PER_VERTEX = 8
R.LE_PER_VERTEX_DEFAULT = 4
function R.light_take(r, g, b)
    if not R.lightOwned then
        if call("le_is_enabled") == true then return false end
        R.lightOwned = true
        R.leModeBefore = callnum("le_get_mode") or 0
        call("le_set_mode", const("LE_MODE_AFFECT_ALL_SHADED", R.LE_ACTORS_MODE))
        call("le_set_max_lights_per_vertex", R.LE_PER_VERTEX)
    end
    call("le_set_ambient_color", r, g, b)
    return true
end
function R.light_give()
    if not R.lightOwned then return false end
    R.lightOwned = false
    call("le_set_ambient_color", 127, 127, 127)
    call("le_set_mode", R.leModeBefore or 0)
    call("le_set_max_lights_per_vertex", R.LE_PER_VERTEX_DEFAULT)
    R.leModeBefore = nil
    R.rig_shade(nil)
    call("le_set_enabled", false)
    return true
end
R.RIG = {
    night = { amb = { 96, 100, 120 }, dir = { 235, 240, 255 } },
    dusk = { amb = { 170, 160, 165 }, dir = { 255, 205, 160 } },
    inside = { amb = { 110, 105, 100 }, dir = { 200, 190, 180 } },
}
R.RIG_FLOOR = 90
function R.rig_of(map)
    local hour = map ~= nil and map.hour or nil
    if hour == nil and map ~= nil and map.night then hour = "night" end
    return R.RIG[hour] or R.RIG.inside
end
function R.rig_shade(rig)
    if rig ~= nil and R.select ~= nil then rig = nil end
    if rig == nil then
        if R.rigShaded then
            R.rigShaded = false
            local look = R.worn ~= nil and R.worn.look or nil
            for i = 0, 2 do
                local shade = look ~= nil and type(look.shade) == "table" and look.shade or nil
                call("set_lighting_color", i, shade ~= nil and shade.dir[i + 1] or 255)
                call("set_lighting_color_ambient", i, shade ~= nil and shade.amb[i + 1] or 255)
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    R.rigShaded = true
    for i = 0, 2 do
        call("set_lighting_color", i, rig.dir[i + 1])
        call("set_lighting_color_ambient", i, 255)
    end
    return true
end
function R.rig_ambient(map, v)
    local dark = tonumber(R.sharedDark) or 208
    local t = 1
    if dark < 255 then t = clamp((255 - v) / (255 - dark), 0, 1) end
    local rig = R.rig_of(map)
    local out = {}
    for c = 1, 3 do
        local a = math.max(R.RIG_FLOOR, rig.amb[c])
        out[c] = math.floor(255 - (255 - a) * t + 0.5)
    end
    return out, rig, t
end
R.moodOn = false
R.darkWorn = false
function R.wear_mood(look)
    R.moodOn = true
    if type(look.dir) == "table" then
        local was = {}
        for i = 0, 2 do was[i + 1] = callnum("get_lighting_dir", i) or 0 end
        R.held.dir = was
        for i = 0, 2 do call("set_lighting_dir", i, look.dir[i + 1] or 0) end
    end
    if type(look.dark) == "table" then
        R.darkWorn = R.light_take(look.dark[1], look.dark[2], look.dark[3])
    end
    if type(look.shade) == "table" then
        for i = 0, 2 do
            call("set_lighting_color", i, look.shade.dir[i + 1])
            call("set_lighting_color_ambient", i, look.shade.amb[i + 1])
        end
    end
    if type(look.tint) == "table" then
        for i = 0, 2 do call("set_vertex_color", i, look.tint[i + 1]) end
    end
    if type(look.fog) == "table" then
        wear_fog(look.fog, true, look.fogDark)
        R.fog_paint(true)
    end
    if type(look.envfx) == "string" then call("set_override_envfx", const(look.envfx, -1)) end
    wear_vision(look.shader, true)
    R.rain = R.rain_start(look.rain)
    R.paint_sky()
end
function R.unwear_mood(look)
    if not R.moodOn then return false end
    R.moodOn = false
    if R.held.dir ~= nil then
        for i = 0, 2 do call("set_lighting_dir", i, R.held.dir[i + 1]) end
        R.held.dir = nil
    end
    if R.darkWorn then
        R.darkWorn = false
        if R.lightsAmbient == nil then R.light_give() end
    end
    if type(look.shade) == "table" then
        for i = 0, 2 do
            call("set_lighting_color", i, 255)
            call("set_lighting_color_ambient", i, 255)
        end
    end
    if type(look.tint) == "table" then
        for i = 0, 2 do call("set_vertex_color", i, 255) end
    end
    if type(look.fog) == "table" then
        R.fog_paint(false)
        wear_fog(look.fog, false)
    end
    if look.envfx ~= nil then call("set_override_envfx", -1) end
    wear_vision(look.shader, false)
    if R.sunHeld then
        R.sunHeld = false
        for i = 0, 2 do call("set_lighting_dir", i, 0) end
    end
    R.rain = nil
    R.paint_sky()
    return true
end
function R.mood_held()
    return R.select ~= nil
end
function R.settle_mood(map)
    if R.worn ~= map then return false end
    local look = map.look or {}
    local want = not R.mood_held()
    local moved = false
    if want and not R.moodOn then
        R.wear_mood(look)
        moved = true
    elseif not want and R.moodOn then
        R.unwear_mood(look)
        moved = true
    end
    if R.moodOn and type(look.dark) == "table" and not R.darkWorn then
        R.darkWorn = R.light_take(look.dark[1], look.dark[2], look.dark[3])
    end
    return moved
end
function R.wear(map)
    local look = map.look or {}
    R.worn = map
    R.held = { look = look }
    local sky = const(look.sky)
    if type(sky) == "number" then call("set_override_skybox", sky) end
    if not R.mood_held() then R.wear_mood(look) end
    local own = R.level_track ~= nil and R.level_track() or nil
    local head = callnum("get_current_background_music")
    if own ~= nil and head ~= nil and head >= 0 and head % 256 == own % 256 then
        call("play_music", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, own, 0)
    end
    if R.music_refresh ~= nil then R.music_refresh() end
end
function R.unwear()
    local map = R.worn
    if map == nil then return false end
    local look = R.held.look or {}
    if look.sky ~= nil then call("set_override_skybox", -1) end
    R.unwear_mood(look)
    R.worn = nil
    if R.music_refresh ~= nil then R.music_refresh() end
    R.rain = nil
    R.held = {}
    return true
end
R.SUN_REACH = 2.0
R.SUN_BASE = { 0, 110 / 127, 63 / 127 }
function R.sun_dir(sun, from, at)
    if type(sun) ~= "table" or from == nil or at == nil then return nil end
    local fx, fy, fz = at.x - from.x, at.y - from.y, at.z - from.z
    local fl = math.sqrt(fx * fx + fy * fy + fz * fz)
    if fl < 1 then return nil end
    fx, fy, fz = fx / fl, fy / fl, fz / fl
    local rx, rz = -fz, fx
    local rl = math.sqrt(rx * rx + rz * rz)
    if rl < 0.001 then return nil end
    rx, rz = rx / rl, rz / rl
    local ux, uy, uz = -rz * fy, rz * fx - rx * fz, rx * fy
    local sl = math.sqrt(sun[1] * sun[1] + sun[2] * sun[2] + sun[3] * sun[3])
    if sl == 0 then return nil end
    local sx, sy, sz = sun[1] / sl, sun[2] / sl, sun[3] / sl
    local k, b = R.SUN_REACH, R.SUN_BASE
    return { k * (sx * rx + sz * rz) - b[1], k * (sx * ux + sy * uy + sz * uz) - b[2],
             k * -(sx * fx + sy * fy + sz * fz) - b[3] }
end
function R.aim_sun(map)
    local look = map.look or {}
    if type(look.sun) ~= "table" or type(look.dir) == "table" or not R.moodOn then return false end
    local eye = G.gLakituState
    local dir = eye ~= nil and R.sun_dir(look.sun, eye.curPos or eye.pos, eye.curFocus or eye.focus) or nil
    if dir == nil then return false end
    R.sunHeld = true
    for i = 0, 2 do call("set_lighting_dir", i, dir[i + 1]) end
    return true
end
R.WEATHER, R.STRIKES, R.FLASH = SHARED_WEATHER.WEATHER, SHARED_WEATHER.STRIKES, SHARED_WEATHER.FLASH
R.RAIN_MAX, R.RAIN_ALPHA = SHARED_WEATHER.RAIN_MAX, SHARED_WEATHER.RAIN_ALPHA
R.RAIN_COLOUR = { SHARED_WEATHER.RAIN_COLOUR.r, SHARED_WEATHER.RAIN_COLOUR.g, SHARED_WEATHER.RAIN_COLOUR.b }
R.SPREAD = { period = 1009, across = 623, down = 385 }
function R.rain_start(kind)
    if kind ~= nil and R.WEATHER[kind] == nil then kind = nil end
    local rain = SHARED_WEATHER.storm_new(kind)
    rain.acrossSeed, rain.downSeed = 0, 0
    return rain
end
function R.rain_spread(rain, which, range)
    local sp = R.SPREAD
    local seed = (rain[which] + (which == "acrossSeed" and sp.across or sp.down)) % sp.period
    rain[which] = seed
    return math.floor(seed * (range * 2 + 1) / sp.period) - range
end
function R.flash_alpha(rain)
    return SHARED_WEATHER.storm_flash(rain)
end
function R.rain_step()
    local rain = R.rain
    if rain == nil or rain.kind == nil then return end
    local bang = SHARED_WEATHER.storm_tick(rain, true, true)
    if bang ~= nil then
        call("set_environmental_camera_shake", G.SHAKE_ENV_EXPLOSION)
        play(bang)
    end
end
function R.draw_rain()
    local rain = R.rain
    if rain == nil or rain.kind == nil then return end
    local m = me_mario()
    if m == nil or m.marioObj == nil then return end
    call("djui_hud_set_resolution", G.RESOLUTION_DJUI)
    local w = callnum("djui_hud_get_screen_width") or 0
    local h = callnum("djui_hud_get_screen_height") or 0
    if w <= 0 or h <= 0 then return end
    SHARED_WEATHER.rain_frame(rain, w, h,
        function(range) return R.rain_spread(rain, "acrossSeed", range) end,
        function(range) return R.rain_spread(rain, "downSeed", range) end,
        R.hud_rect, false)
    call("djui_hud_set_color", 255, 255, 255, 255)
end
function R.water_at(map, x, z, y)
    if map == nil or type(map.water) ~= "table" or type(x) ~= "number" or type(z) ~= "number" then
        return nil
    end
    local spans = {}
    for _, b in ipairs(map.water) do
        if b[1] <= x and x <= b[3] and b[2] <= z and z <= b[4] then
            spans[#spans + 1] = { b[6] or (b[5] - 400), b[5] }
        end
    end
    return SHARED_VENUES.water_surface(spans, y)
end
local SHARED_MOVERS = (function()
    local spawner = {}
spawner.MOVER_MAX_POINTS = 12
spawner.MOVER_MODES = { "loop", "pingpong", "oneway" }
spawner.MOVER_TRIGGERS = { "always", "ridden", "wired" }
local function waits(trigger) return trigger == "ridden" or trigger == "wired" end
spawner.mover_waits = waits
spawner.MOVER_MIN_SPEED = 30
spawner.MOVER_MAX_SPEED = 4200
spawner.MOVER_SPEED = 300
spawner.MOVER_RISE = 50
spawner.MOVER_CARRY = 150
spawner.MOVER_MAX_WAIT = 600
spawner.MOVER_WAIT = 10
spawner.MOVER_CAP = 32
spawner.MOVER_ROLL = 0x40
spawner.MOVER_LOG = 100
spawner.MOVER_LOG_WIDE = 101
local MOVER_FPS = 30
local function clamp(v, lo, hi)
    v = tonumber(v) or lo
    if v < lo then return lo end
    if v > hi then return hi end
    return v
end
local function index_of(list, word, default)
    for i, w in ipairs(list) do if w == word then return i end end
    return default
end
function spawner.mover_normalise(spec)
    spec = type(spec) == "table" and spec or {}
    local out = {
        mode = spawner.MOVER_MODES[index_of(spawner.MOVER_MODES, spec.mode, 2)],
        trigger = spawner.MOVER_TRIGGERS[index_of(spawner.MOVER_TRIGGERS, spec.trigger, 1)],
        phase = math.floor(clamp(spec.phase or 0, 0, 0x3FFFFFFF) + 0.5),
        link = math.floor(clamp(spec.link or 0, 0, 255) + 0.5),
        points = {},
    }
    local limit = 32000
    for i, p in ipairs(type(spec.points) == "table" and spec.points or {}) do
        if i > spawner.MOVER_MAX_POINTS then break end
        table.insert(out.points, {
            x = math.floor(clamp(p.x, -limit, limit) + 0.5),
            y = math.floor(clamp(p.y, -limit, limit) + 0.5),
            z = math.floor(clamp(p.z, -limit, limit) + 0.5),
            speed = math.floor(clamp(p.speed or spawner.MOVER_SPEED,
                spawner.MOVER_MIN_SPEED, spawner.MOVER_MAX_SPEED) + 0.5),
            wait = math.floor(clamp(p.wait or spawner.MOVER_WAIT, 0,
                spawner.MOVER_MAX_WAIT) + 0.5),
        })
    end
    if #out.points == 0 then
        table.insert(out.points, { x = 0, y = 0, z = 0,
            speed = spawner.MOVER_SPEED, wait = spawner.MOVER_WAIT })
    end
    local first = out.points[1]
    first.x, first.y, first.z = 0, 0, 0
    if #out.points == 1 then
        table.insert(out.points, { x = 0, y = 0, z = 600,
            speed = first.speed, wait = first.wait })
    end
    return out
end
function spawner.mover_default()
    return spawner.mover_normalise({ mode = "pingpong", trigger = "always",
        points = { { x = 0, y = 0, z = 0 }, { x = 0, y = 0, z = 1200 } } })
end
local function leg_frames(a, b, speed)
    local dx, dy, dz = b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y, b.z - a.z
    local len = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
    local perFrame = math.max(1, speed / MOVER_FPS)
    local frames = math.ceil(len / perFrame)
    frames = math.max(frames, math.ceil(math.abs(dy) / spawner.MOVER_RISE))
    return math.max(1, frames), len
end
local function yaw_of(dx, dz)
    if dx == 0 and dz == 0 then return nil end
    return math.floor(math.atan(dx, dz) * 0x8000 / math.pi + 0.5) % 0x10000
end
local function short_turn(from, to)
    local d = (to - from) % 0x10000
    if d >= 0x8000 then d = d - 0x10000 end
    return d
end
spawner.mover_turn, spawner.mover_yaw = short_turn, yaw_of
function spawner.mover_timeline(spec)
    spec = spawner.mover_normalise(spec)
    local pts = spec.points
    local n = #pts
    local legs = {}
    local function wait(i, frames)
        table.insert(legs, { wait = true, a = i, frames = frames })
    end
    local function move(i, j, speed)
        local frames, len = leg_frames(pts[i], pts[j], speed)
        table.insert(legs, { a = i, b = j, frames = frames, len = len })
    end
    local w = function(i) return pts[i].wait * 3 end
    if spec.mode == "loop" then
        for i = 1, n do
            wait(i, w(i))
            move(i, i % n + 1, pts[i].speed)
        end
    elseif spec.mode == "oneway" then
        for i = 1, n - 1 do
            wait(i, w(i))
            move(i, i + 1, pts[i].speed)
        end
        wait(n, w(n))
        for i = n, 2, -1 do move(i, i - 1, pts[i - 1].speed) end
    else
        for i = 1, n - 1 do
            wait(i, w(i))
            move(i, i + 1, pts[i].speed)
        end
        wait(n, w(n))
        for i = n, 2, -1 do
            move(i, i - 1, pts[i - 1].speed)
            if i - 1 > 1 then wait(i - 1, w(i - 1)) end
        end
    end
    local at, roll, face = 0, 0, nil
    for _, leg in ipairs(legs) do
        leg.start = at
        at = at + leg.frames
        if not leg.wait then
            local a, b = pts[leg.a], pts[leg.b]
            local yaw = yaw_of(b.x - a.x, b.z - a.z)
            leg.sign = 1
            if yaw ~= nil then
                if face ~= nil and math.abs(short_turn(face, yaw)) > 0x4000 then
                    yaw = (yaw + 0x8000) % 0x10000
                    leg.sign = -1
                end
                face = yaw
            end
            leg.face = face or 0
            leg.roll = roll
            local flat = math.sqrt((b.x - a.x) ^ 2 + (b.z - a.z) ^ 2)
            roll = roll + leg.sign * flat
        end
    end
    local function face_after(k)
        for j = k + 1, #legs + k do
            local leg = legs[(j - 1) % #legs + 1]
            if not leg.wait then return leg.face end
        end
        return 0
    end
    local function face_before(k)
        for j = k - 1, k - #legs, -1 do
            local leg = legs[(j - 1) % #legs + 1]
            if not leg.wait then return leg.face end
        end
        return 0
    end
    local rollAt = 0
    for k, leg in ipairs(legs) do
        if leg.wait then
            leg.faceFrom = face_before(k)
            leg.faceTo = face_after(k)
            leg.roll = rollAt
        else
            rollAt = leg.roll + leg.sign * math.sqrt((pts[leg.b].x - pts[leg.a].x) ^ 2
                + (pts[leg.b].z - pts[leg.a].z) ^ 2)
        end
    end
    return { spec = spec, legs = legs, period = math.max(1, at),
             firstWait = legs[1] ~= nil and legs[1].wait and legs[1].frames or 0 }
end
function spawner.mover_at(timeline, t)
    local legs = timeline.legs
    local pts = timeline.spec.points
    t = t % timeline.period
    for _, leg in ipairs(legs) do
        if t < leg.start + leg.frames or leg == legs[#legs] then
            local f = leg.frames > 0 and (t - leg.start) / leg.frames or 1
            if f < 0 then f = 0 elseif f > 1 then f = 1 end
            if leg.wait then
                local p = pts[leg.a]
                local face = (leg.faceFrom + short_turn(leg.faceFrom, leg.faceTo) * f) % 0x10000
                return p.x, p.y, p.z, math.floor(face + 0.5) % 0x10000,
                    leg.roll * spawner.MOVER_ROLL
            end
            local a, b = pts[leg.a], pts[leg.b]
            local flat = math.sqrt((b.x - a.x) ^ 2 + (b.z - a.z) ^ 2)
            return a.x + (b.x - a.x) * f, a.y + (b.y - a.y) * f, a.z + (b.z - a.z) * f,
                leg.face, (leg.roll + leg.sign * flat * f) * spawner.MOVER_ROLL
        end
    end
    local p = pts[1]
    return p.x, p.y, p.z, 0, 0
end
    return spawner
end)()
local FPS, CARRY = 30, SHARED_MOVERS.MOVER_CARRY
local short_turn = SHARED_MOVERS.mover_turn
function R.timeline(spec)
    local pts = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(spec.points or {}) do
        pts[#pts + 1] = { x = tonumber(p[1]) or 0, y = tonumber(p[2]) or 0, z = tonumber(p[3]) or 0,
                          speed = p[4], wait = p[5] }
    end
    return SHARED_MOVERS.mover_timeline({ mode = spec.mode, trigger = spec.trigger, points = pts })
end
R.mover_at = SHARED_MOVERS.mover_at
function R.mover_phase(mv, now)
    local tl = mv.tl
    if mv.trigger == "ridden" or mv.trigger == "wired" then
        if mv.start == nil or mv.start < 0 then return 0, false end
        local t = tl.firstWait + (now - mv.start)
        if now < mv.start or t >= tl.period then return 0, false end
        return t, true
    end
    return (now + (mv.phase or 0)) % tl.period, true
end
function R.mover_go(mv, t0)
    t0 = math.floor(tonumber(t0) or area_now())
    local cur = mv.start or -1
    local cycle = mv.tl.period - mv.tl.firstWait
    if cur >= 0 and t0 >= cur and t0 < cur + cycle then return false end
    if cur >= 0 and t0 < cur and cur - t0 >= cycle then return false end
    mv.start = t0
    return true
end
R.built = nil
R.things = {}
R.movers, R.stars, R.pieces = {}, {}, {}
R.locals = {}
local function thing(o)
    return o ~= nil and R.things[o] or nil
end
local SOLID, THING = nil, nil
local ONOFF_MESH = { [0] = "purple_switch_seg8_collision_0800C7A8",
    [1] = "breakable_box_seg8_collision_08012D70",
    [2] = "breakable_box_seg8_collision_08012D70" }
R.ONOFF_BLOCK_SCALE = 4.0
R.ONOFF_MESH_SHARE = 0.25
local STAGE_MESH = { [0] = "checkerboard_platform_seg8_collision_0800D710",
    [1] = "metal_box_seg8_collision_08024C28",
    [2] = "breakable_box_seg8_collision_08012D70",
    [3] = "exclamation_box_outline_seg8_collision_08025F78" }
R.STAGE_SPIN_STEP, R.STAGE_LIFT_RANGE, R.STAGE_LIFT_PERIOD = 0x140, 500, 300
local function solid_init(o)
    local row = thing(o)
    if row == nil then return end
    o.collisionData = row.col
    o.oCollisionDistance = 20000
    o.oDrawingDistance = 20000
    o.oFlags = (tonumber(o.oFlags) or 0) | (G.OBJ_FLAG_UPDATE_GFX_POS_AND_ANGLE or 0x8)
    if row.kind == "onoff" and row.what ~= 0 then
        call("obj_scale", o, R.ONOFF_BLOCK_SCALE)
    end
end
local function solid_loop(o)
    local row = thing(o)
    if row == nil then return end
    if o.header ~= nil and o.header.gfx ~= nil then o.header.gfx.skipInViewCheck = true end
    if o.collisionData == nil then o.collisionData = row.col end
    if row.kind == "mover" then
        local mv = row
        local now = area_now()
        local t, running = R.mover_phase(mv, now)
        if mv.trigger == "ridden" then t, running = R.mover_ride(mv, o, now, t, running) end
        local dx, dy, dz, face, roll = R.mover_at(mv.tl, t)
        local ox, oy, oz = o.oPosX, o.oPosY, o.oPosZ
        local nx, ny, nz = mv.x + dx, mv.y + dy, mv.z + dz
        o.oVelX, o.oVelY, o.oVelZ = 0, 0, 0
        o.oAngleVelPitch, o.oAngleVelYaw = 0, 0
        local vx, vy, vz = nx - ox, ny - oy, nz - oz
        if vx * vx + vy * vy + vz * vz <= CARRY * CARRY then
            o.oVelX, o.oVelY, o.oVelZ = vx, vy, vz
        end
        o.oPosX, o.oPosY, o.oPosZ = nx, ny, nz
        if mv.log then
            local pitch = math.floor(roll + 0.5) % 0x10000
            local spin = short_turn(tonumber(o.oFaceAnglePitch) or 0, pitch)
            local turn = short_turn(tonumber(o.oFaceAngleYaw) or 0, face)
            if math.abs(spin) < 0x2000 then o.oAngleVelPitch = spin end
            if math.abs(turn) < 0x2000 then o.oAngleVelYaw = turn end
            o.oFaceAnglePitch = pitch >= 0x8000 and pitch - 0x10000 or pitch
            o.oFaceAngleYaw = face >= 0x8000 and face - 0x10000 or face
            o.oMoveAngleYaw = o.oFaceAngleYaw
        end
    elseif row.kind == "button" then
        local m = me_mario()
        local air = G.ACT_FLAG_AIR or 0x800
        local on = m ~= nil and m.floor ~= nil and m.floor.object == o and ((m.action or 0) & air) == 0
        if on and not row.on then R.report(row.map, "pressed", row.link) end
        row.on = on
        call("obj_scale_xyz", o, 1, on and 0.3 or 1, 1)
    elseif row.kind == "onoff" then
        if not R.onoff_step(row, o) then return end
        local scale = row.what ~= 0 and o.header ~= nil and o.header.gfx ~= nil
            and o.header.gfx.scale or nil
        if scale ~= nil then
            local sx, sy, sz = scale.x, scale.y, scale.z
            local k = R.ONOFF_MESH_SHARE
            scale.x, scale.y, scale.z = sx * k, sy * k, sz * k
            call("load_object_collision_model")
            scale.x, scale.y, scale.z = sx, sy, sz
            return
        end
    elseif row.kind == "stage" then
        R.stage_step(row, o)
    elseif row.kind == "tri" then
        R.tri_step(row, o)
    end
    call("load_object_collision_model")
end
local function thing_init(o)
    local row = thing(o)
    if row == nil then return end
    o.oDrawingDistance = 20000
    o.oFlags = (tonumber(o.oFlags) or 0) | (G.OBJ_FLAG_UPDATE_GFX_POS_AND_ANGLE or 0x8)
end
local function thing_loop(o)
    local row = thing(o)
    if row == nil then return end
    if row.kind == "star" then
        R.star_step(row, o)
    elseif row.kind == "target" then
        R.target_step(row, o)
    elseif row.kind == "zone" then
        R.zone_step(row, o)
    elseif row.kind == "sound" then
        R.sound_step(row, o)
    end
end
if type(G.hook_behavior) == "function" then
    SOLID = G.hook_behavior(nil, G.OBJ_LIST_SURFACE, true, solid_init, solid_loop)
    THING = G.hook_behavior(nil, G.OBJ_LIST_LEVEL, true, thing_init, thing_loop)
end
R.SOLID, R.THING = SOLID, THING
local function spawn(behaviour, model, x, y, z, row, setup)
    if behaviour == nil then return nil end
    local o = call("spawn_non_sync_object", behaviour, model, x, y, z, function(obj)
        R.things[obj] = row
        if setup ~= nil then setup(obj) end
    end)
    if o ~= nil then
        R.things[o] = row
        row.obj = o
    end
    return o
end
R.spawn_local = spawn
local function collision(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return nil end
    local data = G.gGlobalObjectCollisionData
    if data ~= nil and data[name] ~= nil then return data[name] end
    return call("smlua_collision_util_get", name)
end
R.collision = collision
local function model(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return nil end
    local id = const(name)
    if type(id) == "number" then return id end
    id = call("smlua_model_util_get_id", name)
    if type(id) ~= "number" or id == G.E_MODEL_ERROR_MODEL then return nil end
    return id
end
R.model = model
local function to_angle(degrees)
    local t = math.floor((tonumber(degrees) or 0) * 65536 / 360 + 0.5) % 65536
    if t >= 32768 then t = t - 65536 end
    return t
end
R.to_angle = to_angle
local function facing(obj, yaw)
    obj.oFaceAngleYaw, obj.oMoveAngleYaw = yaw, yaw
end
local THING_KINDS = {}
R.THING_KINDS = THING_KINDS
THING_KINDS.spawn = true
THING_KINDS.logic = true
THING_KINDS.track = true
THING_KINDS.onoff = function(map, row, at)
    local what = math.floor(tonumber(row.what) or 0)
    if ONOFF_MESH[what] == nil then what = 0 end
    local r = { kind = "onoff", map = map, what = what, col = collision(ONOFF_MESH[what]) }
    local mdl = what == 0 and G.E_MODEL_PURPLE_SWITCH or G.E_MODEL_EXCLAMATION_BOX
    return spawn(SOLID, mdl, at[1], at[2], at[3], r, function(obj)
        facing(obj, to_angle(at[4]))
        obj.collisionData = r.col
    end), r
end
THING_KINDS.stage = function(map, row, at)
    local shape = math.floor(tonumber(row.shape) or 0)
    if STAGE_MESH[shape] == nil then shape = 0 end
    local r = { kind = "stage", map = map, motion = math.floor(tonumber(row.motion) or 0),
        col = collision(STAGE_MESH[shape]), home = { at[1], at[2], at[3] } }
    return spawn(SOLID, model(row.model) or G.E_MODEL_CHECKERBOARD_PLATFORM, at[1], at[2], at[3], r,
        function(obj)
            facing(obj, to_angle(at[4]))
            obj.collisionData = r.col
        end), r
end
THING_KINDS.tri = function(map, row, at)
    local r = { kind = "tri", map = map, col = collision(R.TRI_COL), age = 0 }
    return spawn(SOLID, model(R.TRI_MODEL) or G.E_MODEL_CHECKERBOARD_PLATFORM, at[1], at[2], at[3], r,
        function(obj)
            facing(obj, to_angle(at[4]))
            obj.collisionData = r.col
        end), r
end
THING_KINDS.sound = function(map, row, at)
    local r = { kind = "sound", map = map, sound = row.sound, on = {} }
    return spawn(THING, G.E_MODEL_PURPLE_SWITCH, at[1], at[2], at[3], r, function(obj)
        facing(obj, to_angle(at[4]))
    end), r
end
THING_KINDS.ball = function(map, row, at, index)
    return R.ball_build(map, row, at, index)
end
function R.build(map)
    R.teardown()
    R.built = map
    R.tendFresh = 6
    R.movers, R.stars, R.pieces, R.locals = {}, {}, {}, {}
    if not R.extras(map) then return 0 end
    local n = 0
    for i, mvRow in ipairs(map.movers or {}) do
        local mv = { kind = "mover", map = map, index = i, x = mvRow.x or 0, y = mvRow.y or 0,
            z = mvRow.z or 0, trigger = mvRow.trigger or "always", phase = mvRow.phase or 0,
            link = mvRow.link, log = mvRow.log == true, col = collision(mvRow.col) }
        mv.tl = R.timeline({ points = mvRow.points, mode = mvRow.mode })
        mv.start = (mv.trigger == "always") and 0 or -1
        local yaw = to_angle(mvRow.yaw)
        local o = spawn(SOLID, model(mvRow.model) or G.E_MODEL_NONE, mv.x, mv.y, mv.z, mv, function(obj)
            obj.oFaceAngleYaw, obj.oMoveAngleYaw = yaw, yaw
            obj.oFaceAnglePitch, obj.oFaceAngleRoll = 0, 0
            obj.collisionData = mv.col
        end)
        R.movers[i] = mv
        if o ~= nil then n = n + 1 end
    end
    n = n + R.place_stars(map)
    for i, p in ipairs(map.pieces or {}) do
        local kind = p[1]
        local row = { kind = kind, map = map, index = i, link = math.floor(tonumber(p[6]) or 1) }
        local yaw = to_angle(p[5])
        local behaviour, mdl = THING, G.E_MODEL_NONE
        if kind == "button" then
            behaviour, mdl = SOLID, G.E_MODEL_PURPLE_SWITCH
            row.col = collision("purple_switch_seg8_collision_0800C7A8")
        elseif kind == "target" then
            mdl = G.E_MODEL_WOODEN_SIGNPOST
        end
        if spawn(behaviour, mdl, p[2], p[3], p[4], row, function(obj)
            obj.oFaceAngleYaw, obj.oMoveAngleYaw = yaw, yaw
            if row.col ~= nil then obj.collisionData = row.col end
        end) then
            n = n + 1
        end
        R.pieces[i] = row
    end
    for i, t in ipairs(map.things or {}) do
        local make = THING_KINDS[t.kind]
        if type(make) == "function" then
            local o, row = make(map, t, t.at or { 0, 0, 0, 0 }, i)
            if row ~= nil then R.locals[#R.locals + 1] = row end
            if o ~= nil then n = n + 1 end
        end
    end
    n = n + R.track_flags(map)
    return n
end
function R.teardown()
    for o in pairs(R.things) do
        if o ~= nil and o.activeFlags ~= nil and o.activeFlags ~= 0 then
            call("obj_mark_for_deletion", o)
        end
    end
    R.things = {}
    R.movers, R.stars, R.pieces, R.locals = {}, {}, {}, {}
    local was = R.built
    R.built = nil
    return was ~= nil
end
function R.mover_ride(mv, o, now, t, running)
    local m = me_mario()
    local ridden = m ~= nil and m.marioObj ~= nil and m.marioObj.platform == o
    local tl = mv.tl
    local cycle = tl.period - tl.firstWait
    local start = mv.start or -1
    if start >= 0 and now >= start + cycle and now <= start + tl.period and mv.decided ~= start then
        mv.decided = start
        if ridden and R.mover_go(mv, start + tl.period) then
            send({ ofmaps = "go", key = mv.map.key, i = mv.index, t0 = mv.start })
            return R.mover_phase(mv, now)
        end
    elseif not running and start <= now and ridden then
        if R.mover_go(mv, now) then
            send({ ofmaps = "go", key = mv.map.key, i = mv.index, t0 = now })
            return R.mover_phase(mv, now)
        end
    end
    return t, running
end
function R.onoff_flips(map)
    local st = R.state[map.key]
    return st ~= nil and st.flips or 0
end
function R.onoff_on(map)
    return R.onoff_flips(map) % 2 == 0
end
function R.onoff_step(row, o)
    local map = row.map
    if row.what == 0 then
        local m = me_mario()
        local air = G.ACT_FLAG_AIR or 0x800
        local standing = m ~= nil and m.floor ~= nil and m.floor.object == o
            and ((m.action or 0) & air) == 0
        if standing and R.onoffStood ~= o then R.report(map, "flip", 0) end
        if standing then
            R.onoffStood = o
        elseif R.onoffStood == o then
            R.onoffStood = nil
        end
        return true
    end
    local on = R.onoff_on(map)
    local solid = (row.what == 1) == on
    local want = solid and G.E_MODEL_EXCLAMATION_BOX or G.E_MODEL_EXCLAMATION_BOX_OUTLINE
    if want ~= nil and row.model ~= want then
        row.model = want
        call("obj_set_model_extended", o, want)
    end
    o.oAnimState = row.what == 1 and 0 or 2
    return solid
end
function R.onoff_heard(map)
    local flips = R.onoff_flips(map)
    if R.onoffSeen == nil or R.onoffSeen.key ~= map.key then
        R.onoffSeen = { key = map.key, flips = flips }
        return false
    end
    if R.onoffSeen.flips == flips then return false end
    R.onoffSeen.flips = flips
    play(G.SOUND_GENERAL2_PURPLE_SWITCH)
    return true
end
local onoffOut = { x = 0, y = 0, z = 0 }
function R.draw_onoff()
    local on = R.built ~= nil and R.onoff_on(R.built)
    local drawn = 0
    for _, row in ipairs(R.locals) do
        if row.kind == "onoff" and row.what == 0 and row.obj ~= nil and row.obj.activeFlags ~= 0 then
            local o = row.obj
            local r, g, b = 255, 90, 90
            if not on then r, g, b = 110, 150, 255 end
