            if R.world_screen({ x = o.oPosX, y = o.oPosY + 140, z = o.oPosZ }, onoffOut) then
                R.hud_label(onoffOut.x, onoffOut.y, on and "ON" or "OFF", 0.6, r, g, b, 230)
                drawn = drawn + 1
            end
        end
    end
    return drawn
end
local resultSeen = { seq = nil, at = 0 }
function R.draw_zones(map)
    local drawn = 0
    for o, row in pairs(R.things) do
        if row.kind == "zone" and row.map == map and o.activeFlags ~= 0 then
            R.world_ring({ x = o.oPosX, y = o.oPosY, z = o.oPosZ }, 300, 24, 2, 120, 220, 255, 120)
            drawn = drawn + 1
        end
    end
    return drawn
end
function R.readout(map, now)
    local st = R.state_of(map.key)
    if st.running then
        return { title = "Course", lines = { R.time_text(math.max(1, area_now() - st.start)),
            "Hits: " .. R.total_hits(st) } }
    end
    if st.result == nil then return nil end
    now = now or frame_now()
    if resultSeen.seq ~= st.seq then resultSeen.seq, resultSeen.at = st.seq, now end
    if now - resultSeen.at > R.RESULT_FRAMES then return nil end
    local lines = { R.time_text(st.result.frames), "Hits: " .. st.result.hits }
    local who = {}
    for gi in pairs(st.hits) do who[#who + 1] = gi end
    table.sort(who)
    for _, gi in ipairs(who) do
        local li = fn("network_local_index_from_global") ~= nil and G.network_local_index_from_global(gi) or nil
        local np = li ~= nil and G.gNetworkPlayers ~= nil and G.gNetworkPlayers[li] or nil
        local name = np ~= nil and np.name or ("#" .. gi)
        lines[#lines + 1] = name .. "  " .. st.hits[gi]
    end
    return { title = "Course done", lines = lines }
end
function R.draw_course(map)
    call("djui_hud_set_resolution", G.RESOLUTION_DJUI)
    R.draw_zones(map)
    local card = R.readout(map)
    if card == nil then return false end
    local w = G.djui_hud_get_screen_width()
    R.hud_label(w * 0.5, 70, card.title, 0.8, 255, 220, 120, 240)
    for i, line in ipairs(card.lines) do
        R.hud_label(w * 0.5, 100 + (i - 1) * 26, line, i == 1 and 0.9 or 0.6, 255, 255, 255, 230)
    end
    return true
end
function R.stage_step(row, o)
    local now = area_now()
    o.oAngleVelYaw = 0
    if row.motion == 1 then
        local yaw = (now * R.STAGE_SPIN_STEP) % 0x10000
        o.oMoveAngleYaw = yaw >= 0x8000 and yaw - 0x10000 or yaw
        o.oFaceAngleYaw = o.oMoveAngleYaw
        o.oAngleVelYaw = R.STAGE_SPIN_STEP
    elseif row.motion == 2 then
        local phase = (now % R.STAGE_LIFT_PERIOD) / R.STAGE_LIFT_PERIOD
        local y = row.home[2] + math.sin(phase * 2 * math.pi) * R.STAGE_LIFT_RANGE
        o.oVelY = y - (tonumber(o.oPosY) or y)
        o.oPosY = y
    end
end
R.SOUND_SPOT_REACH, R.SOUND_SPOT_HEIGHT = 110, 60
function R.sound_step(row, o)
    local ms = G.gMarioStates
    if ms == nil then return 0 end
    local air = G.ACT_FLAG_AIR or 0x800
    local played = 0
    for i = 0, (G.MAX_PLAYERS or 16) - 1 do
        local m = ms[i]
        local here = false
        if m ~= nil and m.pos ~= nil and ((m.action or 0) & air) == 0 then
            local dx, dz = m.pos.x - o.oPosX, m.pos.z - o.oPosZ
            local dy = m.pos.y - o.oPosY
            here = dx * dx + dz * dz <= R.SOUND_SPOT_REACH * R.SOUND_SPOT_REACH
                and math.abs(dy) <= R.SOUND_SPOT_HEIGHT
        end
        if here and not row.on[i] then
            local sound = const(row.sound)
            if type(sound) == "number" then
                local gfx = m.marioObj ~= nil and m.marioObj.header ~= nil and m.marioObj.header.gfx or nil
                call("play_sound", sound, gfx ~= nil and gfx.cameraToObject or G.gGlobalSoundSource)
                played = played + 1
            end
        end
        row.on[i] = here
    end
    return played
end
R.RESET_FRAMES = 90
R.RESULT_FRAMES = 30 * 12
R.state = {}
R.MESSAGES = { "Go!", "The door is open", "Halfway there", "Well done", "Time is up" }
R.book = {}
function R.empty_state()
    return { raised = {}, down = {}, running = false, start = 0, elapsed = 0, result = nil,
             starts = {}, hits = {}, seq = 0, flips = 0 }
end
function R.state_of(key)
    local st = R.state[key]
    if st == nil then
        st = R.empty_state()
        R.state[key] = st
    end
    return st
end
local function wired(map, group)
    for _, w in ipairs(map.wires or {}) do
        if w[3] == "raise" and w[4] == group then return true end
    end
    return false
end
function R.standing(map, row)
    local st = R.state_of(map.key)
    if st.down[row.index] then return false end
    if row.pending ~= nil and frame_now() - row.pending < 45 then return false end
    return st.raised[row.link] == true or not wired(map, row.link)
end
function R.total_hits(st)
    local n = 0
    for _, v in pairs(st.hits) do n = n + v end
    return n
end
function R.message_text(arg)
    local n = tonumber(arg)
    if n ~= nil and R.MESSAGES[n] ~= nil then return R.MESSAGES[n] end
    if type(arg) == "string" and arg ~= "" then return arg end
    return R.MESSAGES[1]
end
function R.time_text(frames)
    if frames == nil or frames <= 0 then return "--:--.--" end
    local totalCentis = math.floor(frames * 100 / 30)
    local minutes = math.floor(totalCentis / 6000)
    local seconds = math.floor(totalCentis / 100) % 60
    local centis = totalCentis % 100
    return string.format("%.0f:%02.0f.%02.0f", minutes, seconds, centis)
end
function R.note_all(text, tone)
    if not is_host() then return false end
    send({ ofmaps = "note", text = text, tone = tone })
    R.note(text, tone)
    return true
end
function R.pack(st)
    local function keys(t)
        local out = {}
        for k, v in pairs(t) do if v then out[#out + 1] = k end end
        table.sort(out)
        return table.concat(out, ",")
    end
    local m = {}
    local links = {}
    for link in pairs(st.starts) do links[#links + 1] = link end
    table.sort(links)
    for _, link in ipairs(links) do
        m[#m + 1] = link .. "." .. st.starts[link].count .. "." .. st.starts[link].t0
    end
    local hits = {}
    local who = {}
    for gi in pairs(st.hits) do who[#who + 1] = gi end
    table.sort(who)
    for _, gi in ipairs(who) do hits[#hits + 1] = gi .. "." .. st.hits[gi] end
    return string.format("%d|%d|%d|%s|%s|%s|%s|%s|%d|%d", st.running and 1 or 0,
        math.floor(st.start), math.floor(st.elapsed),
        st.result ~= nil and (st.result.frames .. "." .. st.result.hits) or "",
        keys(st.raised), keys(st.down), table.concat(m, ","), table.concat(hits, ","), st.seq,
        st.flips or 0)
end
function R.unpack(text)
    local st = R.empty_state()
    if type(text) ~= "string" then return st end
    local f = {}
    for part in string.gmatch(text .. "|", "([^|]*)|") do f[#f + 1] = part end
    st.running = f[1] == "1"
    st.start, st.elapsed = tonumber(f[2]) or 0, tonumber(f[3]) or 0
    local rf, rh = string.match(f[4] or "", "^(%d+)%.(%d+)$")
    if rf ~= nil then st.result = { frames = tonumber(rf), hits = tonumber(rh) } end
    for n in string.gmatch(f[5] or "", "%d+") do st.raised[tonumber(n)] = true end
    for n in string.gmatch(f[6] or "", "%d+") do st.down[tonumber(n)] = true end
    for link, count, t0 in string.gmatch(f[7] or "", "(%d+)%.(%d+)%.(-?%d+)") do
        st.starts[tonumber(link)] = { count = tonumber(count), t0 = tonumber(t0) }
    end
    for gi, n in string.gmatch(f[8] or "", "(%d+)%.(%d+)") do st.hits[tonumber(gi)] = tonumber(n) end
    st.seq = tonumber(f[9]) or 0
    st.flips = tonumber(f[10]) or 0
    return st
end
local function publish(map)
    local st = R.state_of(map.key)
    st.seq = st.seq + 1
    send({ ofmaps = "st", key = map.key, s = R.pack(st) })
end
local function do_action(map, w, t)
    local st = R.state_of(map.key)
    local book = R.book[map.key]
    local act, dst = w[3], w[4]
    if act == "raise" then
        st.raised[dst] = true
        for i, row in ipairs(map.pieces or {}) do
            if row[1] == "target" and math.floor(tonumber(row[6]) or 1) == dst and st.down[i] then
                st.down[i] = nil
                book.fellAt[i] = nil
            end
        end
    elseif act == "start" then
        local s = st.starts[dst] or { count = 0, t0 = 0 }
        st.starts[dst] = { count = (s.count + 1) % 1000, t0 = t }
    elseif act == "timer_start" then
        st.running, st.start, st.elapsed, st.result = true, t, 0, nil
        st.down, st.raised, st.hits = {}, {}, {}
        book.fellAt, book.fired, book.startedAt = {}, {}, frame_now()
        R.note_all("Course started")
    elseif act == "timer_stop" then
        if st.running then st.elapsed = math.max(0, t - st.start) end
        st.running = false
    elseif act == "message" then
        R.note_all(R.message_text(w[5]))
    elseif act == "flip" then
        st.flips = (st.flips or 0) + 1
    elseif act == "finish" then
        if st.running then st.elapsed = math.max(0, t - st.start) end
        st.running = false
        st.result = { frames = st.elapsed, hits = R.total_hits(st) }
        R.note_all("Course done: " .. R.time_text(st.elapsed) .. "  " .. st.result.hits .. " hits", "give")
    end
end
local function run_wires(map, event, n, t)
    local ran = 0
    for pass = 1, 2 do
        for _, w in ipairs(map.wires or {}) do
            local first = w[3] == "timer_start"
            if w[1] == event and w[2] == n and ((pass == 1) == first) then
                do_action(map, w, t)
                ran = ran + 1
            end
        end
    end
    return ran
end
function R.host_report(map, event, n, t, id, left, who)
    if map == nil or not is_host() then return false end
    R.book[map.key] = R.book[map.key] or { fellAt = {}, fired = {} }
    local st = R.state_of(map.key)
    n, t = math.floor(tonumber(n) or -1), math.floor(tonumber(t) or 0)
    if n < 0 or n > 31 then return false end
    if event == "hit" then
        id = math.floor(tonumber(id) or 0)
        if id <= 0 or st.down[id] then return false end
        st.down[id] = true
        R.book[map.key].fellAt[id] = frame_now()
        who = math.floor(tonumber(who) or 0)
        st.hits[who] = (st.hits[who] or 0) + 1
        run_wires(map, "hit", n, t)
        if (tonumber(left) or 1) <= 0 then run_wires(map, "alldown", n, t) end
    elseif event == "pressed" or event == "entered" or event == "left" then
        run_wires(map, event, n, t)
    elseif event == "flip" then
        st.flips = (st.flips or 0) + 1
    else
        return false
    end
    publish(map)
    return true
end
function R.report(map, event, n, id, left)
    local np = me_player()
    local gi = np ~= nil and np.globalIndex or 0
    if is_host() then return R.host_report(map, event, n, area_now(), id, left, gi) end
    send({ ofmaps = "ev", key = map.key, e = event, n = n, t = area_now(), id = id or 0, left = left or 1,
        g = gi })
    return true
end
function R.host_step(map)
    local book = R.book[map.key]
    if book == nil then return false end
    local st = R.state_of(map.key)
    local now, changed = frame_now(), false
    for id, at in pairs(book.fellAt) do
        local row = (map.pieces or {})[id]
        if not st.down[id] then
            book.fellAt[id] = nil
        elseif row ~= nil and now - at >= R.RESET_FRAMES
            and not wired(map, math.floor(tonumber(row[6]) or 1)) then
            st.down[id] = nil
            book.fellAt[id] = nil
            changed = true
        end
    end
    if st.running and book.startedAt ~= nil then
        local seconds = (now - book.startedAt) / FPS
        for i, w in ipairs(map.wires or {}) do
            if w[1] == "timer" and seconds >= w[2] and not book.fired[i] then
                book.fired[i] = true
                do_action(map, w, st.start + math.floor(seconds * FPS))
                changed = true
            end
        end
    end
    if changed then publish(map) end
    return changed
end
function R.host_attend()
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        local st = R.state[map.key]
        if st ~= nil and (st.running or next(st.raised) ~= nil or next(st.down) ~= nil) then
            local anybody = false
            for i = 0, (G.MAX_PLAYERS or 16) - 1 do
                local np = G.gNetworkPlayers ~= nil and G.gNetworkPlayers[i] or nil
                if np ~= nil and (i == 0 or np.connected) and np.currLevelNum == map.level then
                    anybody = true
                end
            end
            if not anybody then
                local flips = st.flips
                R.state[map.key] = R.empty_state()
                R.state[map.key].flips = flips
                R.book[map.key] = { fellAt = {}, fired = {} }
                publish(map)
            end
        end
    end
end
function R.target_step(row, o)
    local map = row.map
    local up = R.standing(map, row)
    local goal = up and 0 or 0x4000
    local pitch = math.floor(tonumber(o.oFaceAnglePitch) or 0)
    if pitch < goal then pitch = math.min(goal, pitch + 0x0C00)
    elseif pitch > goal then pitch = math.max(goal, pitch - 0x0C00) end
    o.oFaceAnglePitch = pitch
    local m = me_mario()
    if not up or m == nil then return end
    local attacking = ((m.action or 0) & (G.ACT_FLAG_ATTACKING or 0x00800000)) ~= 0
    local dx, dz = m.pos.x - o.oPosX, m.pos.z - o.oPosZ
    local dy = (m.pos.y + 60) - (o.oPosY + 120)
    if attacking and dx * dx + dz * dz <= 170 * 170 and math.abs(dy) <= 200 then
        local left = 0
        for _, other in ipairs(R.pieces) do
            if other ~= row and other.kind == "target" and other.link == row.link
                and R.standing(map, other) then
                left = left + 1
            end
        end
        row.pending = frame_now()
        play(G.SOUND_ACTION_HIT)
        R.report(map, "hit", row.link, row.index, left)
    end
end
function R.zone_step(row, o)
    local m = me_mario()
    if m == nil then return end
    local dx, dz, dy = m.pos.x - o.oPosX, m.pos.z - o.oPosZ, m.pos.y - o.oPosY
    local inside = dx * dx + dz * dz <= 300 * 300 and dy >= -50 and dy <= 400
    if row.inside ~= nil and inside ~= row.inside then
        R.report(row.map, inside and "entered" or "left", row.link)
    end
    row.inside = inside
end
function R.wired_step(map)
    local st = R.state_of(map.key)
    for _, mv in ipairs(R.movers) do
        if mv.trigger == "wired" and mv.link ~= nil then
            local s = st.starts[mv.link]
            if s ~= nil and mv.seen ~= s.count then
                mv.seen = s.count
                R.mover_go(mv, s.t0)
            end
        end
    end
end
R.STAR_REACH = 160
R.COIN_STAR_ID = 30
R.RED_STAR_ID = 31
R.COIN_STAR_COINS = 100
R.COIN_STAR_RISE = 300
R.RED_STAR_RISE = 150
R.DANCE_CUT = 79
R.masks = {}
local function store_key(map)
    return "bonus_" .. squash(map.key)
end
function R.store_load(map, k)
    local saved = type(map) == "table" and type(map.saved) == "table" and map.saved or nil
    if saved ~= nil and saved[k] ~= nil then return tonumber(saved[k]) end
    return callnum("mod_storage_load", k)
end
function R.store_save(map, k, v)
    local q = type(map) == "table" and type(map.saveQueue) == "table" and map.saveQueue or nil
    if q ~= nil and type(map.saved) == "table" then
        map.saved[k] = tostring(v)
        q[k] = tostring(v)
        return true
    end
    return call("mod_storage_save", k, tostring(v))
end
function R.coin_star(map)
    return map ~= nil and map.coinStar == true and type(map.stars) == "table" and #map.stars > 0
end
function R.red_star(map)
    return map ~= nil and (tonumber(map.redCoins) or 0) > 0
end
function R.star_ids(map)
    local out = {}
    for i = 1, #(map.stars or {}) do out[#out + 1] = i end
    if R.coin_star(map) then out[#out + 1] = R.COIN_STAR_ID end
    if R.red_star(map) then out[#out + 1] = R.RED_STAR_ID end
    return out
end
function R.star_valid(map, id)
    for _, i in ipairs(R.star_ids(map)) do if i == id then return true end end
    return false
end
function R.star_name(map, id)
    if id == R.COIN_STAR_ID then return "100 Coins" end
    if id == R.RED_STAR_ID then return "Red Coins" end
    local row = (map.stars or {})[id]
    local name = row ~= nil and row[4] or nil
    if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then return name end
    return "Star " .. id
end
function R.star_got(map, id)
    local mask = R.masks[map.key] or 0
    return (mask >> (id - 1)) & 1 == 1
end
function R.stars_got(map)
    local n = 0
    for _, id in ipairs(R.star_ids(map)) do
        if R.star_got(map, id) then n = n + 1 end
    end
    return n
end
function R.stars_publish()
    if not is_host() then return nil end
    local parts = {}
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        local mask = math.floor(R.store_load(map, store_key(map)) or 0)
        R.masks[map.key] = mask
        if mask > 0 then parts[#parts + 1] = map.key .. ":" .. mask end
    end
    local text = table.concat(parts, ",")
    send({ ofmaps = "stars", s = text ~= "" and text or nil })
    return text
end
function R.stars_heard(text)
    R.masks = {}
    for key, mask in string.gmatch(tostring(text or ""), "([%w_]+):(%d+)") do
        R.masks[key] = tonumber(mask)
    end
end
function R.star_record(map, id, who)
    if map == nil or not is_host() or not R.star_valid(map, id) then return false end
    local mask = math.floor(R.store_load(map, store_key(map)) or 0)
    if (mask >> (id - 1)) & 1 == 1 then return false end
    mask = mask | (1 << (id - 1))
    R.store_save(map, store_key(map), mask)
    R.masks[map.key] = mask
    R.stars_publish()
    local line = "> " .. YELLOW .. tostring(who or "Somebody") .. WHITE .. " got " .. GREEN ..
        R.star_name(map, id) .. WHITE .. " in " .. GREEN .. tostring(map.name or map.key) .. WHITE
    send({ ofmaps = "say", text = line, raw = true })
    say(line)
    return true
end
function R.star_dance(m)
    if m == nil then return false end
    return SHARED_VENUES.dance_start(m)
end
function R.dance_step(m)
    return SHARED_VENUES.dance_step(m)
end
local function star_model(taken)
    if taken and G.E_MODEL_TRANSPARENT_STAR ~= nil then return G.E_MODEL_TRANSPARENT_STAR end
    return G.E_MODEL_STAR
end
local function put_star(map, id, x, y, z)
    local taken = R.star_got(map, id)
    local row = { kind = "star", map = map, id = id, takenAtPlace = taken }
    local o = spawn(THING, star_model(taken), x, y, z, row, function(obj) obj.oFaceAngleYaw = 0 end)
    R.stars[id] = row
    return o
end
function R.place_stars(map)
    R.coinStarDone, R.redStarDone = false, false
    R.reds = nil
    local n = 0
    for i, s in ipairs(map.stars or {}) do
        if put_star(map, i, s[1], s[2], s[3]) then n = n + 1 end
    end
    return n
end
R.starSpin = 0
function R.star_step(row, o)
    local map = row.map
    o.oFaceAngleYaw = R.starSpin
    local taken = R.star_got(map, row.id)
    if taken and not row.takenAtPlace then
        call("obj_mark_for_deletion", o)
        return
    end
    if taken or row.touched or R.select ~= nil then return end
    local m = me_mario()
    if m == nil or m.pos == nil or (m.health or 0x880) < 0x100 then return end
    local dx, dy, dz = m.pos.x - o.oPosX, (m.pos.y + 80) - o.oPosY, m.pos.z - o.oPosZ
    if dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz > R.STAR_REACH * R.STAR_REACH then return end
    row.touched = true
    R.star_touch(map, row.id)
end
function R.star_touch(map, id)
    play(G.SOUND_MENU_STAR_SOUND)
    R.star_dance(me_mario())
    local before = R.stars_got(map)
    local np = me_player()
    if is_host() then
        R.star_record(map, id, np ~= nil and np.name or "Somebody")
    else
        send({ ofmaps = "took", key = map.key, id = id, who = np ~= nil and np.name or "Somebody" })
    end
    local all = #R.star_ids(map)
    R.note("Bonus star " .. math.min(all, before + 1) .. "/" .. all .. ": " .. R.star_name(map, id), "give")
    return true
end
function R.coin_star_step(map, m)
    if R.coinStarDone or m == nil or m.pos == nil or not R.coin_star(map) or R.select ~= nil then
        return false
    end
    if (m.numCoins or 0) < R.COIN_STAR_COINS then return false end
    R.coinStarDone = true
    put_star(map, R.COIN_STAR_ID, m.pos.x, m.pos.y + R.COIN_STAR_RISE, m.pos.z)
    return true
end
function R.red_step(map)
    if not R.red_star(map) or G.id_bhvRedCoin == nil then return false end
    local live, last = {}, nil
    each_of(G.id_bhvRedCoin, function(o)
        live[#live + 1] = { o.oPosX, o.oPosY, o.oPosZ }
    end)
    local reds = R.reds
    if reds == nil then
        reds = { most = 0, at = {} }
        R.reds = reds
    end
    if #live > reds.most then reds.most = #live end
    if reds.most > 0 and #live < #reds.at then
        for _, p in ipairs(reds.at) do
            local still = false
            for _, q in ipairs(live) do
                if math.abs(p[1] - q[1]) < 1 and math.abs(p[3] - q[3]) < 1 then still = true end
            end
            if not still then last = p end
        end
        reds.last = last or reds.last
        local got = reds.most - #live
        play((G.SOUND_MENU_COLLECT_RED_COIN or 0) + (math.max(0, math.min(7, got - 1)) << 16))
    end
    reds.at = live
    if #live == 0 and reds.most > 0 and not R.redStarDone and reds.last ~= nil then
        R.redStarDone = true
        put_star(map, R.RED_STAR_ID, reds.last[1], reds.last[2] + R.RED_STAR_RISE, reds.last[3])
        return true
    end
    return false
end
R.HUD_STARS_X, R.HUD_ROW, R.HUD_GAP = 78, 15, 8
function R.draw_count()
    local worlds, count = 0, 0
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        if type(map.stars) == "table" and #map.stars > 0 then
            worlds = worlds + 1
            count = count + R.stars_got(map)
        end
    end
    if worlds == 0 or R.select ~= nil or call("hud_is_hidden") == true then return false end
    local flags = call("hud_get_value", G.HUD_DISPLAY_FLAGS)
    if type(flags) ~= "number" or G.HUD_DISPLAY_FLAG_STAR_COUNT == nil
        or flags & G.HUD_DISPLAY_FLAG_STAR_COUNT == 0 then
        return false
    end
    return SHARED_VENUES.counter_paint(count)
end
R.SELECT_AT = { 0, 7000, 0 }
R.SELECT_WALL_Z, R.SELECT_CAMERA = -1500, 1000
R.SELECT_STAR_Y, R.SELECT_STAR_Z, R.SELECT_FOV = 248, -300, 45
R.SELECT_FADE, R.SELECT_READY, R.SELECT_AFTER, R.SELECT_ENTRY_FADE = 16, 11, 10, 0x1A
R.SELECT_STICK = 60
R.SELECT_WALL_GEO = "of_star_backdrop_geo"
R.SELECT_MUSIC = 0x0D
R.SELECT_MUSIC_FADE = 0xBE
R.COURSE_FIRST_BONUS = 16
R.SELECT_AWAY_AT = { 0, 20000, 0 }
R.SELECT_ARRIVE_FRAMES = 150
R.selectArrive = nil
R.MENU_GLYPH, R.DIALOG_FONT = 0.45, 0.5
R.select = nil
local SHARED_SELECT = (function()
    local FIELD = { selecting = "select", selectArrive = "selectArrive" }
    local venues = setmetatable({}, {
        __index = function(_, k)
            if FIELD[k] ~= nil then return R[FIELD[k]] end
            if k == "built" then return R.built ~= nil and R.built.key or nil end
            if k == "levelSeq" then return R.level_track ~= nil and R.level_track() or nil end
            return R[k]
        end,
        __newindex = function(t, k, v)
            if FIELD[k] ~= nil then R[FIELD[k]] = v else rawset(t, k, v) end
        end,
    })
    local model_of = model
    venues.optional = optional
    venues.LAYOUTS = setmetatable({}, { __index = function(_, k)
        return R.byKey ~= nil and R.byKey[k] or nil
    end })
    function venues.select_wanted(key)
        local map = venues.LAYOUTS[key]
        return map ~= nil and R.extras(map) and type(map.stars) == "table" and #map.stars > 0
    end
    function venues.bonus_taken(key, i)
        local map = venues.LAYOUTS[key]
        return map ~= nil and R.star_got(map, i) == true
    end
    function venues.settle_mood()
        if R.worn ~= nil then R.settle_mood(R.worn) end
    end
    local hudHeld = nil
    local SELECT_HUD = { "HUD_DISPLAY_FLAG_LIVES", "HUD_DISPLAY_FLAG_COIN_COUNT",
        "HUD_DISPLAY_FLAG_STAR_COUNT", "HUD_DISPLAY_FLAG_CAMERA_AND_POWER", "HUD_DISPLAY_FLAG_TIMER" }
    function venues.select_hud(down)
        local flags = call("hud_get_value", G.HUD_DISPLAY_FLAGS)
        if type(flags) ~= "number" then return end
        if down then
            local mask = 0
            for _, name in ipairs(SELECT_HUD) do mask = mask | (G[name] or 0) end
            if hudHeld == nil then hudHeld = flags & mask end
            call("hud_set_value", G.HUD_DISPLAY_FLAGS, flags & ~mask)
        elseif hudHeld ~= nil then
            call("hud_set_value", G.HUD_DISPLAY_FLAGS, flags | hudHeld)
            hudHeld = nil
        end
    end
    function venues.picking(localIndex) return R.is_picking(localIndex) end
    function venues.select_publish(on)
        send({ ofmaps = "pick", on = on == true, g = me_player() ~= nil and me_player().globalIndex or 0 })
        return true
    end
    function venues.alive(o) return o ~= nil and o.activeFlags ~= nil end
    function venues.spawn_non_sync(kind, mdl, x, y, z, setup)
        return call("spawn_non_sync_object", kind, mdl, x, y, z, setup)
    end
    function venues.keep_awake(m) return R.keep_awake(m) end
    venues.binding = fn
    function venues.play_sound(id) play(id) end
    function venues.hushed_elsewhere() return 0 end
    function venues.music_after_select()
        if R.music_back ~= nil then R.music_back() end
    end
    function venues.pad_more() return 0, 0 end
    function venues.warp_local(a)
        local map = a ~= nil and R.byKey ~= nil and R.byKey[a.key] or nil
        return map ~= nil and R.warp(map)
    end
local function add_flag(value, flag)
    if flag == nil or flag == 0 then return value end
    value = value or 0
    if math.floor(value / flag) % 2 == 1 then return value end
    return value + flag
end
function venues.scene()
    local s = venues.selecting
    return s ~= nil and s.at or venues.SELECT_AT
end
function venues.select_keep_pieces(s)
    if s == nil then return 0 end
    local bhv = rawget(_G, "id_bhvStaticObject")
    if bhv == nil then return 0 end
    local back = 0
    local function gone(o) return o == nil or not venues.alive(o) or o.activeFlags == 0 end
    if gone(s.wall) then
        local wall = model_of(venues.SELECT_WALL_GEO)
        if wall ~= nil then
            local at = s.at
            s.wall = venues.spawn_non_sync(bhv, wall, at[1], at[2], at[3] + venues.SELECT_WALL_Z,
                function(o) o.oFaceAngleYaw = 0 end)
            if s.wall ~= nil then back = back + 1 end
        end
    end
    if s.phase == "pick" then
        for i = 1, s.n do
            if gone(s.stars[i]) then
                local x, y, z = venues.select_star_at(i, s.n)
                local model = venues.bonus_taken(s.key, i) and E_MODEL_STAR or E_MODEL_TRANSPARENT_STAR
                s.stars[i] = venues.spawn_non_sync(bhv, model or E_MODEL_STAR, x, y, z,
                    function(o) o.oFaceAngleYaw = 0 end)
                if s.stars[i] ~= nil then back = back + 1 end
            end
        end
    end
    return back
end
function venues.select_star_at(i, n)
    local at = venues.scene()
    return at[1] + 75 - 75 * n + 152 * (i - 1), at[2] + venues.SELECT_STAR_Y,
        at[3] + venues.SELECT_STAR_Z
end
function venues.select_camera_z()
    local fov = tonumber(venues.optional("get_current_fov", venues.SELECT_FOV)) or venues.SELECT_FOV
    if fov <= 1 or fov >= 179 then fov = venues.SELECT_FOV end
    local plane = venues.SELECT_CAMERA - venues.SELECT_STAR_Z
    local k = math.tan(math.rad(venues.SELECT_FOV / 2)) / math.tan(math.rad(fov / 2))
    return plane * k + venues.SELECT_STAR_Z
end
local function copy_vec(v)
    if v == nil then return nil end
    return { v.x, v.y, v.z }
end
local function put_vec(v, t)
    if v == nil or t == nil then return end
    v.x, v.y, v.z = t[1], t[2], t[3]
end
local function clear_level_queue()
    for _ = 1, 8 do
        local head = venues.optional("get_current_background_music", nil)
        if type(head) ~= "number" or head < 0 or head == 65535 then return end
        venues.optional("stop_background_music", nil, head)
        local after = venues.optional("get_current_background_music", nil)
        if after == head then return end
    end
end
function venues.select_open(key, away)
    if venues.selecting ~= nil or not venues.select_wanted(key) then return false end
    local layout = venues.LAYOUTS[key]
    local n = #layout.stars
    local s = { key = key, n = n, cursor = n, timer = 0, faded = 0, phase = "in", out = 0,
                stars = {}, sizes = {}, hold = 0, holdTimer = 0, music = true,
                away = away, at = away ~= nil and venues.SELECT_AWAY_AT or venues.SELECT_AT }
    for i = 1, n do
        if not venues.bonus_taken(key, i) then s.cursor = i break end
    end
    local bhv = rawget(_G, "id_bhvStaticObject")
    if bhv == nil then return false end
    local wall = model_of(venues.SELECT_WALL_GEO)
    if wall ~= nil then
        local at = s.at
        s.wall = venues.spawn_non_sync(bhv, wall, at[1], at[2], at[3] + venues.SELECT_WALL_Z,
            function(o) o.oFaceAngleYaw = 0 end)
    end
    if gLakituState ~= nil then
        s.cam = { pos = copy_vec(gLakituState.pos), curPos = copy_vec(gLakituState.curPos),
                  goalPos = copy_vec(gLakituState.goalPos), focus = copy_vec(gLakituState.focus),
                  curFocus = copy_vec(gLakituState.curFocus),
                  goalFocus = copy_vec(gLakituState.goalFocus), yaw = gLakituState.yaw }
    end
    venues.selecting = s
    venues.settle_mood()
    venues.select_hud(true)
    venues.select_hush(s)
    venues.select_publish(true)
    clear_level_queue()
    venues.optional("play_transition", nil, WARP_TRANSITION_FADE_FROM_COLOR,
        venues.SELECT_FADE, 255, 255, 255)
    venues.select_camera()
    venues.optional("skip_camera_interpolation", nil)
    return true
end
function venues.select_begin()
    local s = venues.selecting
    if s == nil or s.phase ~= "in" then return false end
    local bhv = rawget(_G, "id_bhvStaticObject")
    for i = 1, s.n do
        local x, y, z = venues.select_star_at(i, s.n)
        local model = venues.bonus_taken(s.key, i) and E_MODEL_STAR or E_MODEL_TRANSPARENT_STAR
        if bhv ~= nil then
            s.stars[i] = venues.spawn_non_sync(bhv, model or E_MODEL_STAR, x, y, z,
                function(o) o.oFaceAngleYaw = 0 end)
        end
        s.sizes[i] = 1.0
    end
    s.phase, s.timer = "pick", 0
    clear_level_queue()
    venues.optional("play_music", nil, SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL,
        rawget(_G, "SEQ_MENU_STAR_SELECT") or venues.SELECT_MUSIC, 0)
    return true
end
function venues.select_camera()
    local s = gLakituState
    if s == nil or s.pos == nil then return false end
    local at = venues.scene()
    local pos = { at[1], at[2], at[3] + venues.select_camera_z() }
    put_vec(s.pos, pos)
    put_vec(s.curPos, pos)
    put_vec(s.goalPos, pos)
    put_vec(s.focus, at)
    put_vec(s.curFocus, at)
    put_vec(s.goalFocus, at)
    s.posHSpeed, s.posVSpeed, s.focHSpeed, s.focVSpeed = 0, 0, 0, 0
    s.yaw = 0
    return true
end
function venues.holds_camera()
    return venues.selecting ~= nil
end
function venues.select_hush_mask()
    local count = tonumber(rawget(_G, "SOUND_BANK_COUNT")) or 13
    local mask = (1 << count) - 1
    local menu = rawget(_G, "SOUND_BANK_MENU")
    if type(menu) == "number" then mask = mask & ~(1 << menu) end
    return mask
end
function venues.select_hush(sel)
    if sel == nil or venues.binding("sound_banks_disable") == nil then return false end
    local mask = venues.select_hush_mask()
    venues.optional("sound_banks_disable", nil, 0, mask)
    if sel.hush == nil then
        sel.hush = mask
        venues.optional("stop_sounds_in_continuous_banks", nil)
    end
    return true
end
function venues.select_unhush(sel)
    if sel == nil or sel.hush == nil then return false end
    venues.optional("sound_banks_enable", nil, 0, sel.hush & ~venues.hushed_elsewhere())
    sel.hush = nil
    return true
end
local PICK_HIDDEN = GRAPH_RENDER_INVISIBLE or 0x10
function venues.picking_before(m)
    if m == nil or not venues.picking(m.playerIndex) then return false end
    m.visibleToObjects = false
    if m.playerIndex == 0 then venues.keep_awake(m) end
    return true
end
function venues.picking_hide(m)
    if m == nil or m.playerIndex == 0 or not venues.picking(m.playerIndex) then return false end
    local obj = m.marioObj
    if obj == nil or obj.header == nil or obj.header.gfx == nil or obj.header.gfx.node == nil then
        return false
    end
    local node = obj.header.gfx.node
    node.flags = add_flag(node.flags or 0, PICK_HIDDEN)
    return true
end
local function player_of_object(o)
    if o == nil or gMarioStates == nil then return nil end
    for i = 0, (MAX_PLAYERS or 16) - 1 do
        local other = gMarioStates[i]
        if other ~= nil and other.marioObj ~= nil and other.marioObj == o then return i end
    end
    return nil
end
function venues.picking_interact(m, o, interactType)
    if m == nil or interactType ~= INTERACT_PLAYER then return nil end
    if venues.picking(m.playerIndex) then return false end
    local other = player_of_object(o)
    if other ~= nil and venues.picking(other) then return false end
    return nil
end
function venues.select_music_back(sel, levelGone)
    if sel == nil or not sel.music then return false end
    local menu = rawget(_G, "SEQ_MENU_STAR_SELECT") or venues.SELECT_MUSIC
    local head = venues.optional("get_current_background_music", nil)
    if type(head) == "number" and head % 256 == menu % 256 then
        venues.optional("stop_background_music", nil, menu)
    end
    if levelGone then return true end
    if venues.levelSeq ~= nil and venues.levelSeq ~= 0 then
        venues.optional("play_music", nil, SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL, venues.levelSeq, 0)
    end
    venues.music_after_select()
    return true
end
function venues.awake_again(m)
    if m == nil then return false end
    venues.keep_awake(m)
    if m.action == ACT_IDLE then m.actionState, m.actionTimer = 0, 0 end
    return true
end
function venues.select_close(fadeIn, levelGone)
    local s = venues.selecting
    if s == nil then return false end
    venues.selecting = nil
    venues.awake_again(gMarioStates ~= nil and gMarioStates[0] or nil)
    venues.settle_mood()
    for _, o in pairs(s.stars) do
        if o ~= nil and venues.alive(o) and o.activeFlags ~= 0 then
            venues.optional("obj_mark_for_deletion", nil, o)
        end
    end
    if s.wall ~= nil and venues.alive(s.wall) and s.wall.activeFlags ~= 0 then
        venues.optional("obj_mark_for_deletion", nil, s.wall)
    end
    local c = gLakituState
    if c ~= nil and s.cam ~= nil then
        put_vec(c.pos, s.cam.pos)
        put_vec(c.curPos, s.cam.curPos)
        put_vec(c.goalPos, s.cam.goalPos)
        put_vec(c.focus, s.cam.focus)
        put_vec(c.curFocus, s.cam.curFocus)
        put_vec(c.goalFocus, s.cam.goalFocus)
        if s.cam.yaw ~= nil then c.yaw = s.cam.yaw end
        c.posHSpeed, c.posVSpeed, c.focHSpeed, c.focVSpeed = 0.3, 0.3, 0.8, 0.3
    end
    venues.optional("skip_camera_interpolation", nil)
    venues.select_hud(false)
    venues.select_music_back(s, levelGone)
    venues.select_unhush(s)
    venues.select_publish(false)
    if fadeIn then
        venues.optional("play_transition", nil, WARP_TRANSITION_FADE_FROM_COLOR,
            venues.SELECT_ENTRY_FADE, 255, 255, 255)
        local m = gMarioStates ~= nil and gMarioStates[0] or nil
        if m ~= nil and CHAR_SOUND_LETS_A_GO ~= nil then
            venues.optional("play_character_sound", nil, m, CHAR_SOUND_LETS_A_GO)
        end
    end
    return true
end
function venues.select_input(m)
    local s = venues.selecting
    if s == nil or m == nil or m.playerIndex ~= 0 or m.controller == nil then return false end
    local c = m.controller
    local stick = tonumber(c.rawStickX) or tonumber(c.stickX) or 0
    local down = tonumber(c.buttonDown) or 0
    local pressed = tonumber(c.buttonPressed) or 0
    local moreDown, morePressed = venues.pad_more(m)
    down, pressed = down | moreDown, pressed | morePressed
    if s.phase == "pick" then
        local index = 0
        if stick > venues.SELECT_STICK or (R_JPAD ~= nil and down & R_JPAD ~= 0) then
            index = index + 2
        end
        if stick < -venues.SELECT_STICK or (L_JPAD ~= nil and down & L_JPAD ~= 0) then
            index = index + 1
        end
        local fresh = (index ~ s.hold) & index
        if fresh == 2 and s.cursor < s.n then
            s.cursor = s.cursor + 1
            venues.play_sound(SOUND_MENU_CHANGE_SELECT)
        elseif fresh == 1 and s.cursor > 1 then
            s.cursor = s.cursor - 1
            venues.play_sound(SOUND_MENU_CHANGE_SELECT)
        end
        if s.holdTimer == 10 then
            s.holdTimer, s.hold = 8, 0
        else
            s.holdTimer, s.hold = s.holdTimer + 1, index
        end
        if index & 3 == 0 then s.holdTimer = 0 end
        if s.timer >= venues.SELECT_READY
            and pressed & (A_BUTTON | B_BUTTON | START_BUTTON) ~= 0 then
            venues.select_choose()
        end
    end
    c.buttonDown, c.buttonPressed = 0, 0
    c.stickX, c.stickY, c.stickMag = 0, 0, 0
    return true
end
function venues.select_choose()
    local s = venues.selecting
    if s == nil or s.phase ~= "pick" then return false end
    s.phase, s.out = "out", 0
    venues.play_sound(SOUND_MENU_STAR_SOUND)
    if s.music then venues.optional("fadeout_level_music", nil, venues.SELECT_MUSIC_FADE) end
    venues.optional("play_transition", nil, WARP_TRANSITION_FADE_INTO_COLOR,
        venues.SELECT_FADE, 255, 255, 255)
    return true
end
function venues.select_step()
    local s = venues.selecting
    if s == nil then return false end
    if not venues.select_wanted(s.key) or (s.away == nil and venues.built ~= s.key) then
        venues.select_close(false)
        if s.away ~= nil then venues.select_unwhite() end
        return false
    end
    venues.select_hush(s)
    if s.phase ~= "out" then venues.select_keep_pieces(s) end
    if s.phase == "in" then
        s.faded = s.faded + 1
        if s.faded >= venues.SELECT_FADE then venues.select_begin() end
        venues.select_camera()
        return true
    end
    s.timer = s.timer + 1
    if s.phase == "pick" then
        for i, o in pairs(s.stars) do
            if o ~= nil and venues.alive(o) and o.activeFlags ~= 0 then
                if i == s.cursor then
                    s.sizes[i] = math.min(1.3, s.sizes[i] + 0.1)
                    o.oFaceAngleYaw = (o.oFaceAngleYaw + 0x800) % 0x10000
                else
                    s.sizes[i] = math.max(1.0, s.sizes[i] - 0.1)
                    o.oFaceAngleYaw = 0
                end
                venues.optional("obj_scale", nil, o, s.sizes[i])
            end
        end
    end
    venues.select_camera()
    if s.phase == "out" then
        s.out = s.out + 1
        if s.out >= venues.SELECT_FADE + venues.SELECT_AFTER then
            if s.away ~= nil then venues.select_leave(s) else venues.select_close(true) end
        end
    end
    return true
end
function venues.select_leave(s)
    local a = s ~= nil and s.away or nil
    if a == nil or venues.selecting ~= s then return false end
    venues.selectPassing = true
    local ok = venues.warp_local(a)
    venues.selectPassing = false
    if not ok then
        venues.select_close(false)
        venues.select_unwhite()
        return false
    end
    for _, o in pairs(s.stars) do
        if o ~= nil and venues.alive(o) and o.activeFlags ~= 0 then
            venues.optional("obj_mark_for_deletion", nil, o)
        end
    end
    if s.wall ~= nil and venues.alive(s.wall) and s.wall.activeFlags ~= 0 then
        venues.optional("obj_mark_for_deletion", nil, s.wall)
    end
    s.stars, s.wall, s.cam = {}, nil, nil
    s.arriveFrames = 0
    venues.selecting = nil
    venues.settle_mood()
    venues.selectArrive = s
    return true
end
function venues.select_unwhite()
    venues.optional("play_transition", nil, WARP_TRANSITION_FADE_FROM_COLOR,
        venues.SELECT_FADE, 255, 255, 255)
    return true
end
function venues.select_arrived(s, landed)
    if s == nil then return false end
    if venues.selectArrive == s then venues.selectArrive = nil end
    venues.awake_again(gMarioStates ~= nil and gMarioStates[0] or nil)
    venues.select_hud(false)
    venues.select_music_back(s, true)
    venues.select_unhush(s)
    venues.select_publish(false)
    venues.optional("play_transition", nil, WARP_TRANSITION_FADE_FROM_COLOR,
        venues.SELECT_ENTRY_FADE, 255, 255, 255)
    local m = gMarioStates ~= nil and gMarioStates[0] or nil
    if landed and m ~= nil and CHAR_SOUND_LETS_A_GO ~= nil then
        venues.optional("play_character_sound", nil, m, CHAR_SOUND_LETS_A_GO)
    end
    return true
end
    return venues
end)()
R.scene = SHARED_SELECT.scene
R.select_star_at = SHARED_SELECT.select_star_at
R.select_camera_z = SHARED_SELECT.select_camera_z
R.select_camera = SHARED_SELECT.select_camera
R.select_begin = SHARED_SELECT.select_begin
R.select_keep_pieces = SHARED_SELECT.select_keep_pieces
R.awake_again = SHARED_SELECT.awake_again
R.select_close = SHARED_SELECT.select_close
R.select_input = SHARED_SELECT.select_input
R.select_choose = SHARED_SELECT.select_choose
R.select_step = SHARED_SELECT.select_step
R.select_leave = SHARED_SELECT.select_leave
R.select_unwhite = SHARED_SELECT.select_unwhite
R.select_arrived = SHARED_SELECT.select_arrived
R.picking_before = SHARED_SELECT.picking_before
R.picking_hide = SHARED_SELECT.picking_hide
R.picking_interact = SHARED_SELECT.picking_interact
function R.open_select(map, away)
    if map == nil or not R.extras(map) then return false end
    if away ~= nil then away.key = away.key or map.key end
    return SHARED_SELECT.select_open(map.key, away)
end
function R.keep_awake(m)
    if m == nil then return false end
    local act = m.action
    if act == G.ACT_IDLE then
        if (m.actionState or 0) >= 3 then
            m.actionState = 0
            m.actionTimer = 0
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    if act ~= nil and (act == G.ACT_START_SLEEPING or act == G.ACT_SLEEPING
            or act == G.ACT_WAKING_UP) then
        call("set_mario_action", m, G.ACT_IDLE, 0)
        return true
    end
    return false
end
function R.select_number(map)
    if map == nil then return 0 end
    local rows = {}
    for _, m in ipairs(R.maps) do rows[m.key] = m end
    if rows[map.key] == nil then rows[map.key] = map end
    return SHARED_VENUES.course_number(rows, map.key)
end
function R.coin_best(map)
    return math.floor(R.store_load(map, "coins_" .. squash(map.key)) or 0)
end
function R.coin_note(map, coins)
    coins = math.floor(tonumber(coins) or 0)
    if coins <= R.coin_best(map) then return false end
    R.store_save(map, "coins_" .. squash(map.key), coins)
    return true
end
function R.draw_select()
    local s = R.select
    local map = s ~= nil and (s.map or R.byKey[s.key]) or nil
    if map == nil then return false end
    return SHARED_VENUES.select_paint({ best = R.coin_best(map), world = map.name or map.key,
        number = R.select_number(map), phase = s.phase, n = s.n, act = R.star_name(map, s.cursor) })
end
R.picking = {}
function R.is_picking(localIndex)
    if localIndex == nil then return false end
    if localIndex == 0 then return R.select ~= nil end
    local np = G.gNetworkPlayers ~= nil and G.gNetworkPlayers[localIndex] or nil
    return np ~= nil and R.picking[np.globalIndex] == true
end
R.LAY_SETTLE = 30
R.LAY_WAIT = 150
R.LAY_PER_FRAME = 8
R.MARK = 0x4F4D4150
R.lay = nil
local function mark_here()
    local found = false
    each_of(THING, function(o)
        if math.floor(tonumber(o.oBehParams) or 0) == R.MARK and R.things[o] == nil then
            found = true
            return false
        end
    end)
    return found
end
R.mark_here = mark_here
local function lower_in_room()
    local me, nps = me_player(), G.gNetworkPlayers
    if me == nil or nps == nil then return false end
    local mine = tonumber(me.globalIndex) or 0
    for i = 1, (G.MAX_PLAYERS or 16) - 1 do
        local np = nps[i]
        if same_room(np, me) and (tonumber(np.globalIndex) or 99) < mine then return true end
    end
    return false
end
R.TEND_FRESH = 6
function R.lay_row(map, row)
    local id = bhv(row.bhv)
    if id == nil then return nil end
    R.tendFresh = R.TEND_FRESH
    local at = row.at or { 0, 0, 0, 0 }
    local yaw = to_angle(at[4])
    local pitch = row.pitch ~= nil and to_angle(row.pitch) or nil
    local mdl = model(row.model) or G.E_MODEL_NONE
    local o = call("spawn_sync_object", id, mdl, at[1], at[2], at[3], function(obj)
        obj.oFaceAngleYaw, obj.oMoveAngleYaw = yaw, yaw
        if pitch ~= nil then obj.oFaceAnglePitch = pitch end
        if row.second ~= nil then obj.oBehParams2ndByte = row.second end
        if row.params ~= nil then obj.oBehParams = row.params end
        if row.reach ~= nil then obj.oCollisionDistance = row.reach end
        if row.action ~= nil then obj.oAction = row.action end
    end)
    if o ~= nil and type(row.friend) == "table" then
        local fid = bhv(row.friend.bhv)
        if fid ~= nil then
            call("spawn_sync_object", fid, model(row.friend.model) or G.E_MODEL_NONE, at[1], at[2], at[3],
                function(obj)
                    if row.friend.second ~= nil then
                        obj.oBehParams2ndByte = row.friend.second
                        obj.oBehParams = (math.floor(row.friend.second) & 0xFF) << 16
                    end
                    obj.parentObj = o
                end)
        end
    end
    return o
end
function R.lay_step(map)
    local lay = R.lay
    if lay == nil or lay.map ~= map then
        lay = { map = map, waited = 0, next = 1, rows = {} }
        for _, t in ipairs(map.things or {}) do
            if t.kind == "spawn" then lay.rows[#lay.rows + 1] = t end
        end
        lay.done = #lay.rows == 0
        R.lay = lay
    end
    if lay.done then return 0 end
    local np = me_player()
    if np == nil or not np.currAreaSyncValid then return 0 end
    if not lay.decided then
        lay.waited = lay.waited + 1
        if room_shared() and lay.waited <= R.LAY_SETTLE then return 0 end
        if mark_here() then
            lay.done = true
            return 0
        end
        if lower_in_room() and lay.waited < R.LAY_WAIT then return 0 end
        lay.decided = true
        local w = map.world or { 0, 0, 0 }
        call("spawn_sync_object", THING, G.E_MODEL_NONE, w[1] or 0, (w[2] or 0) - 2000, w[3] or 0,
            function(obj) obj.oBehParams = R.MARK end)
    end
    local stood = 0
    while lay.next <= #lay.rows and stood < R.LAY_PER_FRAME do
        if R.lay_row(map, lay.rows[lay.next]) ~= nil then stood = stood + 1 end
        lay.next = lay.next + 1
    end
    if lay.next > #lay.rows then lay.done = true end
    return stood
end
R.LIFT_DROP = 512
local settled = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
function R.tend(map)
    local n = 0
    for _, row in ipairs(map.things or {}) do
        if row.kind == "spawn" and (row.reach ~= nil or row.settle ~= nil) then
            local at = row.at
            each_of(bhv(row.bhv), function(o)
                if math.abs((tonumber(o.oHomeX) or 0) - at[1]) < 1
                    and math.abs((tonumber(o.oHomeZ) or 0) - at[3]) < 1 then
                    if row.reach ~= nil and (tonumber(o.oCollisionDistance) or 0) < row.reach then
                        o.oCollisionDistance = row.reach
                        n = n + 1
                    end
                    if row.settle ~= nil and not settled[o] and (tonumber(o.oTimer) or 0) >= 1 then
                        settled[o] = true
                        if row.settle == "log" then
                            o.oPitouneUnkF8, o.oPitouneUnkFC = at[1], at[3]
                        elseif row.settle == "lift" then
                            o.oElevatorUnkF8 = at[2]
                            o.oElevatorUnkF4 = at[2] - R.LIFT_DROP
                            o.oElevatorUnkFC = (o.oElevatorUnkF4 + o.oElevatorUnkF8) / 2
                        end
                        n = n + 1
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    return n
end
local function fixing(o)
    return R.leading() and not R.stepping_aside() and R.built ~= nil and o ~= nil
end
local SHARED_FIXES = (function()
    local spawner = {}
    local placed_by_a_mod = placed
    local CHILD_WALK_LIMIT = 512
    local function no_setup() end
    local function reap_children(kind, parent)
        if kind == nil or fn("obj_get_first_with_behavior_id") == nil then return 0 end
        local doomed, guard = {}, 0
        local object = G.obj_get_first_with_behavior_id(kind)
        while object ~= nil and guard < CHILD_WALK_LIMIT do
            local mine = object.parentObj
            if parent ~= nil then
                if mine == parent then doomed[#doomed + 1] = object end
            elseif mine ~= nil and mine ~= object and mine.activeFlags == 0 then
                doomed[#doomed + 1] = object
            end
            object = G.obj_get_next_with_same_behavior_id(object)
            guard = guard + 1
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(doomed) do call("obj_mark_for_deletion", child) end
        return #doomed
    end
    function spawner.level()
        local np = me_player()
        return np ~= nil and np.currLevelNum or nil
    end
    function spawner.spawn_non_sync(kind, model, x, y, z, setup)
        return call("spawn_non_sync_object", kind, model, x, y, z, setup)
    end
    function spawner.set_model(o, model) return call("obj_set_model_extended", o, model) end
    function spawner.dress_unplaced() return false end
    function spawner.held_here() return false end
    function spawner.play_sound(id) play(id) end
    spawner.optional = optional
    spawner.binding = fn
spawner.TRAMPOLINE = {
    HEIGHT = 150,
    SINK = 110,
    RISE = 45,
    PULL = 0.30,
    DAMP = 0.86,
    BASE = 30,
    STRENGTH = {
        [0] = { name = "gentle", low = 45, high = 65 },
        [1] = { name = "standard", low = 70, high = 100 },
        [2] = { name = "launch", low = 105, high = 145 },
    },
    KNOCK = 0.34,
    KNOCK_MIN = 9,
    POUND_FRAMES = 8,
    POUND_WEIGHT = 2.0,
    HOLD = 6,
}
do
    local T = spawner.TRAMPOLINE
    local before = {}
    local now = {}
    local fell = {}
    local held = {}
    local pounded = {}
    local seen = -1
    local function remember()
        local at = get_global_timer()
        if at == seen then return end
        seen = at
        for i = 0, MAX_PLAYERS - 1 do
            local m = gMarioStates[i]
            local o = m ~= nil and m.marioObj or nil
            before[i] = now[i]
            now[i] = o ~= nil and o.platform or nil
            if m ~= nil then
                local down = 0
                if m.vel ~= nil and type(m.vel.y) == "number" and m.vel.y < 0 then
                    down = -m.vel.y
                end
                if down > 0 then
                    fell[i] = down
                    held[i] = T.HOLD
                else
                    local left = (held[i] or 0) - 1
                    if left > 0 then
                        held[i] = left
                    else
                        held[i] = 0
                        fell[i] = 0
                    end
                end
                if m.action == ACT_GROUND_POUND
                    or m.action == ACT_GROUND_POUND_LAND then
                    pounded[i] = T.POUND_FRAMES
                else
                    local left = (pounded[i] or 0) - 1
                    pounded[i] = left > 0 and left or 0
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function strength_of(o)
        local byte = o ~= nil and o.oBehParams2ndByte or nil
        if type(byte) ~= "number" then return T.STRENGTH[1] end
        return T.STRENGTH[math.floor(byte)] or T.STRENGTH[1]
    end
    local function throw_for(o, speed)
        local band = strength_of(o)
        local want = T.BASE + speed
        if want < band.low then want = band.low end
        if want > band.high then want = band.high end
        return want
    end
    local function throw_local(owed)
        local m = gMarioStates[0]
        if m == nil or owed <= 0 then return false end
        if m.action == ACT_TWIRLING then return false end
        mario_stop_riding_and_holding(m)
        set_mario_action(m, ACT_TWIRLING, 0)
        if m.vel ~= nil then m.vel.y = owed end
        return true
    end
    local function trampoline_top(o)
        if o == nil then return false end
        remember()
        local home = o.oHomeY
        if type(home) ~= "number" then return false end
        local displace = o.oForwardVel or 0
        local speed = o.oVelY or 0
        local owed = o.oGravity or 0
        local knock = 0
        for i = 0, MAX_PLAYERS - 1 do
            if now[i] == o and before[i] ~= o then
                local came = fell[i] or 0
                if (pounded[i] or 0) > 0 then came = came * T.POUND_WEIGHT end
                local hit = T.KNOCK_MIN + came * T.KNOCK
                if hit > knock then knock = hit end
                if i == 0 then owed = throw_for(o, came) end
            end
        end
        speed = speed - knock
        speed = (speed - displace * T.PULL) * T.DAMP
        displace = displace + speed
        if displace < -T.SINK then
            displace = -T.SINK
            if speed < 0 then speed = 0 end
        elseif displace > T.RISE then
            displace = T.RISE
            if speed > 0 then speed = 0 end
        end
        if owed > 0 and displace < 0 and speed >= 0 then
            throw_local(owed)
            owed = 0
        end
        if owed <= 0 and displace < 0.5 and displace > -0.5
            and speed < 0.5 and speed > -0.5 then
            displace = 0
            speed = 0
        end
        o.oForwardVel = displace
        o.oVelY = speed
        o.oGravity = owed
        o.oPosY = home + displace
        return true
    end
    local function trampoline_spring(o)
        if o == nil then return false end
        local parent = o.parentObj
        if parent == nil or parent == o then return false end
        if parent.activeFlags == 0 then
            reap_children(id_bhvStaticObject, parent)
            obj_mark_for_deletion(o)
            return true
        end
        local home = o.oHomeY
        if type(home) ~= "number" or type(o.oPosY) ~= "number" then return false end
        local scale = 1.0 + (o.oPosY - home) / T.HEIGHT
        if scale < 0.05 then scale = 0.05 end
        local wide = 1.0
        local gfx = parent.header ~= nil and parent.header.gfx or nil
        if gfx ~= nil and gfx.scale ~= nil and type(gfx.scale.x) == "number" then
            wide = gfx.scale.x
        end
        obj_scale_xyz(o, wide, wide * scale, wide)
        return true
    end
    spawner.trampoline_top, spawner.trampoline_spring = trampoline_top, trampoline_spring
end
local function has_flag(value, flag)
    if value == nil or flag == nil or flag == 0 then return false end
    return math.floor(value / flag) % 2 == 1
end
spawner.CHILD_MODEL = {
    { bhv = id_bhvCapSwitchBase, list = OBJ_LIST_SURFACE, model = E_MODEL_CAP_SWITCH_BASE },
    { bhv = id_bhvFloorTrapInCastle, list = OBJ_LIST_SURFACE, model = E_MODEL_CASTLE_BOWSER_TRAP },
    { bhv = id_bhvSlidingSnowMound, list = OBJ_LIST_SURFACE, model = E_MODEL_SL_SNOW_TRIANGLE },
    { bhv = id_bhvWigglerBody, list = OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR, model = E_MODEL_WIGGLER_BODY },

      byParent = { [0] = E_MODEL_BITS_BLUE_PLATFORM, [1] = E_MODEL_BITDW_BLUE_PLATFORM } },
    { bhv = id_bhvTumblingBridgePlatform, list = OBJ_LIST_SURFACE, model = E_MODEL_BBH_TUMBLING_PLATFORM_PART,
      byParent = { [1] = E_MODEL_BBH_TUMBLING_PLATFORM_PART, [2] = E_MODEL_LLL_FALLING_PLATFORM,
                   [3] = E_MODEL_BITFS_TUMBLING_PLATFORM_PART } },
    { bhv = id_bhvCheckerboardPlatformSub, list = OBJ_LIST_SURFACE, model = E_MODEL_CHECKERBOARD_PLATFORM },

    { bhv = id_bhvDddMovingPole, list = OBJ_LIST_POLELIKE, model = E_MODEL_BITFS_BLUE_POLE },
    { bhv = id_bhvKoopaShell, list = OBJ_LIST_LEVEL, model = E_MODEL_KOOPA_SHELL },
    { bhv = id_bhvSnufitBalls, list = OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR, model = E_MODEL_BOWLING_BALL },
    { bhv = id_bhvBobomb, list = OBJ_LIST_DESTRUCTIVE, model = E_MODEL_BLACK_BOBOMB },
    { bhv = id_bhvSpiny, list = OBJ_LIST_PUSHABLE, model = E_MODEL_SPINY_BALL,
      keeps = { E_MODEL_SPINY } },
}
function spawner.child_model(row, o)
    if row == nil then return nil end
    if row.head ~= nil and o ~= nil and math.floor(tonumber(o.oBehParams2ndByte) or 0) == 0 then
        return row.head
    end
    if row.byParent ~= nil then
        local parent = o ~= nil and o.parentObj or nil
        local byte = parent ~= nil and math.floor(tonumber(parent.oBehParams2ndByte) or 0) or 0
        return row.byParent[byte]
    end
    return row.model
end
local CHILD_HOPS = 3
function spawner.of_a_placed_family(o)
    local at = o ~= nil and o.parentObj or nil
    for _ = 1, CHILD_HOPS do
        if at == nil then return false end
        if placed_by_a_mod(at) then return true end
        if at.parentObj == at then return false end
        at = at.parentObj
    end
    return false
end
local function wears(o, model)
    if type(obj_has_model_extended) ~= "function" then return false end
    if E_MODEL_ERROR_MODEL ~= nil and obj_has_model_extended(o, E_MODEL_ERROR_MODEL) ~= 0 then
        return false
    end
    return obj_has_model_extended(o, model) ~= 0
end
function spawner.dress_child(row, o)
    if o == nil or row == nil then return false end
    local model = spawner.child_model(row, o)
    if model == nil or wears(o, model) then return false end
    for _, other in ipairs(row.keeps or {}) do
        if wears(o, other) then return false end
    end
    if not spawner.of_a_placed_family(o) then
        if not spawner.dress_unplaced(o) then return false end
    end
    if type(obj_set_model_extended) ~= "function" then return false end
    spawner.set_model(o, model)
    return true
end
local ALIVE = (ACTIVE_FLAG_ACTIVE or 1) + (ACTIVE_FLAG_UNK8 or 0x100)
function spawner.wake_mips(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if o.activeFlags ~= 0 then return false end
    o.activeFlags = ALIVE
    o.oBehParams2ndByte = 0
    o.oMipsForwardVelocity = 40.0
    return true
end
local YOSHI_IDLE = YOSHI_ACT_IDLE or 0
local YOSHI_STAYS = {
    [YOSHI_ACT_WALK or 1] = true,
    [YOSHI_ACT_WALK_JUMP_OFF_ROOF or 3] = true,
    [YOSHI_ACT_FINISH_JUMPING_AND_DESPAWN or 4] = true,
    [YOSHI_ACT_GONE or 6] = true,
    [YOSHI_ACT_REAPPEAR or 7] = true,
}
local INVISIBLE = GRAPH_RENDER_INVISIBLE or 16
local function show(o)
    local node = o.header ~= nil and o.header.gfx ~= nil and o.header.gfx.node or nil
    if node ~= nil and has_flag(node.flags or 0, INVISIBLE) then
        node.flags = node.flags - INVISIBLE
    end
end
function spawner.keep_yoshi(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if not YOSHI_STAYS[o.oAction] then return false end
    o.oAction = YOSHI_IDLE
    o.oForwardVel = 0
    o.oVelY = 0
    o.oIntangibleTimer = 0
    show(o)
    return true
end
spawner.CANNON_SINK = 340
function spawner.lift_cannon(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    o.oPosY = o.oPosY + spawner.CANNON_SINK
    o.oHomeY = o.oHomeY + spawner.CANNON_SINK
    return true
end
function spawner.keep_bub(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    local mario = gMarioStates[0] ~= nil and gMarioStates[0].marioObj or nil
    if mario == nil then return false end
    o.parentObj = mario
    return true
end
local AMP_ASLEEP = HOMING_AMP_ACT_INACTIVE or 0
function spawner.show_amp(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if o.oAction ~= AMP_ASLEEP then return false end
    show(o)
    return true
end
spawner.CAP_HOLD_TIMER = 60
function spawner.keep_cap(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if (tonumber(o.oTimer) or 0) <= 250 then return false end
    o.oTimer = spawner.CAP_HOLD_TIMER
    return true
end
spawner.HOLD_CLOCK = {
    { bhv = id_bhvSingleCoinGetsSpawned, list = OBJ_LIST_LEVEL, past = 350, back = 300 },
    { bhv = id_bhvBlueCoinJumping, list = OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR, action = 3, past = 50, back = 0 },
    { bhv = id_bhv1upWalking, list = OBJ_LIST_LEVEL, action = 1, past = 250, back = 200 },
}
spawner.CLOCK_MARK = 0x4F000000
function spawner.hold_clock(row, o)
    if row == nil or o == nil or o.oBehParams ~= spawner.CLOCK_MARK then return false end
    if not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if row.action ~= nil and o.oAction ~= row.action then return false end
    if (tonumber(o.oTimer) or 0) <= row.past then return false end
    o.oTimer = row.back
    return true
end
spawner.BOX_FALL = 20
function spawner.settle_box(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if spawner.held_here(o) then return false end
    if type(find_floor_height) ~= "function" then return false end
    local floor = tonumber(find_floor_height(o.oPosX, o.oPosY + 10, o.oPosZ)) or -11000
    if floor <= -10000 or o.oPosY <= floor then return false end
    o.oPosY = math.max(floor, o.oPosY - spawner.BOX_FALL)
    o.oHomeY = o.oPosY
    return true
end
spawner.BOWSER_SKIP_INTRO = 2
local BOWSER_WAITING = { [5] = true, [6] = true, [20] = true }
function spawner.wake_bowser(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    o.oBowserUnk88 = spawner.BOWSER_SKIP_INTRO
    if BOWSER_WAITING[o.oAction] then o.oAction = 0 end
    return true
end
local BOWSER_ARENA_LEVELS = {}
for _, name in ipairs({ "LEVEL_BOWSER_1", "LEVEL_BOWSER_2", "LEVEL_BOWSER_3" }) do
    if type(_G[name]) == "number" then BOWSER_ARENA_LEVELS[_G[name]] = true end
end
spawner.BOWSER_JUMP_ONTO_STAGE = 2
spawner.BOWSER_LAND_RADIUS = 200
function spawner.home_bowser(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if BOWSER_WAITING[o.oAction] then return spawner.wake_bowser(o) end
    if BOWSER_ARENA_LEVELS[spawner.level() or -1] then return false end
    local hx, hz = tonumber(o.oHomeX) or 0, tonumber(o.oHomeZ) or 0
    local moved = false
    if o.oPosX == 0 and o.oPosZ == 0 and o.oPosY == (tonumber(o.oHomeY) or 0) + 2000 then
        o.oPosX, o.oPosZ = hx, hz
        moved = true
    end
    if o.oAction == spawner.BOWSER_JUMP_ONTO_STAGE and o.oSubAction == 2 then
        local dx, dz = hx - o.oPosX, hz - o.oPosZ
        o.oMoveAngleYaw = atan2s(dz, dx)
        local r = spawner.BOWSER_LAND_RADIUS
        if dx * dx + dz * dz < r * r then o.oForwardVel = 0 end
        moved = true
    end
    return moved
end
spawner.FINAL_BOWSER = 2
spawner.FINAL_BOWSER_HITS = 3
spawner.FINAL_BOWSER_UNLIT = 1
function spawner.is_final_bowser(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    local params = math.floor(tonumber(o.oBehParams) or 0)
    return ((params >> 16) & 0xFF) == spawner.FINAL_BOWSER
end
function spawner.final_bowser(o, init)
    if not spawner.is_final_bowser(o) then return false end
    o.oBehParams2ndByte = spawner.FINAL_BOWSER
    o.oBowserUnk1B2 = spawner.FINAL_BOWSER_UNLIT
    if init then o.oHealth = spawner.FINAL_BOWSER_HITS end
    return true
end
function spawner.start_bowser(o)
    local woke = spawner.wake_bowser(o)
    spawner.final_bowser(o, true)
    return woke
end
function spawner.keep_bowser(o)
    spawner.final_bowser(o, false)
    return spawner.home_bowser(o)
end
spawner.GRAND_STAR_FLYING = 1
function spawner.drop_grand_star(o)
    if o == nil or o.oAction ~= spawner.GRAND_STAR_FLYING or o.oSubAction ~= 0 then return false end
    if LEVEL_BOWSER_3 ~= nil and spawner.level() == LEVEL_BOWSER_3 then return false end
    local boss = o.parentObj
    if boss == nil or boss == o or obj_has_behavior_id(boss, id_bhvBowser) == 0
        or not placed_by_a_mod(boss) then return false end
    o.oForwardVel = 0
    return true
end
spawner.CHILL_FALL = 1000
spawner.BULLY_CHILL_BIT = 0x10
spawner.BULLY_LAVA_DEATH = 100
local BULLY_ON_ITS_FEET = { [0] = true, [1] = true, [2] = true, [3] = true }
local function chill_line()
    local values = gBehaviorValues
    local line = values ~= nil and tonumber(values.ChillBullyDeathPosY) or nil
    return line or 1030
end
function spawner.chill_keeps_the_game_line(o)
    if o == nil or LEVEL_SL == nil or spawner.level() ~= LEVEL_SL then return false end
    return (tonumber(o.oHomeY) or -20000) >= chill_line()
end
function spawner.unchill(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if spawner.chill_keeps_the_game_line(o) then return false end
    o.oBullySubtype = 0
    if o.oAction == spawner.BULLY_LAVA_DEATH and (tonumber(o.oPosY) or 0) < chill_line() then
        o.oAction = 0
    end
    return true
end
function spawner.chill_edge(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if o.oBullySubtype ~= 0 then return false end
    if not BULLY_ON_ITS_FEET[o.oAction] then return false end
    if (tonumber(o.oPosY) or 0) >= (tonumber(o.oHomeY) or 0) - spawner.CHILL_FALL then return false end
    o.oBullySubtype = spawner.BULLY_CHILL_BIT
    o.oAction = spawner.BULLY_LAVA_DEATH
    return true
end
spawner.SPINY_MARK = 0x4F460000
spawner.SPINY_ANCHOR_Y = -30000
local SPINY_ALIVE = (ACTIVE_FLAG_ACTIVE or 1) + (ACTIVE_FLAG_UNK8 or 0x100)
local spinyAnchor = nil
local spinyOpen = {}
function spawner.is_placed_spiny(o)
    return o ~= nil and o.oBehParams == spawner.SPINY_MARK and placed_by_a_mod(o)
end
function spawner.forget_spiny_anchor()
    spinyAnchor = nil
end
function spawner.spiny_anchor()
    if spinyAnchor ~= nil and spinyAnchor.activeFlags ~= 0 then return spinyAnchor end
    spinyAnchor = nil
    if id_bhvEnemyLakitu == nil then return nil end
    spinyAnchor = spawner.spawn_non_sync(id_bhvEnemyLakitu, E_MODEL_NONE,
        0, spawner.SPINY_ANCHOR_Y, 0, no_setup)
    return spinyAnchor
end
function spawner.open_spiny_window(m)
    if m == nil or m.playerIndex ~= 0 then return 0 end
    for k in pairs(spinyOpen) do spinyOpen[k] = nil end
    if id_bhvSpiny == nil or spawner.binding("obj_get_first_with_behavior_id") == nil then
        return 0
    end
    local opened, guard = 0, 0
    local spiny = obj_get_first_with_behavior_id(id_bhvSpiny)
    while spiny ~= nil and guard < CHILD_WALK_LIMIT do
        if spiny.activeFlags ~= 0 and spawner.is_placed_spiny(spiny) then
            local anchor = spawner.spiny_anchor()
            if anchor == nil then return opened end
            spiny.parentObj = anchor
            spinyOpen[spiny.oSyncID or 0] = true
            opened = opened + 1
        end
        spiny = obj_get_next_with_same_behavior_id(spiny)
        guard = guard + 1
    end
    return opened
end
function spawner.close_spiny_window(o)
    if not spawner.is_placed_spiny(o) then return false end
    o.parentObj = o
    if o.activeFlags == 0 and spinyOpen[o.oSyncID or 0] then
        o.activeFlags = SPINY_ALIVE
    end
    return true
end
spawner.BOMB_BURST_SCALE = 1
function spawner.burst_bowser_bomb(o)
    if o == nil or not placed_by_a_mod(o) then return false end
    if (tonumber(o.activeFlags) or 0) ~= 0 then return false end
    local hitMine = INT_STATUS_HIT_MINE or 0x00200000
    if has_flag(math.floor(tonumber(o.oInteractStatus) or 0), hitMine) then
        return false
    end
    local x, y, z = o.oPosX or 0, o.oPosY or 0, o.oPosZ or 0
    if id_bhvBowserBombExplosion ~= nil and E_MODEL_BOWSER_FLAMES ~= nil then
        spawner.spawn_non_sync(id_bhvBowserBombExplosion,
            E_MODEL_BOWSER_FLAMES, x, y, z, nil)
    end
    if SOUND_GENERAL_BOWSER_BOMB_EXPLOSION ~= nil then
        spawner.play_sound(SOUND_GENERAL_BOWSER_BOMB_EXPLOSION)
    end
    spawner.optional("set_camera_shake_from_point", nil, SHAKE_POS_LARGE, x, y, z)
    return true
end
    return spawner
end)()
R.FIX = SHARED_FIXES
local FIX = SHARED_FIXES
local function fixed(f)
    if f == nil then return nil end
    return function(o) if fixing(o) then return f(o) end return false end
end
R.FIX_HOOKS = {
    { "id_bhvWingCap", "OBJ_LIST_LEVEL", nil, FIX.keep_cap },
    { "id_bhvPushableMetalBox", "OBJ_LIST_SURFACE", nil, FIX.settle_box },
    { "id_bhvBowser", "OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR", FIX.start_bowser, FIX.keep_bowser },
    { "id_bhvGrandStar", "OBJ_LIST_LEVEL", nil, FIX.drop_grand_star },
    { "id_bhvSmallChillBully", "OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR", FIX.unchill, FIX.chill_edge },
    { "id_bhvBigChillBully", "OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR", FIX.unchill, FIX.chill_edge },
    { "id_bhvSpiny", "OBJ_LIST_PUSHABLE", nil, FIX.close_spiny_window },
    { "id_bhvBowserBomb", "OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR", nil, FIX.burst_bowser_bomb },
    { "id_bhvMips", "OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR", FIX.wake_mips, nil },
    { "id_bhvYoshi", "OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR", FIX.keep_yoshi, FIX.keep_yoshi },
    { "id_bhvCannon", "OBJ_LIST_LEVEL", FIX.lift_cannon, nil },
    { "id_bhvBub", "OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR", FIX.keep_bub, nil },
    { "id_bhvHomingAmp", "OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR", FIX.show_amp, FIX.show_amp },
    { "id_bhvBetaTrampolineTop", "OBJ_LIST_SURFACE", nil, FIX.trampoline_top },
    { "id_bhvBetaTrampolineSpring", "OBJ_LIST_DEFAULT", nil, FIX.trampoline_spring },
}
if type(G.hook_behavior) == "function" then
    for _, row in ipairs(FIX.CHILD_MODEL) do
        if row.bhv ~= nil and row.model ~= nil then
            G.hook_behavior(row.bhv, row.list, false, fixed(function(o) return FIX.dress_child(row, o) end), nil)
        end
    end
    for _, row in ipairs(FIX.HOLD_CLOCK) do
        if row.bhv ~= nil then
            G.hook_behavior(row.bhv, row.list, false, nil, fixed(function(o) return FIX.hold_clock(row, o) end))
        end
    end
    for _, row in ipairs(R.FIX_HOOKS) do
        if bhv(row[1]) ~= nil and G[row[2]] ~= nil then
            G.hook_behavior(bhv(row[1]), G[row[2]], false, fixed(row[3]), fixed(row[4]))
        end
    end
end
function R.open_spiny_window(m)
    if m == nil or m.playerIndex ~= 0 or not R.leading() or R.stepping_aside() or R.built == nil then
        return 0
    end
    return FIX.open_spiny_window(m)
end
local SHARED_SPAWNER = (function()
    local spawner = {}
local AREA_LOOP = AREA_TIMER_TYPE_LOOP or 1
local AREA_MAXIMUM = AREA_TIMER_TYPE_MAXIMUM or 2
function spawner.catch_up_area_timer(o)
    if o == nil or type(get_network_area_timer) ~= "function" then return false end
    local kind = math.floor(tonumber(o.areaTimerType) or 0)
    local period = math.floor(tonumber(o.areaTimerDuration) or 0)
    local now = math.floor(tonumber(get_network_area_timer()) or 0)
    local timer = math.floor(tonumber(o.areaTimer) or 0)
    local behind = now - timer
    if kind == AREA_MAXIMUM then
        if behind < period or timer >= 10 then return false end
        o.areaTimer = now
        return true
    end
    if period <= 0 or behind < period then return false end
    o.areaTimer = timer + math.floor(behind / period) * period
    return true
end
spawner.AREA_TIMED = {
    { id_bhvSslMovingPyramidWall, OBJ_LIST_SURFACE },
    { id_bhvExclamationBox, OBJ_LIST_SURFACE },
    { id_bhvSmallBomp, OBJ_LIST_SURFACE },
    { id_bhvLargeBomp, OBJ_LIST_SURFACE },
    { id_bhvWfSlidingPlatform, OBJ_LIST_SURFACE },
    { id_bhvWfRotatingWoodenPlatform, OBJ_LIST_SURFACE },
    { id_bhvCheckerboardPlatformSub, OBJ_LIST_SURFACE },
    { id_bhvDddMovingPole, OBJ_LIST_POLELIKE },
}
spawner.WIGGLER_UNINITIALIZED = WIGGLER_ACT_UNINITIALIZED or 0
spawner.WIGGLER_DIALOG_READ = WIGGLER_TEXT_STATUS_COMPLETED_DIALOG or 2
function spawner.mend_wiggler(o)
    if o == nil then return false end
    local mended = false
    if o.oWigglerSegments == nil and o.oAction ~= spawner.WIGGLER_UNINITIALIZED then
        o.oAction = spawner.WIGGLER_UNINITIALIZED
        mended = true
    end
    local np = gNetworkPlayers[0]
    if np ~= nil and np.currLevelNum ~= LEVEL_THI
        and (tonumber(o.oWigglerTextStatus) or 0) < spawner.WIGGLER_DIALOG_READ then
        o.oWigglerTextStatus = spawner.WIGGLER_DIALOG_READ
    end
    return mended
end
    return spawner
end)()
R.spawner = SHARED_SPAWNER
if type(G.hook_behavior) == "function" then
    for _, row in ipairs(SHARED_SPAWNER.AREA_TIMED or {}) do
        if row[1] ~= nil and row[2] ~= nil then
            G.hook_behavior(row[1], row[2], false, SHARED_SPAWNER.catch_up_area_timer, nil)
        end
    end
    if G.id_bhvWigglerHead ~= nil then
        G.hook_behavior(G.id_bhvWigglerHead, G.OBJ_LIST_GENACTOR, false, SHARED_SPAWNER.mend_wiggler, nil)
    end
end
R.PIPE_DELAY, R.PIPE_GIVE_UP = 20, 90
R.pipeArmed, R.pipeTravel = true, nil
function R.pipe_rows(map)
    local out = {}
    for _, t in ipairs(map ~= nil and map.things or {}) do
        if t.kind == "spawn" and type(t.pipe) == "table" then out[#out + 1] = t end
    end
    return out
end
function R.pipe_object(row)
    local found = nil
    each_of(G.id_bhvWarpPipe, function(o)
        if math.abs((tonumber(o.oHomeX) or 0) - row.at[1]) < 1
            and math.abs((tonumber(o.oHomeZ) or 0) - row.at[3]) < 1 then
            found = o
            return false
        end
    end)
    return found
end
function R.is_map_pipe(o)
    if R.built == nil or o == nil then return false end
    for _, row in ipairs(R.pipe_rows(R.built)) do
        if math.abs((tonumber(o.oHomeX) or 0) - row.at[1]) < 1
            and math.abs((tonumber(o.oHomeZ) or 0) - row.at[3]) < 1 then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end
local function fade(into)
    if into then
        call("play_transition", G.WARP_TRANSITION_FADE_INTO_STAR, 0x14, 0, 0, 0)
    else
        call("play_transition", G.WARP_TRANSITION_FADE_FROM_STAR, 0x14, 0, 0, 0)
    end
end
local function pipe_by_tag(map, tag)
    for _, row in ipairs(R.pipe_rows(map)) do
        if tonumber(row.pipe.tag) == tonumber(tag) then return row end
    end
    return nil
end
R.PIPE_NOWHERE = "This pipe leads nowhere anymore"
function R.destination_act(level, area)
    local inArea, inLevel = nil, nil
    for i = 0, (tonumber(G.MAX_PLAYERS) or 16) - 1 do
        local np = G.gNetworkPlayers ~= nil and G.gNetworkPlayers[i] or nil
        if np ~= nil and np.connected and np.currLevelSyncValid and np.currLevelNum == level then
            local act = math.floor(tonumber(np.currActNum) or 0)
            if act ~= 99 then
                if np.currAreaSyncValid and np.currAreaIndex == area then
                    if inArea == nil then inArea = act end
                elseif inLevel == nil then
                    inLevel = act
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if inArea ~= nil then return inArea end
    if inLevel ~= nil then return inLevel end
    for _, name in ipairs({ "LEVEL_CASTLE", "LEVEL_CASTLE_GROUNDS", "LEVEL_CASTLE_COURTYARD" }) do
        if G[name] ~= nil and G[name] == level then return 0 end
    end
    return 1
end
function R.pipe_enter(m, row)
    local dest = row.pipe
    local kind = tonumber(dest.kind) or 0
    R.pipeArmed = false
    if kind == 2 and pipe_by_tag(R.built, dest.pair) == nil then return false end
    if kind ~= 1 and kind ~= 2 then return false end
    play(G.SOUND_MENU_ENTER_PIPE)
    call("set_mario_action", m, G.ACT_DISAPPEARED, 0)
    fade(true)
    R.pipeTravel = { left = R.PIPE_DELAY, row = row, map = R.built }
    return true
end
function R.pipe_step(m)
    if m == nil or R.built == nil then return end
    local t = R.pipeTravel
    if t ~= nil then
        if m.action ~= G.ACT_DISAPPEARED then R.pipeTravel = nil return end
        t.left = t.left - 1
        if t.left > 0 then return end
        R.pipeTravel = nil
        local dest = t.row.pipe
        if tonumber(dest.kind) == 2 then
            local other = pipe_by_tag(t.map, dest.pair)
            if other ~= nil then
                local o = R.pipe_object(other)
                local at = o ~= nil and { o.oPosX, o.oPosY, o.oPosZ } or other.at
                m.pos.x, m.pos.y, m.pos.z = at[1], at[2], at[3]
                m.vel.x, m.vel.y, m.vel.z = 0, 0, 0
                m.forwardVel = 0
                m.faceAngle.y = to_angle(other.at[4])
            end
            call("set_mario_action", m, G.ACT_EMERGE_FROM_PIPE, 0)
            fade(false)
            return
        end
        fade(false)
        local level = tonumber(dest.level)
        local area = math.max(1, tonumber(dest.area) or 1)
        local function out_of_mouth()
            local o = R.pipe_object(t.row)
            local at = o ~= nil and { o.oPosX, o.oPosY, o.oPosZ } or t.row.at
            m.pos.x, m.pos.y, m.pos.z = at[1], at[2], at[3]
            m.vel.x, m.vel.y, m.vel.z = 0, 0, 0
            m.forwardVel = 0
            call("set_mario_action", m, G.ACT_EMERGE_FROM_PIPE, 0)
            R.note(R.PIPE_NOWHERE, "warn")
        end
        if level == nil or level <= 0 then
            out_of_mouth()
            return
        end
        for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
            if map.level == level then
                call("set_mario_action", m, G.ACT_IDLE, 0)
                if not R.go(map) then out_of_mouth() end
                return
            end
        end
        if call("warp_to_level", level, area, R.destination_act(level, area)) == false then
            out_of_mouth()
        end
        return
    end
    if m.action == G.ACT_EMERGE_FROM_PIPE or m.action == G.ACT_DISAPPEARED then return end
    local touching = nil
    for _, row in ipairs(R.pipe_rows(R.built)) do
        local o = R.pipe_object(row)
        if o ~= nil and m.marioObj ~= nil and call("obj_check_hitbox_overlap", m.marioObj, o) then
            touching = row
            break
        end
    end
    if touching == nil then
        R.pipeArmed = true
        return
    end
    if R.pipeArmed and (m.health or 0x880) > 0xFF then R.pipe_enter(m, touching) end
end
R.TRI_MODEL, R.TRI_COL = "of_triblock_geo", "of_triblock_col"
R.WALL_RUN = nil
if type(G.allocate_mario_action) == "function" then
    R.WALL_RUN = G.allocate_mario_action((G.ACT_GROUP_AIRBORNE or 0x080) | (G.ACT_FLAG_AIR or 0x800))
end
local SHARED_WALLRUN = (function()
    local spawner = { WALL_RUN = R.WALL_RUN, optional = optional }
    function spawner.in_a_round() return false end
    function spawner.play_sound(id) play(id) end
spawner.TRIBLOCK_WIDE = 200
spawner.TRIBLOCK_DEEP = 200
spawner.TRIBLOCK_TALL = 200
spawner.TRIBLOCK_RUN_SPEED = 29
spawner.TRIBLOCK_DOCK_REACH = 600
spawner.WALL_RUN_SPEED = 40
spawner.WALL_RUN_LEAST = 24
spawner.WALL_RUN_GAP = 50
spawner.WALL_RUN_MOST = 240
spawner.WALL_RUN_HOP = 52
do
    local PITCH_SLOPE, PITCH_WALL = -0x2000, -0x4000
    local function has_flag(value, flag)
        if value == nil or flag == nil or flag == 0 then return false end
        return math.floor(value / flag) % 2 == 1
    end
    local function facing(yaw)
        local turn = (tonumber(yaw) or 0) * 2 * math.pi / 0x10000
        return math.sin(turn), math.cos(turn)
    end
    local function yaw_of(x, z)
        return math.floor(math.atan(x, z) * 0x8000 / math.pi) % 0x10000
    end
    local function ray(sx, sy, sz, dx, dy, dz)
        if collision_find_surface_on_ray == nil then return nil end
        local info = collision_find_surface_on_ray(sx, sy, sz, dx, dy, dz)
        if info == nil or info.surface == nil or info.surface.normal == nil then return nil end
        return info.hitPos.x, info.hitPos.y, info.hitPos.z, info.surface.normal
    end
    function spawner.dock_triblock(o)
        if o == nil then return false end
        local bx, bz = facing(o.oFaceAngleYaw)
        local tries = { { -bx, -bz } }
        for k = 0, 7 do
            local a = k * math.pi / 4
            tries[#tries + 1] = { math.sin(a), math.cos(a) }
        end
