    { "SEQ_LEVEL_BOSS_KOOPA", "Bowser Battle" }, { "SEQ_LEVEL_BOSS_KOOPA_FINAL", "Final Bowser" },
    { "SEQ_EVENT_BOSS", "Boss Fight" }, { "SEQ_EVENT_POWERUP", "Powerful Mario" },
    { "SEQ_EVENT_METAL_CAP", "Metal Cap Theme" }, { "SEQ_MENU_TITLE_SCREEN", "Title Theme" },
    { "SEQ_MENU_FILE_SELECT", "File Select" }, { "SEQ_MENU_STAR_SELECT", "Star Select" },
    { "SEQ_EVENT_CUTSCENE_INTRO", "Peach's Letter" }, { "SEQ_EVENT_CUTSCENE_VICTORY", "Victory" },
    { "SEQ_EVENT_CUTSCENE_ENDING", "Ending" }, { "SEQ_EVENT_CUTSCENE_CREDITS", "Credits" },
}
function R.music_list()
    local out = {}
    for _, t in ipairs(R.TRACKS) do
        local seq = G[t[1]]
        if type(seq) == "number" then out[#out + 1] = { key = "v:" .. t[1], name = t[2], seq = seq } end
    end
    local seen = {}
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        local look = map.look or {}
        if type(look.musicFile) == "string" and not seen[look.musicFile] then
            seen[look.musicFile] = true
            local name = (map.name or map.key) .. " (" .. look.musicFile .. ")"
            if type(look.musicSeq) == "number" then
                out[#out + 1] = { key = "m:" .. look.musicFile, name = name, seq = look.musicSeq, map = true }
            elseif look.stream ~= nil then
                out[#out + 1] = { key = "m:" .. look.musicFile, name = name, stream = look.stream, map = true,
                                  volume = look.musicVolume }
            end
        end
        for _, t in ipairs(type(look.tracks) == "table" and look.tracks or {}) do
            if type(t) == "table" and type(t.file) == "string" and not seen[t.file] then
                seen[t.file] = true
                local tname = type(t.name) == "string" and t.name or t.file
                if type(t.seq) == "number" then
                    out[#out + 1] = { key = "m:" .. t.file, name = tname, seq = t.seq, map = true }
                elseif t.handle ~= nil then
                    out[#out + 1] = { key = "m:" .. t.file, name = tname, stream = t.handle, map = true,
                                      volume = t.volume }
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end
function R.music_entry(key)
    if key == nil or key == "" or key == 0 then return nil end
    if key == R.SILENCE then return { key = R.SILENCE, name = "silence" } end
    for _, e in ipairs(R.music_list()) do if e.key == key then return e end end
    return nil
end
function R.music_find(word)
    word = string.lower(tostring(word or ""))
    if word == "" then return nil end
    if word == "silence" or word == "quiet" then return R.music_entry(R.SILENCE) end
    local list = R.music_list()
    local n = tonumber(word)
    if n ~= nil then return list[math.floor(n)] end
    for pass = 1, 3 do
        for _, e in ipairs(list) do
            local name = string.lower(e.name)
            if (pass == 1 and name == word) or (pass == 2 and string.sub(name, 1, #word) == word)
                or (pass == 3 and string.find(name, word, 1, true) ~= nil) then
                return e
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local wanted, playing, orphan = nil, nil, nil
local silent, volumeTouched = false, false
local mineKey = nil
local streamOn, streamHeld = nil, false
local sinceCheck = 0
R.stall = { acc = nil, still = 0 }
R.jingle = { acc = nil, still = 0, on = false }
local EMPTY_QUEUE = 65535
local function queue_head()
    local head = callnum("get_current_background_music")
    if head == nil or head == EMPTY_QUEUE or head < 0 then return nil end
    return head
end
local function level_track()
    local m = me_mario()
    local args = m ~= nil and m.area ~= nil and m.area.musicParam2 or nil
    if args == nil or args == 0 then return nil end
    return args
end
R.level_track = level_track
local function same_seq(a, b)
    if a == nil or b == nil then return false end
    return a % 256 == b % 256
end
local function ours_at_head()
    return same_seq(queue_head(), wanted)
end
local function sweep_orphan()
    if orphan == nil then return false end
    if same_seq(wanted, orphan) or same_seq(level_track(), orphan) then
        orphan = nil
        return false
    end
    local head = queue_head()
    if not same_seq(head, orphan) then return false end
    call("stop_background_music", head)
    orphan = nil
    return true
end
function R.music_volume()
    local v = SYNC ~= nil and tonumber(SYNC[R.VOLUME_KEY]) or nil
    if v == nil then return 100 end
    return math.max(0, math.min(100, math.floor(v)))
end
local function hold_quiet()
    call("seq_player_fade_out", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, R.QUIET_FADE)
end
R.STREAM_BOOST = SHARED_MUSIC.STREAM_BOOST
R.STREAM_PAUSED = SHARED_MUSIC.PAUSE_SCALE
R.NO_LOAD = SHARED_MUSIC.NO_LOAD
function R.stream_gain(e, paused)
    if e == nil then return 0 end
    return SHARED_MUSIC.stream_gain(e.volume, R.music_volume(), paused, R.jingle.on)
end
local function apply_volume()
    if streamOn ~= nil then
        call("fade_volume_scale", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0,
            streamOn.parked and math.floor(127 * R.music_volume() / 100) or 0, 1)
        if streamOn.stream ~= nil then
            local target = (silent or streamOn.parked) and 0
                or R.stream_gain(streamOn, call("is_game_paused") == true)
            local g = SHARED_MUSIC.gain_toward(streamOn.gain or target, target, streamOn.gain == nil,
                R.QUIET_FADE)
            if g ~= streamOn.gain then
                streamOn.gain = g
                call("audio_stream_set_volume", streamOn.stream, g)
            end
        end
        return
    end
    local percent = R.music_volume()
    if percent >= 100 and not volumeTouched then return end
    volumeTouched = true
    call("fade_volume_scale", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, math.floor(127 * percent / 100), R.QUIET_FADE)
end
local function stop_stream()
    if streamOn == nil then return false end
    if streamOn.stream ~= nil then call("audio_stream_stop", streamOn.stream) end
    streamOn.parked, streamOn.gain = false, nil
    streamOn = nil
    call("fade_volume_scale", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, math.floor(127 * R.music_volume() / 100), 1)
    if R.starved then
        R.starved = false
        local head = queue_head()
        if head ~= nil then call("play_music", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, head, R.MUSIC_FADE_IN) end
    end
    return true
end
local function pop_ours()
    if playing == nil then return end
    call("stop_background_music", playing)
    playing = nil
    if queue_head() == nil then
        local args = level_track()
        if args ~= nil then call("play_music", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, args, R.MUSIC_FADE_IN) end
    end
    apply_volume()
    if silent then hold_quiet() end
end
local function selecting()
    if R.select ~= nil then return true end
    local sel = G.id_bhvActSelector
    return sel ~= nil and fn("obj_get_first_with_behavior_id") ~= nil
        and G.obj_get_first_with_behavior_id(sel) ~= nil
end
local function push()
    if wanted == nil or selecting() then return end
    if not ours_at_head() then call("play_music", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, wanted, R.MUSIC_FADE_IN) end
    if ours_at_head() then playing = wanted end
    apply_volume()
    if silent then hold_quiet() end
end
function R.music_in_force()
    if R.stepping_aside() then return nil end
    if mineKey ~= nil then return mineKey end
    local room = SYNC ~= nil and SYNC[R.MUSIC_KEY] or nil
    if type(room) == "string" and room ~= "" then return room end
    local map = R.worn
    local look = map ~= nil and map.look or nil
    if look ~= nil and look.stream ~= nil and type(look.musicFile) == "string" then
        return "m:" .. look.musicFile
    end
    return nil
end
local function start(key)
    local e = R.music_entry(key)
    if e ~= nil and e.seq ~= nil and not silent and wanted == R.MUSIC_PRIORITY * 256 + e.seq
        and playing ~= nil and ours_at_head() then
        return
    end
    if e ~= nil and e.stream ~= nil and streamOn ~= nil and streamOn.key == e.key then
        if streamOn.parked and not selecting() then
            streamOn.parked, streamOn.gain = false, nil
            call("audio_stream_play", e.stream, true, R.stream_gain(e))
        end
        apply_volume()
        return
    end
    stop_stream()
    if playing == nil and wanted ~= nil then orphan = wanted % 256 end
    pop_ours()
    local wasSilent = silent
    silent = false
    if e == nil then
        wanted = nil
        if wasSilent then
            local args = level_track()
            if args ~= nil then call("play_music", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, args, R.MUSIC_FADE_IN) end
            apply_volume()
        end
        return
    end
    if e.key == R.SILENCE then
        wanted = nil
        silent = true
        hold_quiet()
        return
    end
    if e.stream ~= nil then
        wanted = nil
        streamOn = e
        e.gain = nil
        call("audio_stream_set_looping", e.stream, true)
        if selecting() then
            e.parked = true
        else
            e.parked = false
            call("audio_stream_play", e.stream, true, R.stream_gain(e))
        end
        apply_volume()
        return
    end
    wanted = R.MUSIC_PRIORITY * 256 + e.seq
    if wasSilent then call("play_music", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, wanted, R.MUSIC_FADE_IN) end
    push()
end
R.music_start = start
function R.music_refresh()
    if R.stepping_aside() then return end
    start(R.music_in_force())
end
function R.music_arrived()
    if R.stepping_aside() then return end
    local count = tonumber(G.SEQ_COUNT) or 0x23
    if playing ~= nil and playing % 256 > count then
        call("stop_background_music", playing)
    end
    playing, wanted, orphan = nil, nil, nil
    if streamOn ~= nil then stop_stream() end
    R.music_refresh()
end
local function level_player_stalled()
    return SHARED_MUSIC.level_stalled(R.stall)
end
function R.music_watch()
    if R.stepping_aside() then return false end
    if streamOn ~= nil and streamOn.stream ~= nil then
        SHARED_MUSIC.env_step(R.jingle)
        if selecting() and not streamOn.parked then
            streamOn.parked = true
            call("audio_stream_pause", streamOn.stream)
        elseif not selecting() and streamOn.parked then
            start(streamOn.key)
        end
        apply_volume()
    end
    if wanted ~= nil and not silent and ours_at_head() and level_player_stalled() then
        call("play_music", G.SEQ_PLAYER_LEVEL or 0, wanted, R.MUSIC_FADE_IN)
        apply_volume()
        R.stall.acc, R.stall.still = nil, 0
    end
    sinceCheck = sinceCheck + 1
    if sinceCheck < R.MUSIC_CHECK then return false end
    sinceCheck = 0
    local force = R.music_in_force()
    if streamOn == nil and type(force) == "string" and force:sub(1, 2) == "m:" and not selecting() then
        R.music_refresh()
        return true
    end
    if sweep_orphan() then return true end
    apply_volume()
    if silent then
        hold_quiet()
        return true
    end
    if wanted == nil then return false end
    if ours_at_head() then
        playing = wanted
        return false
    end
    push()
    return true
end
function R.music_back()
    playing = nil
    R.music_refresh()
end
function R.music_heard()
    if R.leading() then R.music_refresh() end
end
function R.music_mine()
    return mineKey
end
function R.music_selecting()
    return selecting()
end
function R.music_stream_in_force()
    if streamOn ~= nil then return streamOn.stream ~= nil end
    local e = R.music_entry(R.music_in_force())
    return e ~= nil and e.stream ~= nil
end
function R.music_state()
    return { wanted = wanted, playing = playing, orphan = orphan, silent = silent,
             stream = streamOn ~= nil and streamOn.key or nil, mine = mineKey }
end
local function music_lines()
    local lines = { YELLOW .. "Music you can put on:" }
    for i, e in ipairs(R.music_list()) do
        lines[#lines + 1] = WHITE .. "  " .. i .. "  " .. (e.map and GREEN or WHITE) .. e.name
    end
    local room = R.music_entry(SYNC ~= nil and SYNC[R.MUSIC_KEY] or nil)
    lines[#lines + 1] = GREY .. "Now: " .. (room ~= nil and room.name or "each level's own music") ..
        (mineKey ~= nil and ("; for you: " .. (R.music_entry(mineKey) or { name = "?" }).name) or "")
    lines[#lines + 1] = WHITE .. "Type " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap music 3" .. WHITE .. " (host: for everybody), " ..
        YELLOW .. "/ofmap music off" .. WHITE .. ", or " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap music me 3" .. WHITE ..
        " just for you."
    return lines
end
function R.music_command(rest)
    rest = tostring(rest or "")
    local words = {}
    for w in string.gmatch(rest, "%S+") do words[#words + 1] = w end
    local first = string.lower(words[1] or "")
    if R.stepping_aside() then
        say(GREY .. "Oldfart's Mod Menu plays the music here - use its own Music page.")
        return true
    end
    if first == "" or first == "list" then
        for _, line in ipairs(music_lines()) do say(line) end
        return true
    end
    if first == "me" then
        local word = table.concat(words, " ", 2)
        local lw = string.lower(word)
        if lw == "off" or lw == "" or lw == "room" then
            mineKey = nil
            R.music_refresh()
            say(YELLOW .. "Your music follows the room again.")
            return true
        end
        local e = R.music_find(word)
        if e == nil then
            say(RED .. "No track called \"" .. word .. "\". " .. WHITE .. "Type " .. YELLOW ..
                "/ofmap music" .. WHITE .. " to see the list.")
            return true
        end
        mineKey = e.key
        R.music_refresh()
        say(YELLOW .. "For you only: " .. e.name)
        return true
    end
    if not is_host() then
        say(RED .. "Only the host picks the music for everybody. Type " .. YELLOW ..
            "/ofmap music me " .. rest .. RED .. " to hear it yourself.")
        return true
    end
    if first == "volume" then
        local v = tonumber(words[2])
        if v == nil then
            say(YELLOW .. "Music volume: " .. R.music_volume() .. "%")
            return true
        end
        SYNC[R.VOLUME_KEY] = math.max(0, math.min(100, math.floor(v)))
        apply_volume()
        say(YELLOW .. "Music volume " .. R.music_volume() .. "% for everybody.")
        return true
    end
    if first == "off" or first == "level" then
        SYNC[R.MUSIC_KEY] = ""
        R.music_refresh()
        say(YELLOW .. "Back to each level's own music.")
        return true
    end
    local e = R.music_find(rest)
    if e == nil then
        say(RED .. "No track called \"" .. rest .. "\". " .. WHITE .. "Type " .. YELLOW .. "/ofmap music" ..
            WHITE .. " to see the list.")
        return true
    end
    SYNC[R.MUSIC_KEY] = e.key
    R.music_refresh()
    say(YELLOW .. "Music for everybody: " .. e.name)
    send({ ofmaps = "say", text = "Music: " .. e.name })
    return true
end
R.lastLevel = nil
local SHARED_LIGHTS = (function()
    local venues = {}
venues.LIGHTS_NEAR = 16
venues.LIGHTS_PICK_EVERY = 8
venues.LIGHTS_MARGIN = 2500
venues.LIGHTS_HZ = 30
local function light_hash(i, seed)
    local h = (i * 1103515245 + seed * 12345 + 7) & 0x7fffffff
    h = (h ~ (h >> 13)) * 1274126177 & 0x7fffffff
    return (h % 20001) / 10000.0 - 1.0
end
function venues.light_noise(u, seed)
    local i = math.floor(u)
    local f = u - i
    local a, b = light_hash(i, seed), light_hash(i + 1, seed)
    local w = (1.0 - math.cos(f * math.pi)) * 0.5
    return a + (b - a) * w
end
function venues.light_flicker(L, t)
    local a1, f1, a2, f2 = tonumber(L[9]) or 0, tonumber(L[10]) or 0, tonumber(L[11]) or 0,
        tonumber(L[12]) or 0
    if a1 <= 0 and a2 <= 0 then return 1.0 end
    local seed = (math.floor(L[1]) * 3 + math.floor(L[2]) * 5 + math.floor(L[3]) * 7) & 0xffff
    local s = t / venues.LIGHTS_HZ
    local k = 1.0 + a1 * venues.light_noise(s * f1, seed) + a2 * venues.light_noise(s * f2, seed + 101)
    if k < 0.2 then k = 0.2 elseif k > 1.6 then k = 1.6 end
    return k
end
function venues.light_pick(lights, x, y, z, most)
    local near = {}
    for i, L in ipairs(lights or {}) do
        local dx, dy, dz = L[1] - x, L[2] - y, L[3] - z
        local d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz
        local reach = (L[7] or 0) + venues.LIGHTS_MARGIN
        if d2 <= reach * reach then near[#near + 1] = { i, d2 } end
    end
    table.sort(near, function(a, b)
        if a[2] ~= b[2] then return a[2] < b[2] end
        return a[1] < b[1]
    end)
    local out = {}
    for k = 1, math.min(most or venues.LIGHTS_NEAR, #near) do out[k] = near[k][1] end
    return out
end
venues.LIGHTS_FADE_IN = 9
local function smooth01(v)
    v = math.max(0, math.min(1, v))
    return v * v * (3 - 2 * v)
end
function venues.lights_apply(st, lights, x, y, z, t, on, le)
    st.ids = st.ids or {}
    st.on = st.on or {}
    st.level = st.level or {}
    local want = {}
    local most = st.most or venues.LIGHTS_NEAR
    if on and lights ~= nil and #lights > 0 then
        if st.chosen == nil or st.pickedAt == nil or t - st.pickedAt >= venues.LIGHTS_PICK_EVERY
            or t < st.pickedAt or st.pickedMost ~= most then
            if st.picker ~= nil then
                st.chosen = st.picker(lights, most, st.chosen)
            else
                st.chosen = venues.light_pick(lights, x, y, z, most)
            end
            st.pickedAt, st.pickedMost = t, most
        end
        for _, i in ipairs(st.chosen) do want[i] = true end
    else
        st.chosen = nil
    end
    local step = 1 / venues.LIGHTS_FADE_IN
    local parks = {}
    for i in pairs(st.on) do
        if not want[i] then parks[#parks + 1] = i end
    end
    table.sort(parks)
    local fading = 0
    for _, i in ipairs(parks) do
        local level = st.fade and lights ~= nil and lights[i] ~= nil and (st.level[i] or 0) - step or 0
        if level > 0 then
            st.level[i] = level
            le.intensity(st.ids[i], lights[i][8] * smooth01(level))
            if st.shown ~= nil then st.shown[i] = lights[i][8] * smooth01(level) end
            fading = fading + 1
        else
            le.intensity(st.ids[i], 0)
            st.on[i], st.level[i] = nil, nil
            if st.shown ~= nil then st.shown[i] = nil end
        end
    end
    local active = 0
    for _, i in ipairs(st.chosen or {}) do
        local L = lights[i]
        local k = venues.light_flicker(L, t)
        local r, g, b = math.min(255, math.floor(L[4] * k + 0.5)),
            math.min(255, math.floor(L[5] * k + 0.5)), math.min(255, math.floor(L[6] * k + 0.5))
        local level = 1
        if st.fade then
            level = math.min(1, (st.level[i] or 0) + step)
            st.level[i] = level
        end
        local v = L[8] * smooth01(level)
        if st.ids[i] == nil then
            local id = le.add(L[1], L[2], L[3], r, g, b, L[7], v)
            if type(id) == "number" and id >= 0 then
                st.ids[i], st.on[i] = id, true
                if L[13] ~= nil and le.normals ~= nil then le.normals(id, L[13] == 1) end
            end
        else
            if not st.on[i] or (st.fade and (st.shown == nil or st.shown[i] ~= v)) then
                le.intensity(st.ids[i], v)
                st.on[i] = true
            end
            le.colour(st.ids[i], r, g, b)
        end
        if st.fade then
            st.shown = st.shown or {}
            st.shown[i] = v
        end
        if st.on[i] then active = active + 1 end
    end
    return active
end
venues.NIGHT_AMBIENT = { 96, 100, 120 }
venues.NIGHT_FLOOR = 90
venues.ACTOR_LIGHT = { 235, 240, 255 }
venues.LIGHTS_ACTIVE = 8
venues.LIGHTS_EYE = 4
venues.LIGHTS_PER_MARIO = 2
venues.LIGHTS_MARIO_REACH = 6000
venues.LIGHTS_HOLD = 0.75
venues.LE_MODE_LEVEL = 0
venues.LE_MODE_ACTORS = 1
venues.LE_MOST_WORN = 8
venues.LE_MOST_PLAIN = 4
function venues.lightfog_v2(layout)
    if type(layout) == "table" then
        local v = tonumber(layout.lightfog or layout.LIGHTFOG_CONTRACT or layout.lightfogContract)
        if v ~= nil then return v >= 2 end
    end
    return venues.cullMarked == true
end
function venues.light_pick_v2(lights, eye, marios, most, prev)
    most = math.min(most or venues.LIGHTS_ACTIVE, venues.LIGHTS_ACTIVE)
    local burning = {}
    for _, i in ipairs(prev or {}) do burning[i] = true end
    local function ranked(p, reach)
        local near = {}
        for i, L in ipairs(lights or {}) do
            local dx, dy, dz = L[1] - p.x, L[2] - p.y, L[3] - p.z
            local d = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
            if reach == nil or d <= reach then
                if burning[i] then d = d * venues.LIGHTS_HOLD end
                near[#near + 1] = { i, d }
            end
        end
        table.sort(near, function(a, b)
            if a[2] ~= b[2] then return a[2] < b[2] end
            return a[1] < b[1]
        end)
        return near
    end
    local out, taken = {}, {}
    local function take(list, n)
        local got = 0
        for _, e in ipairs(list) do
            if got >= n or #out >= most then return end
            if not taken[e[1]] then
                taken[e[1]] = true
                out[#out + 1] = e[1]
                got = got + 1
            end
        end
    end
    if eye ~= nil then take(ranked(eye, nil), venues.LIGHTS_EYE) end
    for _, p in ipairs(marios or {}) do
        take(ranked(p, venues.LIGHTS_MARIO_REACH), venues.LIGHTS_PER_MARIO)
    end
    if eye ~= nil then take(ranked(eye, nil), most) end
    return out
end
function venues.light_viewers()
    local lk = rawget(_G, "gLakituState")
    local eye = lk ~= nil and (lk.pos or lk.focus) or nil
    local marios = {}
    local ms, nps = rawget(_G, "gMarioStates"), rawget(_G, "gNetworkPlayers")
    if ms ~= nil then
        for i = 0, 15 do
            local m, np = ms[i], nps ~= nil and nps[i] or nil
            if m ~= nil and m.pos ~= nil and (i == 0 or (np ~= nil and np.connected)) then
                marios[#marios + 1] = { x = m.pos.x, y = m.pos.y, z = m.pos.z }
            end
        end
    end
    if eye == nil and marios[1] ~= nil then eye = marios[1] end
    return eye, marios
end
function venues.le_wear(S, on, le)
    if (S.leWorn == true) == (on == true) then return false end
    S.leWorn = on == true
    if le.mode ~= nil then le.mode(on and venues.LE_MODE_ACTORS or venues.LE_MODE_LEVEL) end
    if le.most ~= nil then le.most(on and venues.LE_MOST_WORN or venues.LE_MOST_PLAIN) end
    return true
end
function venues.lights_release(st, le, gone)
    if st == nil or st.ids == nil then return 0 end
    local ids = {}
    for i in pairs(st.ids) do ids[#ids + 1] = i end
    table.sort(ids)
    if not gone then
        for _, i in ipairs(ids) do le.remove(st.ids[i]) end
    end
    st.ids, st.on, st.chosen, st.pickedAt, st.pickedMost = {}, {}, nil, nil, nil
    st.level, st.shown = {}, nil
    return #ids
end
function venues.lights_engine()
    local function call(name, ...)
        local f = rawget(_G, name)
        if type(f) ~= "function" then return nil end
        return f(...)
    end
    return {
        add = function(x, y, z, r, g, b, radius, v) return call("le_add_light", x, y, z, r, g, b, radius, v) end,
        remove = function(id) return call("le_remove_light", id) end,
        intensity = function(id, v) return call("le_set_light_intensity", id, v) end,
        colour = function(id, r, g, b) return call("le_set_light_color", id, r, g, b) end,
        enabled = function() return call("le_is_enabled") == true end,
        normals = function(id, on) return call("le_set_light_use_surface_normals", id, on == true) end,
        mode = function(m) return call("le_set_mode", m) end,
        most = function(n) return call("le_set_max_lights_per_vertex", n) end,
    }
end
function venues.room_kind(rooms, room)
    if type(rooms) ~= "table" or type(room) ~= "number" or room < 1 then return "open" end
    return rooms[room] or "open"
end
function venues.lights_dark(layout, room)
    if layout == nil then return false end
    if layout.night == true then return true end
    local kind = venues.room_kind(layout.rooms, room)
    return kind == "cave" or kind == "inside"
end
function venues.room_watch(st, rooms)
    if type(rooms) ~= "table" or #rooms < 2 then
        venues.room_release(st)
        return nil
    end
    local lk = rawget(_G, "gLakituState")
    local ms = rawget(_G, "gMarioStates")
    local p = lk ~= nil and lk.pos or nil
    if p == nil and lk ~= nil then p = lk.focus end
    if p == nil and ms ~= nil and ms[0] ~= nil then p = ms[0].pos end
    local find = rawget(_G, "collision_find_floor")
    local set = rawget(_G, "set_room_override")
    if p == nil or find == nil or set == nil then return st.room end
    local s = find(p.x, p.y + 100, p.z)
    local room = s ~= nil and tonumber(s.room) or nil
    if room ~= nil and room >= 1 and room <= #rooms then
        if st.room ~= room or not st.set then set(room) end
        st.room, st.set = room, true
    end
    return st.room
end
function venues.room_release(st)
    if st ~= nil and st.set then
        local set = rawget(_G, "set_room_override")
        if set ~= nil then set(-1) end
    end
    if st ~= nil then st.room, st.set = nil, false end
end
venues.FLAMES_MOST = 48
venues.FLAME_SCALE = 7
function venues.flames_keep(st, key, rows, spawn)
    if st.key ~= key then
        st.key, st.list = key, {}
        for i, r in ipairs(type(rows) == "table" and rows or {}) do
            if i > venues.FLAMES_MOST then break end
            local o = spawn(r[4] == 1, r[1], r[2], r[3])
            if o ~= nil then
                st.list[#st.list + 1] = { o = o, scale = tonumber(r[5]) or venues.FLAME_SCALE,
                                          sized = (tonumber(r[5]) or venues.FLAME_SCALE) == venues.FLAME_SCALE }
            end
        end
    end
    local scale = rawget(_G, "obj_scale")
    for _, f in ipairs(st.list or {}) do
        if not f.sized and f.o ~= nil and (tonumber(f.o.oTimer) or 0) >= 1 and scale ~= nil then
            scale(f.o, f.scale)
            f.sized = true
        end
    end
    return #(st.list or {})
end
function venues.flames_release(st, gone)
    local del = rawget(_G, "obj_mark_for_deletion")
    if not gone and del ~= nil then
        for _, f in ipairs(st.list or {}) do
            if f.o ~= nil then del(f.o) end
        end
    end
    st.key, st.list = nil, {}
end
function venues.flame_spawn(blue, x, y, z)
    local spawn = rawget(_G, "spawn_non_sync_object")
    local bhv = rawget(_G, "id_bhvFlame")
    local model = blue and rawget(_G, "E_MODEL_BLUE_FLAME") or rawget(_G, "E_MODEL_RED_FLAME")
    if spawn == nil or bhv == nil or model == nil then return nil end
    return spawn(bhv, model, x, y, z, nil)
end
venues.DARK_AMBIENT = 208
venues.POCKET_FADE = 14
function venues.pocket_warp(shift, d)
    if type(shift) ~= "table" or #shift ~= 3 or d == nil then return false end
    local dx, dy, dz = tonumber(d.x) or 0, tonumber(d.y) or 0, tonumber(d.z) or 0
    for _, sign in ipairs({ 1, -1 }) do
        if math.abs(dx - sign * shift[1]) < 1 and math.abs(dy - sign * shift[2]) < 1
            and math.abs(dz - sign * shift[3]) < 1 then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end
function venues.pocket_door(shift, d)
    if not venues.pocket_warp(shift, d) then return false end
    local fade = rawget(_G, "play_transition")
    if fade ~= nil then
        fade(rawget(_G, "WARP_TRANSITION_FADE_FROM_COLOR") or 1, venues.POCKET_FADE, 0, 0, 0)
    end
    return true
end
venues.LIGHTS_RUNGS = { 16, 8, 4 }
venues.LIGHTS_PACE_WINDOW = 60
venues.LIGHTS_SLOW_MS = 40
venues.LIGHTS_FAST_MS = 36
venues.LIGHTS_CALM = 3
venues.LIGHTS_CALM_MOST = 48
venues.LIGHTS_HITCH_MS = 250
function venues.lights_pace(P, ms)
    P.rung = P.rung or 1
    P.n, P.sum, P.calm = P.n or 0, P.sum or 0, P.calm or 0
    P.need = P.need or venues.LIGHTS_CALM
    if type(ms) == "number" and ms > 0 and ms < venues.LIGHTS_HITCH_MS then
        P.n, P.sum = P.n + 1, P.sum + ms
        if P.n >= venues.LIGHTS_PACE_WINDOW then
            local mean = P.sum / P.n
            P.n, P.sum = 0, 0
            if mean > venues.LIGHTS_SLOW_MS then
                if P.climbed then P.need = math.min(venues.LIGHTS_CALM_MOST, P.need * 2) end
                if P.rung < #venues.LIGHTS_RUNGS then P.rung = P.rung + 1 end
                P.calm = 0
            elseif mean < venues.LIGHTS_FAST_MS then
                P.calm = P.calm + 1
                if P.calm >= P.need and P.rung > 1 then
                    P.rung, P.calm = P.rung - 1, 0
                    P.climbed = true
                    return venues.LIGHTS_RUNGS[P.rung]
                end
            else
                P.calm = 0
            end
            P.climbed = false
        end
    end
    return venues.LIGHTS_RUNGS[P.rung]
end
function venues.lights_paced(S, burning)
    S.lightsPace = S.lightsPace or {}
    local P = S.lightsPace
    local clock = rawget(_G, "clock_elapsed_f64")
    local now = burning and type(clock) == "function" and tonumber(clock()) or nil
    if now ~= nil and P.at ~= nil then venues.lights_pace(P, (now - P.at) * 1000) end
    P.at = now
    return venues.LIGHTS_RUNGS[P.rung or 1]
end
venues.LIGHTS_FADE = 30
function venues.lights_frame(S, key, layout, x, y, z, door)
    venues.flames_keep(S.flameState, key, layout.flames, venues.flame_spawn)
    local le = venues.lights_engine()
    local v2 = venues.lightfog_v2(layout)
    venues.le_wear(S, v2, le)
    local dresser = door.owns() == true
    local dark = venues.lights_dark(layout, venues.room_watch(S.roomState, layout.rooms))
    local target
    if v2 then
        target = {}
        for c = 1, 3 do target[c] = math.max(venues.NIGHT_FLOOR, venues.NIGHT_AMBIENT[c]) end
    else
        local A = venues.DARK_AMBIENT
        target = { A, A, A }
    end
    local function at_fade()
        local f = S.lightsFade / venues.LIGHTS_FADE
        local c = {}
        for i = 1, 3 do c[i] = math.floor(255 - (255 - target[i]) * f + 0.5) end
        return c[1], c[2], c[3]
    end
    if dark and not dresser then
        S.lightsFade = math.min(venues.LIGHTS_FADE, S.lightsFade + 1)
        if door.set(at_fade()) then S.lightsAmbient = target[1] end
        if v2 and door.actor ~= nil then door.actor(true) end
    elseif S.lightsAmbient ~= nil then
        S.lightsFade = S.lightsFade - 1
        if S.lightsFade <= 0 or dresser then
            S.lightsAmbient, S.lightsFade = nil, 0
            door.clear()
            if door.actor ~= nil then door.actor(false) end
        else
            door.set(at_fade())
        end
    end
    local lights = type(layout.lights) == "table" and layout.lights or {}
    local on = #lights > 0 and (dresser or S.lightsAmbient ~= nil)
    S.lightsState.most = venues.lights_paced(S, on)
    S.lightsState.fade = v2
    if v2 then
        S.lightsState.most = math.min(S.lightsState.most, venues.LIGHTS_ACTIVE)
        S.lightsState.picker = function(list, most, prev)
            local eye, marios = venues.light_viewers()
            return venues.light_pick_v2(list, eye, marios, most, prev)
        end
    else
        S.lightsState.picker = nil
    end
    S.lightsFrame = S.lightsFrame + 1
    return venues.lights_apply(S.lightsState, lights, x, y, z, S.lightsFrame, on, le)
end
function venues.lights_drop(S, gone, door)
    venues.le_wear(S, false, venues.lights_engine())
    if door.actor ~= nil then door.actor(false) end
    venues.lights_release(S.lightsState, venues.lights_engine(), gone)
    venues.flames_release(S.flameState, gone)
    venues.room_release(S.roomState)
    if S.lightsPace ~= nil then S.lightsPace.at = nil end
    if S.lightsAmbient ~= nil then
        S.lightsAmbient, S.lightsFade = nil, 0
        door.clear()
    end
end
venues.CULL_ASPECT = 2.4
venues.CULL_MARGIN = 15
venues.cullChunks = nil
local GRAPH_ACTIVE = 1
local function cull_call(name, default, ...)
    local f = rawget(_G, name)
    if type(f) ~= "function" then return default end
    local ok, out = pcall(f, ...)
    if not ok then return default end
    return out
end
local function walk_graph(first, visit, budget)
    budget = budget or { left = 8192 }
    local node = first
    while node ~= nil and budget.left > 0 do
        budget.left = budget.left - 1
        visit(node)
        if node.children ~= nil then walk_graph(node.children, visit, budget) end
        node = node.next
        if node == first then break end
    end
end
function venues.cull_collect(root)
    local out = {}
    if root == nil then return out end
    local TRANSLATION = GRAPH_NODE_TYPE_TRANSLATION or 0x016
    local LOD = GRAPH_NODE_TYPE_LEVEL_OF_DETAIL or 0x00B
    local MARKER = GRAPH_NODE_TYPE_CULLING_RADIUS or 0x02F
    venues.cullMarked = false
    walk_graph(root, function(node)
        local kid = node.children
        if node.type ~= TRANSLATION or kid == nil then return end
        local tn = cull_call("cast_graph_node", nil, node)
        local t = tn ~= nil and tn.translation or nil
        if t == nil then return end
        if kid.type == MARKER then
            local mn = cull_call("cast_graph_node", nil, kid)
            local r = mn ~= nil and tonumber(mn.cullingRadius) or nil
            if r == nil or r <= 0 then return end
            venues.cullMarked = true
            out[#out + 1] = { node = kid, x = t.x, y = t.y, z = t.z, r = r }
            return
        end
        if kid.type ~= LOD then return end
        local ln = cull_call("cast_graph_node", nil, kid)
        local r = ln ~= nil and tonumber(ln.minDistance) or nil
        if r == nil or r >= 0 then return end
        out[#out + 1] = { node = node, x = t.x, y = t.y, z = t.z, r = -r }
    end)
    return out
end
function venues.cull_cone()
    local fov = tonumber(cull_call("get_current_fov", 45)) or 45
    if fov <= 0 or fov >= 170 then fov = 45 end
    local half = math.atan(math.tan(math.rad(fov) / 2) * math.sqrt(1 + venues.CULL_ASPECT ^ 2))
    return half + math.rad(venues.CULL_MARGIN)
end
function venues.cull_sees(ex, ey, ez, fx, fy, fz, cone, c)
    local dx, dy, dz = c.x - ex, c.y - ey, c.z - ez
    local d = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
    if d <= c.r then return true end
    local cos = (dx * fx + dy * fy + dz * fz) / d
    if cos > 1 then cos = 1 elseif cos < -1 then cos = -1 end
    return math.acos(cos) - math.asin(c.r / d) <= cone
end
function venues.cull_step()
    local chunks = venues.cullChunks
    if chunks == nil or #chunks == 0 then return 0 end
    local lk = gLakituState
    local eye, at = lk ~= nil and lk.pos or nil, lk ~= nil and lk.focus or nil
    if eye == nil or at == nil then return 0 end
    local fx, fy, fz = at.x - eye.x, at.y - eye.y, at.z - eye.z
    local len = math.sqrt(fx * fx + fy * fy + fz * fz)
    local off = 0
    local cone = venues.cull_cone()
    for _, c in ipairs(chunks) do
        local on = len < 1 or venues.cull_sees(eye.x, eye.y, eye.z, fx / len, fy / len, fz / len, cone, c)
        local flags = c.node.flags
        local now = (flags & GRAPH_ACTIVE) ~= 0
        if on ~= now then c.node.flags = on and (flags | GRAPH_ACTIVE) or (flags & ~GRAPH_ACTIVE) end
        if not on then off = off + 1 end
    end
    return off
end
function venues.cull_prepare()
    venues.cull_release()
    local m = gMarioStates ~= nil and gMarioStates[0] or nil
    local area = m ~= nil and m.area or nil
    local root = area ~= nil and area.root or nil
    local first = root ~= nil and root.node or nil
    local chunks = venues.cull_collect(first)
    venues.cullChunks = #chunks > 1 and chunks or nil
    return #chunks
end
function venues.cull_release(gone)
    local chunks = venues.cullChunks
    venues.cullChunks = nil
    venues.cullMarked = false
    if gone or chunks == nil then return end
    for _, c in ipairs(chunks) do
        local flags = c.node.flags
        if (flags & GRAPH_ACTIVE) == 0 then c.node.flags = flags | GRAPH_ACTIVE end
    end
end
    return venues
end)()
R.sharedDark = SHARED_LIGHTS.DARK_AMBIENT
R.shared = SHARED_LIGHTS
R.lightsState = { ids = {}, on = {} }
R.roomState = {}
R.flameState = { list = {} }
R.lightsAmbient = nil
R.lightsFade = 0
R.lightsFrame = 0
R.lightsPace = nil
function R.light_door(map)
    return {
        owns = function()
            return R.darkWorn
        end,
        set = function(v, g, b)
            if g ~= nil and b ~= nil then return R.light_take(v, g, b) end
            local a, rig, t = R.rig_ambient(map or R.here(), v)
            if not R.light_take(a[1], a[2], a[3]) then return false end
            if t > 0 then R.rig_shade(rig) end
            return true
        end,
        actor = function(on)
            if R.select ~= nil then on = false end
            if (R.actorLit == true) == (on == true) then return end
            R.actorLit = on == true
            local tint = SHARED_LIGHTS.ACTOR_LIGHT
            for i = 0, 2 do
                call("set_lighting_color", i, on and tint[i + 1] or 255)
                call("set_lighting_color_ambient", i, 255)
            end
        end,
        clear = function()
            local dark = R.worn ~= nil and R.worn.look ~= nil and R.worn.look.dark or nil
            if R.darkWorn and type(dark) == "table" then
                call("le_set_ambient_color", dark[1], dark[2], dark[3])
            else
                R.light_give()
            end
        end,
    }
end
function R.lights_forget(gone)
    SHARED_LIGHTS.lights_drop(R, gone, R.light_door(nil))
end
function R.lights_step(map)
    local m = me_mario()
    if m == nil or m.pos == nil then return 0 end
    return SHARED_LIGHTS.lights_frame(R, map, map, m.pos.x, m.pos.y, m.pos.z, R.light_door(map))
end
R.LODS = false
R.lodsShipped = nil
function R.lods(on)
    local bv = G.gBehaviorValues
    if bv == nil or bv.ProcessLODs == nil then return false end
    if on then
        if R.lodsShipped == nil then R.lodsShipped = bv.ProcessLODs end
        bv.ProcessLODs = 1
    elseif R.lodsShipped ~= nil then
        bv.ProcessLODs = R.lodsShipped
        R.lodsShipped = nil
    end
    return true
end
R.WORLD_RING = 160
function R.arrival_spot(map, globalIndex)
    if type(map) ~= "table" then return nil end
    return SHARED_VENUES.arrival_of(map, map.race == true, globalIndex)
end
function R.arrive(map)
    local np, m = me_player(), me_mario()
    if np == nil or m == nil or m.pos == nil then return false end
    local spot = R.arrival_spot(map, np.globalIndex)
    if spot == nil then return false end
    m.pos.x, m.pos.y, m.pos.z = spot.x, spot.y, spot.z
    if m.vel ~= nil then m.vel.x, m.vel.y, m.vel.z = 0, 0, 0 end
    m.forwardVel = 0
    local o = m.marioObj
    if o ~= nil then o.oPosX, o.oPosY, o.oPosZ = spot.x, spot.y, spot.z end
    if m.faceAngle ~= nil then m.faceAngle.y = to_angle(spot.yaw) end
    return true
end
function R.update()
    local map = R.here()
    local np = me_player()
    local level = np ~= nil and np.currLevelNum or nil
    if R.worn ~= nil and R.worn ~= map then R.unwear() end
    if map ~= R.built then
        if map ~= nil then
            R.build(map)
            if R.extras(map) then SHARED_LIGHTS.cull_prepare() end
            if R.extras(map) and R.worn ~= map then R.wear(map) end
            if R.lastLevel ~= level then R.arrive(map) end
            if R.extras(map) and R.lastLevel ~= level and R.skipSelect ~= level then R.open_select(map) end
            R.skipSelect = nil
        else
            R.teardown()
            R.forget_balls()
            if R.run ~= nil then R.note("Track stopped - you left its level", "lasting") end
            R.run = nil
        end
    end
    if R.backLanding ~= nil then
        if frame_now() > (R.backLanding.untilFrame or 0) then R.backLanding = nil else R.back_land(level) end
    end
    R.lastLevel = level
    if R.select ~= nil and R.select.away ~= nil and (map == nil or not R.extras(map)) then
        R.select_step()
    end
    local arriving = R.selectArrive
    if arriving ~= nil then
        arriving.arriveFrames = (arriving.arriveFrames or 0) + 1
        if arriving.arriveFrames > R.SELECT_ARRIVE_FRAMES then R.select_arrived(arriving, false) end
    end
    if is_host() then
        for _, m in ipairs(R.maps) do R.host_step(m) end
        if frame_now() % 30 == 0 then R.host_attend() end
    end
    R.music_watch()
    if map ~= nil and R.extras(map) then
        local m = me_mario()
        R.starSpin = (R.starSpin + 0x800) % 0x10000
        R.lay_step(map)
        if (R.tendFresh or 0) > 0 then
            R.tendFresh = R.tendFresh - 1
            R.tend(map)
        elseif frame_now() % 10 == 0 then
            R.tend(map)
        end
        R.wired_step(map)
        R.onoff_heard(map)
        R.settle_mood(map)
        R.aim_sun(map)
        R.rain_step()
        R.select_step()
        R.coin_star_step(map, m)
        R.red_step(map)
        if m ~= nil and R.select == nil then R.coin_note(map, m.numCoins) end
        R.run_step(map)
        R.ball_frame()
        if m ~= nil then R.ride_update(m) end
        if map.look ~= nil and map.look.fog ~= nil and R.moodOn then R.fog_paint(true) end
        R.lights_step(map)
        R.lods(R.LODS)
        SHARED_LIGHTS.cull_step()
    else
        if next(R.lightsState.ids) ~= nil or R.lightsAmbient ~= nil or R.roomState.set
            or #(R.flameState.list or {}) > 0 then
            R.lights_forget(false)
        end
        R.lods(false)
        SHARED_LIGHTS.cull_release(false)
    end
end
function R.level_init()
    R.lights_forget(true)
    R.darkWorn = false
    SHARED_LIGHTS.cull_release(true)
    R.lods(false)
    R.things = {}
    R.built = nil
    R.lay = nil
    R.forget_balls()
    R.forget_wall_run()
    R.FIX.forget_spiny_anchor()
    R.pipeTravel = nil
    if R.worn ~= nil then
        R.fog_paint(false)
    end
    for _, row in ipairs(R.pieces) do row.on = nil end
    if R.select ~= nil then
        R.select.cam = nil
        R.select.stars, R.select.wall = {}, nil
        R.select_close(false)
    end
    local arriving = R.selectArrive
    if arriving ~= nil then
        local np = me_player()
        local here = np ~= nil and np.currLevelNum or nil
        local landed = arriving.away ~= nil and here == arriving.away.level
        if landed then R.skipSelect = here end
        R.select_arrived(arriving, landed)
    end
    R.music_arrived()
end
function R.before_mario(m)
    if m == nil then return end
    R.picking_before(m)
    if m.playerIndex ~= 0 then return end
    if R.select ~= nil then
        R.select_input(m)
        return
    end
    local map = R.built
    if map == nil or not R.extras(map) then return end
    R.run_hold(m)
    R.work_the_ride(m)
    R.push_players(m)
    R.pipe_step(m)
    R.open_spiny_window(m)
end
function R.after_mario(m)
    if m == nil then return end
    R.picking_hide(m)
    if R.built == nil or not R.extras(R.built) then return end
    R.wall_run_pose(m)
    R.ride_picture(m)
    if m.playerIndex == 0 then R.dance_step(m) end
end
function R.hud_behind()
    if R.stepping_aside() then return end
    if R.select ~= nil then
        call("djui_hud_set_resolution", G.RESOLUTION_DJUI)
        R.draw_select()
    end
    R.draw_count()
end
function R.hud()
    R.draw_notes()
    if R.select ~= nil then return end
    local map = R.here()
    if map == nil or not R.extras(map) then return end
    call("djui_hud_set_resolution", G.RESOLUTION_DJUI)
    R.draw_rain()
    R.draw_course(map)
    R.draw_onoff()
    R.draw_tracks(map)
end
function R.packet(data)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
    local kind = data.ofmaps
    if kind == "all" then
        local map = R.byKey[data.key]
        if map ~= nil and not is_host() then
            say(YELLOW .. "The host is taking everybody to " .. (map.name or map.key) .. "!")
            R.go(map)
        end
    elseif kind == "lock" then
        R.locked = data.on == true
    elseif kind == "say" then
        say((data.raw and "" or YELLOW) .. tostring(data.text or ""))
    elseif kind == "stars" then
        R.stars_heard(data.s)
    elseif kind == "took" then
        R.star_record(R.byKey[data.key], math.floor(tonumber(data.id) or 0), data.who)
    elseif kind == "st" then
        local st = R.unpack(data.s)
        R.state[data.key] = st
    elseif kind == "ev" then
        R.host_report(R.byKey[data.key], data.e, data.n, data.t, data.id, data.left, data.g)
    elseif kind == "note" then
        if not is_host() then R.note(tostring(data.text or ""), data.tone) end
    elseif kind == "go" then
        for _, mv in ipairs(R.movers) do
            if mv.map.key == data.key and mv.index == data.i then R.mover_go(mv, data.t0) end
        end
    elseif kind == "ball" then
        if R.built ~= nil and R.built.key == data.key then R.ball_heard(data) end
    elseif kind == "ride" then
        R.ride_heard(data, tonumber(data.g))
    elseif kind == "pick" then
        if tonumber(data.g) ~= nil then R.picking[tonumber(data.g)] = data.on == true or nil end
    else
        return false
    end
    return true
end
function R.connected()
    if R.select ~= nil then
        send({ ofmaps = "pick", on = true, g = me_player() ~= nil and me_player().globalIndex or 0 })
    end
    if not is_host() then return end
    send({ ofmaps = "lock", on = R.locked })
    R.stars_publish()
    for _, map in ipairs(R.maps) do
        if R.state[map.key] ~= nil then publish(map) end
    end
end
function R.seq_load(player, seq)
    if player ~= 0 or type(seq) ~= "number" then return nil end
    if R.stepping_aside() then return nil end
    if SHARED_MUSIC.refuses_load(player, R.music_selecting(), R.music_stream_in_force()) then
        R.starved = true
        return R.NO_LOAD
    end
    local map = R.here()
    if map == nil or not R.extras(map) then return nil end
    local base = R.level_track()
    if base ~= nil and seq % 256 ~= base % 256 then return nil end
    local asked = R.music_state().wanted
    if asked ~= nil and asked % 256 == seq % 256 then return nil end
    return R.track(map)
end
function R.mods_loaded()
    R.settle()
    local n = R.register_levels()
    if is_host() then R.stars_publish() end
    return n
end
local function leader_api()
    local lead = R.leader()
    return lead ~= nil and lead.api or nil
end
if type(G.hook_event) == "function" then
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_MODS_LOADED, function()
        R.settle()
        if R.leading() then R.mods_loaded() end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_UPDATE, function()
        if R.leading() then R.update() end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_LEVEL_INIT, function()
        if R.leading() then R.level_init() end
    end)
    if G.HOOK_ON_INSTANT_WARP ~= nil then
        G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_INSTANT_WARP, function(area, id, displacement)
            if not R.leading() then return end
            local map = R.here()
            if map ~= nil and R.extras(map) then SHARED_LIGHTS.pocket_door(map.pocketShift, displacement) end
        end)
    end
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_HUD_RENDER, function()
        if R.leading() then R.hud() end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_HUD_RENDER_BEHIND or G.HOOK_ON_HUD_RENDER, function()
        if R.leading() then R.hud_behind() end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_PACKET_RECEIVE, function(data)
        if type(data) ~= "table" or data.ofmaps == nil then return end
        local api = leader_api()
        if api ~= nil then api.packet(data) end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_PLAYER_CONNECTED, function()
        if R.leading() then R.connected() end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_SEQ_LOAD, function(player, seq)
        if R.leading() then return R.seq_load(player, seq) end
        return nil
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_FIND_WATER_LEVEL, function(x, z)
        if not R.leading() then return nil end
        local map = R.here()
        if map == nil or not R.extras(map) then return nil end
        local m = me_mario()
        return R.water_at(map, x, z, m ~= nil and m.pos ~= nil and m.pos.y or nil)
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_OBJECT_UNLOAD, function(o)
        if R.things[o] ~= nil then R.things[o] = nil end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_EXIT, function()
        if R.leading() then R.unwear() end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_BEFORE_MARIO_UPDATE, function(m)
        if R.leading() then R.before_mario(m) end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_MARIO_UPDATE, function(m)
        if R.leading() then R.after_mario(m) end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ON_WARP, function()
        if R.leading() then R.music_arrived() end
    end)
    G.hook_event(G.HOOK_ALLOW_INTERACT, function(m, o, interactType)
        if not R.leading() or R.stepping_aside() then return nil end
        if interactType == G.INTERACT_WARP and R.is_map_pipe(o) then return false end
        return R.picking_interact(m, o, interactType)
    end)
end
if type(G.hook_on_sync_table_change) == "function" and R.SYNC ~= nil then
    G.hook_on_sync_table_change(R.SYNC, R.MUSIC_KEY, 0, function() R.music_heard() end)
end
if not hub.commandHooked and type(G.hook_chat_command) == "function" then
    hub.commandHooked = true
    G.hook_chat_command("ofmap", "[map] - visit a map made with Oldfart Studio (no map: the list)",
        function(text)
            local api = leader_api()
            if api == nil then return false end
            return api.command(text)
        end)
end
